# CHANGELOG

The forward-going record of substantive changes to the framework. Entries are ordered most-recent-first, dated, and grouped by theme within a date.

> [!warning]
> **Goal-Update Coupling Class numbering changed 2026-05-09.** Anything older than git tag `pre-guc-rename-2026-05-09` uses the old Class numbering:
>
> | historical | actual current     | sometimes AKA  |
> | ---------- | ------------------ | -------------- |
> | Class 1    | GUC Class 1: Separated | Modular        |
> | Class 2    | GUC Class 3: Coupled   | Undirected     |
> | Class 3    | GUC Class 2: Partial   | Operational    |
>
> Cycle entries in this file dated **before 2026-05-09** use the old Class vocabulary verbatim — the body entries are frozen and not retroactively edited. This callout is the rosetta stone for reading those entries.

## How this file relates to TODO.md, LOG.md, audits/, and git

- **[TODO.md](TODO.md)** — current tactical work items (active findings, queued spike follow-ups, deferred items, open MEDIUM items, missing segments).
- **CHANGELOG.md** *(this file)* — consolidated narrative of substantive changes from 2026-04-24 onward. Cycle-shape narratives ("what changed about how the theory thinks", structural moves, conventions adopted) go here. Entry granularity is the day; commit hashes appear inline for traceability.
- **[LOG.md](LOG.md)** — frozen archaeology of cycles through 2026-04-24. Read it when the *origin* of a current commitment matters: whether a "settled" item rests on derivation or on a cycle's working consensus, or how an audit finding's prior status should shape current routing.
- **`audits/pending-findings-YYYY-MM-DD.md`** — original audit-finding records (per-cycle burden-of-proof writeups and resolution trails).
- **Git** — primary source for "what changed in code/segments." Use `git log --follow` for slug-renamed segments.

CHANGELOG carries the *narrative shape* of each cycle (what conventions changed, what disciplines emerged, what the cycle was about); the per-cycle `audits/pending-findings-*` files carry the *checklist trail* (which findings were verified, what fixes landed). They overlap in scope but serve different reading needs.

---


## 2026-08-22 — impl-series status-tagging pass (audit 731548 §B-4), batch two: `impl-persistence-and-limits`, `impl-causal-access`, `impl-strategy-structure` — with the first audit-gold lift

Three more chapter-ends aligned (`5f46328`, `ec30af5`, `1ddd5d9`), and the method changed on Joseph's direction: *before* any writing, one inventory agent per chapter drained the audit reports, the `AUDIT-WORKING` reflections (gold), spikes, and trackers against the chapter, and a fourth agent mined the AISI-responses corpus for distillations and his own lines; then a fork that had read the chapter whole wrote; then a different-substrate verifier (grok-4.6, refute posture, sources whole) — each returned approve-with-required-fixes, each having found a hole the writer had not, all applied and re-verified. What the inventories changed about the work: persistence's defect was less overclaim than **stale coverage** (the 05-13 impl ignored Corollary A.1S.1, the certificate voice, mood + the MG discharge, and the exact $\epsilon_{\max}$); causal-access's was three stale facts surviving the 05-31 rewrite plus a **coverage hole** (the chapter's exact new theorem absent from its own chapter-end); strategy-structure's was eight genuine overshoots and two under-claims, with the "unique broadly-available escape" item resolved by *carrying the definition* from `#der-causal-insufficiency-detection`, not softening. New content, each at the tier earned: the sum identity $\delta_{\text{sat}}+\delta_{\text{regret}} = V^{\min}-V_O(\pi_{\text{current}})$ (exact, with preconditions and the RHS's convention-invariance); the two-rung monotonicity with the regret reversal; policy-class relativity of the diagnostic at discussion grade with Result 5a's truth-relative and agent-relative halves kept apart; the separation-theorem bookend (ceiling vs floor); Lemma A.1N / A.1S.1 / adaptive reserve at the front of the persistence chapter-end. The verifiers' catches worth naming as patterns: a canon-cites-only-canon fix that replaced a paper name with a *false canon attribution* (wrapping's (C1)–(C3) are a different triple from the loop's); a Model-D "$P=0$" clause added to an exact segment without its $\alpha R \gt \rho$ gate; a Vol-4 result quoted via a miner's excerpt rather than read whole. One guard from the AISI miner applied: the "control budget = exploration budget" line is sealed pre-reset phrasing and was not re-imported — live ASF (Prop B.4) makes exploration a gating factor on $\alpha_\Sigma$.

Chapter-local source fixes landed with each batch (all first-hand re-verified, regression pickaxe on each old form): `deriv-graph-structure-uniqueness` (on-policy residuals cannot reveal insufficiency — the no-go; overestimate scoped by node-type sign), `strategy-structure-intro`, `def-strategy-dag` (escape definition; OR/AND sign; CMC → Markov factorization, AND/OR parameterization additionally required), `der-chain-confidence-decay` (non-increasing), `def-control-regret` (preconditions), `result-sector-condition-stability` and `result-persistence-condition` (A.1S.1 paragraphs), `result-sector-persistence-template` and `deriv-persistence-cost` (six → seven instantiations), `persistence-and-limits-intro`, `der-loop-interventional-access` (Mode 3 landed in the text four months late, sub-scoped), `disc-ciy-unified-objective` (tier inherited from the LMI segment), `scope-ciy-observational-proxy`.

Drains in the same batch: TODO items for `der-loop` status, sat-gap/control-regret convention note, and J-1 deleted as landed; Tier-3 line and the impl-series item updated (3 of 9 remain); the source-defect list extended with everything the three passes reported and did not fix (incl. two 193847 findings dispositioned "PROPOSALS candidate" in May that never entered a tracker); STATUS rows for 731548 and 773921; adjudication 218564 Fresh-7 closed; PROPOSALS SP-9 carries the `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing` table-vs-prose inconsistency; INDEX row for `spike-c2-star-to-integrate` says dormant; JOSEPH-TODO gets the (PI) hoist nod. **Audit gold, first lift under the resolved gate:** 28 reflection files moved to `audits/.integrated/` with per-file item→landing entries in its MANIFEST (731548/26–35; 374162 batches 05–07; 451729 batches 05–07; 963715/34-38; 773921/34; 471203/43-46); held under the gate by the writers' own ledgers: 731548/36 and verify/06, 451729/08–10, 471203/35-38, 628417, 374162 batches 01–04 and 08. Remaining in the series: `impl-strategy-dynamics`, `impl-composition-machinery`, `impl-strategic-composition`, then TST ×4 and LLM ×4; the hub-and-spoke proof-flow figure stays open (526815's drafted `.tex` figures are its inputs).

## 2026-08-22 — `#hyp-truth-serving-loophole` (sketch): goal-declared rather than goal-blind; a proposed fourth mode of loop-interventional access

New exploratory segment in Part III after `#hyp-directed-separation-under-composition`, at Joseph's request, from two of his claims recorded in the AISI influx notes (2026-08) — *"There is a fundamental resolution at higher levels of agency and intelligence for directed separation"* (love of truth above self as the one independently-inculcated goal) and *"Real goal blindness is self-defeating at higher levels of cognition where your action-space actually cascades from your goals"* — and an agent's derivation of their consequences, first checked against the primaries here. Three hypotheses at `sketch`: (i) the degradation norm of `#der-directed-separation` carries an unstated premise (the goal points away from accuracy); with truth as the terminal objective the $G_t \to f_M$ coupling is corrective — causal discipline by objective choice rather than wiring; (ii) for an agent whose action space cascades from its goals the certified $\kappa = 0$ is unreachable from inside (the agent is its own query-selection policy), so the achievable discipline is goal-*declared* — $G_t$ on an out-of-band record, the update's dependence on it auditable, a certified bound rather than a zero; (iii) goal declaration as a fourth, semantically distinct mode of `#der-loop-interventional-access` (intervening on the purposeful substate; the behavioral $\hat\kappa$ estimator already performs it as an external probe). Stated against what canon already holds: directed separation is scoped to *processing* with selection allowed, so the "self-defeat" is of a stronger reading than canon's; the content-axis boundary already makes model-space exploration correctly goal-directed. Open, named in WN: whether "truth as terminal objective" is a value functional at all; the sign of the goal-induced displacement; whether Mode 4 is a fifth floor instance; declaration vs detection. Not verified by a second agent — it is a sketch, and says so.

## 2026-08-22 — ρ-factorization spike landed on the exact decomposition: `#deriv-mismatch-budget-attribution` (policy is the measure, not a term)

The 2026-04-22 spike's positive content — what `#internal-external-decomposition` had marked "the finer $\mathcal M/\pi$/cross split … under separate investigation" — restated on `#result-mismatch-decomposition`'s exact three-term split and strengthened (`9d2e0c9`; author a Claude agent, verifier ≠ author on grok-4.6, refute posture, every proposition re-derived). **Prop 1** (exact under GA-1 + a chronica-measurable policy): the estimation, state-uncertainty, and channel terms are policy-independent kernels averaged under the on-policy law $\mathbb P_\pi$ — the policy is the measure, not a fourth term, so there is no $\Delta_\pi^2$ and no cross term; the spike's §5.4 entanglement ("policy-benignity" is the policy concentrating the trajectory where the model class is adequate), §5.2 $g \equiv 1$ for control-invariant linear-Gaussian innovation, and §12 "measure change, not projection" are corollaries, and the channel floor turns out irreducible at kernel level yet policy-movable in expectation (canon's own `#example-kalman` sensor modes). **Prop 2**: exact log form of viability, $\mathcal V = [\text{floor-relative}] - \tfrac12\log(1+x_\pi)$, log-additive in estimation + state-uncertainty to first order with remainder $\le x^2/4$ — the precise sense in which the multiplicative intuition was approximately right, and its "factors" are a modeling/acting split, not model/policy. **Prop 3** (exact): the information form by the chain rule of log-loss — channel entropy + state-uncertainty information + estimation divergence — with no e-flat/m-flat condition and no alignment qualifier (answers `#result-mismatch-decomposition`'s WN wondering on variance-only loss). **Prop 4** (conditional, sub-scope $\alpha$): class-ceiling / within-class Pythagorean split on an e-flat predictive class. Not landed: (R-V) with cross terms (superseded), the $\rho_{\text{external}}$ reference-agent menu (dissolved — the floor is a kernel), §13's parent-spike advice (moot). Spike §4.2's per-edge variance is off by a factor of 4. Ripples: `#internal-external-decomposition` reservation replaced by present truth and its "cannot move at all" floor sentences reconciled with Cor. 1c; `#disc-identifiability-floor` Instance 4's "$\mathcal M/\pi/$cross" ghost replaced by the $(E_\pi,U_\pi,C_\pi)$ coordinates; `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing` §AV names the budget as principal member and classifies the information form *adopted*. Verifier's four required fixes (misattributed adversarial exclusion; channel differential entropy not non-negative; matched/adopted mix; the Instance-4 ghost) applied before commit. Open in the segment's WN: `#example-kalman` deliberately out of `depends:` (outline order) — revisit at `deps-verified`; the class-ceiling ↔ `#def-model-class-fitness` link stays discussion-grade.

The morning's filing of this spike as "integrated" on the headline alone, and its reversal, are recorded in the spikes-corpus-drain entry below.

## 2026-08-22 — impl-series status-tagging pass (audit 731548 §B-4), pilot batch of three: `impl-cooperative-adversarial`, `impl-orient-cascade`, `impl-unity-communication`

The pass the July audit routed and nobody ran: align each chapter-end implication to the status its source segments actually carry, strengthening where the source earns more than the prose claimed, restating at the source's tier where it overshot, adding a one-line proof-provenance orientation at each chapter open. Each segment was edited by one agent (sources read whole), independently verified by a second agent on a different substrate (`grok-dev`, sources read whole, report only), and the verifier's required fixes applied before commit. Regression pickaxe on every restated form: clean (no add-then-delete history in `src/`; the deleted forms originate in the gold-lift / implications-series first drafts, not in corrected-away canon). Per Joseph's same-day directive, the segments' Working Notes now carry only regression guards and forward pointers; the per-item dispositions live here.

**Common to all three:** a *Proof provenance* paragraph after the chapter-open naming each source's tier and what within it is formulation vs derived; chronicle dates removed from bodies; forward references into later chapters tagged as such; resolved off-ramp blocks deleted rather than annotated.

**`impl-cooperative-adversarial`** (526815 F222–F232; item (i) was executed 2026-07-03, the rest now). The three-boundaries paragraph had misdescribed all three boundaries the way `#der-interaction-channel-classification` warns against (magnitude conflated with information content; the sector-region boundary called a "prediction-confidence interval"; a magnitude shock called model-class strain) — rewritten to the source's boundaries with its precedence-convention caveat. Magnitude-shock repair put on the source's *capacity* axis ($R_B$, $\alpha_B$, tempo), keeping the earnable Kalman case $\alpha_B = \eta_B^\ast$. The class claim "contraction machinery cannot handle strategic regimes" was contradicted by canon itself — `#deriv-strategic-composition` sub-scope $\alpha'$ transfers the same sector machinery on $\xi = \pi - \pi^\ast$ — so the boundary is now on the *state variable*; the genuine no-go is $\beta'$; passivity restated with the supply-rate mechanism; the "systematically wrong in three directions" flourish deleted. OODA: the product threshold $\gamma_A\mathcal T_A \gt \Delta\rho_B^\ast$ is the derived one, and under linear Model D it reads $\mathcal T_A \gt \mathcal T_B R_B/\gamma_A - \rho_{B,\text{base}}/\gamma_A$, so the old "$\mathcal T_A \gt \mathcal T_B/k$" is *earned* with $k = \gamma_A/R_B$ exactly as its $\rho_{\text{base}} = 0$ case (a strengthening, not a soften); "destabilizes iff" → certificate voice (the B-1 register slip), with forced escape only in the strict radially-tight case and the at-equality asymptote named. Noise injection: the body had conflated the non-coupling-dominant *budget* regime (`#result-adversarial-exponent-regimes`, $b \to 1$ Model D / $\to 1/2$ Model S) with observation-noise gating (`#obs-gated-tempo-advantage`, empirical); now two gatings at their own tiers, the defender-side prescription an unmodeled trade (hypothesis), the "cooperative-game-like dynamics" claim deleted. F231 strengthened: too-strong *feedback interconnection* does break composite contraction via (CC-feedback)'s sign-free small-gain condition and (CM2-M), but this is a loop-gain topology statement, not the chapter's signed $\gamma \lt 0$; the reward-shaping case is partially-opposing objectives (Ch.5). Trust bridge: coupling sign is *evidence* updating $U_{\text{align},ji}$ in the trust meta-model, with the source's adversary-presents-as-cooperative caveat. `depends:` gained `deriv-sector-condition`.

**`impl-orient-cascade`** (F88–F91). F88: provenance paragraph; the 16/24 survival count qualified as *statement-level* (definedness is not extractability, per `#result-section-ii-survival`); the approximate-survival lift scoped to the Track 1/2 sub-scopes. F89 — strengthened past both the original and the auditor's proposed narrowing: the sources split the scaffolding claim in two — the cascade *ordering* can be imposed behaviorally on a coupled component (a template; `#result-coupled-diagnostic-framework`, normative design, discussion-grade), but *goal-blindness of step 1* cannot be produced within one goal-conditioned call (the Class 3 definition), so a goal-blind cascade requires the separated belief-update query of W₁ (`#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping`, exact under (C1)–(C3), structural bound additionally needing (C2′)); W₂ gives ordering with only behavioral separation. Deleted as false: "necessary to recover Part II's persistence guarantees" — 16 of 24 Part II results survive exactly on a raw Class 3 agent. The lead paragraph, which still carried the old claim, aligned. The Kahneman / organizational transfer tagged *[Hypothesis]*. F90: "deliberation is Pearl-$do$ on a simulated trajectory" replaced by the oracle contrast the sources support (loop obtains Level-2 data from the world; deliberation evaluates interventional *and* counterfactual queries against $M_t$, ceiling = model fidelity — the auditor's "counterfactual only" was narrower than the source); "first formally well-posed here" deleted. F91 — strengthened: the floor-invariance claim is now *derived* in the body (data-processing: internal computation cannot add observation-channel information), within `#deriv-persistence-cost`'s scope, with the two channels that *can* move the target named (policy → $\rho^{\text{eff}}$; model class); "$n\alpha/2$ per dimension" corrected to $n\alpha/2$ total, $\alpha/2$ per dimension. Adjacent fixes: the body's claim that the coupled-diagnostic framework "has no segment home" was false (`#result-coupled-diagnostic-framework`); the κ·𝒜 scaling sentence now says both tracks tighten the *distance* as $\sqrt I$ ($W_2^2 \propto I$, $d_{FR} \propto \sqrt{2I}$) with $I$ the theorems' information term and $\mathcal A$ only the coarser envelope; "the empirical pattern supports" → a prediction; unsourced "alternative orderings in adjacent literatures" → the derivation's own exclusion; the representational-capacity "unification" demoted from theorem to framing. Surfaced, not attempted: under the shared linear-Gaussian scope the Mitter–Newton rate is $\tfrac12\operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal I_o P_{ss})$ — the bandwidth floor and the survival LMI constrain the same Fisher trace, so a derived per-direction floor (trace identity through the Riccati steady state; continuous-vs-discrete-time seam to name) would promote the §2 composition from discussion-grade to conditional. `depends:` gained `result-coupled-diagnostic-framework`, `result-section-ii-survival`.

**`impl-unity-communication`** (F173–F181). F173: every "the framework predicts" resting on hypothesis-grade content now reads as the hypothesis's prediction; where a derived source exists the tier is named. ## 2026-07-29 — `#hyp-solicitable-escape` landed: the three escapes from an absorbing observability region are not peers

New segment **`#hyp-solicitable-escape`** (`hypothesis`, `discussion-grade`, `exploratory`) partitions the escape list that `#der-observability-dominance` names — external shock, proactive observability investment, another agent's coverage — by what the agent must *already possess* for the route to be available to it as an action. Shock is untargeted but unsolicitable (it happens to the agent). Self-instrumentation is solicitable but *targeted*: instrumenting node $v$ requires representing $v$ as deficient, which is precisely the recognition the absorbing condition removes, so for the blind spots that are actually self-locking the route's precondition is unavailable. Blanket instrumentation is the apparent counterexample and is dismissed conditionally: its cost scales with the node set while per-node value is the sector-parameter difference that node contributes, so under any budget short of blanket coverage the agent must rank nodes and targeting returns. What remains is that a differently-positioned observer is the only escape both untargeted and choosable — which gives a structural, not merely efficiency-based, reason for a network of differently-positioned agents. Max attainable is `conditional`, with both promotion steps named in Epistemic Status (formalize the resource bound against the investment tradeoff; sharpen the targeting precondition, which may reduce to existing strategy-representation machinery and turn the segment into a corollary).

**Placement adjudicated, not inherited.** The authoring agent proposed Part II Ch. Strategy Dynamics immediately after its parent `#der-observability-dominance`. That slot is wrong on two counts: it precedes the segment's own `#hyp-communication-gain` dependency (Part III) — a backward dependency `bin/lint-outline` would flag, and one no whitelist row should absorb since it is not an introduced-before-used placement — and it contradicts the parent's own Working-Notes routing, which had already sent this material to Part III (*"Pointer toward the unity/communication cluster, not this segment"*). Landed instead in Part III Ch. Unity, Communication, and Shared Intent, directly after `#hyp-communication-gain`; both dependencies now precede it and `bin/lint-outline` reports no new violation. The chapter comment's internal arc gained a closing clause: the chapter's other segments develop the communication channel as machinery, and this one names the case where the channel is not an efficiency but the only available move.

The landing discharges two open Working-Notes items in the parent, both updated in place: the "belongs elsewhere" pointer at §Belongs elsewhere (now marked landed, with the sharpening noted — the borrowed-observations route is the only one whose availability does not presuppose what the absorbing condition removes), and the open question *"how does a frozen-belief agent know it needs to invest in observability"* (now answered in the negative for the self-locking case, with belief-variance monitoring identified as the third mechanism the new segment's falsifier invites). Segment prose Americanized (`localisation`/`formalising`/`favours`); `bin/lint-md` clean on all three files.

**Corrected the same day — the targeting premise was inherited from prose, and the fix is a missing dependency edge.** The landing above leaned on `#der-observability-dominance`'s Discussion phrasing (*"cannot learn and cannot recognize that it cannot learn"*). That segment *derives* gain collapse — $\eta_{\text{edge}} \to 0$ via $U_{\text{obs}} \to \infty$, i.e. observations arriving and carrying no weight — and asserts the recognition conjunct separately; read as signal-*absence* it also slides toward the zero-information corner AAT excludes by scope, which would remove the segment's subject rather than strengthen it. Part I had already carved the distinction properly: `#def-mismatch-signal`'s **zero-aporia trichotomy** — $\delta_t \approx 0$ as (a) genuine adequacy, (b) confirmation bias, or (c) a channel too noisy to detect model error, *"silence can mean peace, or it can mean deafness"* — where case (c) **is** the absorbing condition and (a)-vs-(c) inseparability from within is the premise. The mechanical cause of the slide: `#der-observability-dominance` carried no `depends` edge to `#def-mismatch-signal` and never cited the trichotomy, so a reader arriving through the dependency graph met the strong prose without its disambiguation. That edge is now added, with a paragraph in the parent stating that what freezes is the *update*, not the *input*. `#hyp-solicitable-escape` now argues the premise from three upstream commitments (the trichotomy; `#def-observation-function`'s epistemic opacity, which forces $U_o$ to be estimated from the very innovations a low-$\sigma_v$ node suppresses; and `#emp-update-gain`'s dogmatism/nihilism behavioral identity, so that even correctly registering zero gain does not localize the fault), and carries a new `*[Scope (innovation-estimated observability)]*` clause: the targeting claim fails where $\sigma_v$ is known *architecturally*, since an agent that knows by construction which nodes it cannot sense can rank exactly those. That exception tracks a boundary de-novo readers had already flagged against the epistemic-opacity clause (a Kalman filter is handed its noise covariance rather than estimating it). Net effect is a narrower claim with a sharper falsifier, and both promotion steps in Epistemic Status are now specific rather than gestural. This was the fourth independent instance of the same inversion at a fourth site; the recurrence series and a proposed Part I Ch.3 pedagogy note — prescribed by audit 731548 in July and never routed — are worked in `spikes/spike-escape-standpoint-axis-2026-07-29.md` §10, under adversarial review before anything lands in Part I.

The segment carries a regression guard worth surfacing: the philosophical companion's appendix reported the parent's escape clause as *"not more effort by the same agent but another agent whose observations cover the blind spot"* — a false contrast, since instrumenting one's own unmonitored nodes *is* more effort by the same agent. The appendix's conclusion survives on the better grounds this segment argues (the self-route exists but cannot be aimed), and the one-route reading is not to be restored. **Open, deliberately not taken here:** the corresponding one-line correction on the ACA-appendix side, left for Joseph; and the slug has not been through a naming round — `solicitable` is load-bearing and may not survive standalone-citability review.

## 2026-07-16 — Level-4 strictness spike lands: mechanism counterfactuals separate strictly from Level 3 (the morning refusal reversed, with the theorem)

Joseph commissioned an independent push on the "Level 4" idea hours after canon deliberately refused it for lack of a strictness witness — "because if it fails, that's teaching us something about imagination and the causal levels as well." The boundary is the *specification language*: replacements written over Level-3-exposed channels (parents, mechanism outputs, fresh noise, shift-scale) reduce to L3; latent-anchored replacements ("this very background under a different law") need not, and the witness's provably does not. Pearl's §3.2.2 selector internalization re-represents without collapsing ($M \mapsto M^{\dagger}$ is not an L3 functional). Independently verified pre-commit (confirmer≠author): full enumeration of all intervention patterns and cross-world cells, brute force over all 16 deterministic L3-specified replacements, primary-source checks on Bareinboim Def. 7 and Saha et al. 2025, ghost-deletion audit clean. `#disc-structural-imagination`'s refusal paragraph deleted and replaced per integration-is-replacement: imagination bifurcates into *navigation* (L3-computable re-wirings/policies) and *positing* (law-changes on the actual background — provably underdetermined by all experimentable content, well-defined only relative to a committed representation). Naming caution recorded in-segment: say "mechanism counterfactuals separate strictly from Level 3," reserving bare "Level 4" for contexts where the relative-to-variable-set sense is established. Owed before promotion past draft: Pearl §7.2.4/§3.2.2 verification against an attached document, genericity, exact boundary characterization.

**Same-day completion (integration cycle, evening).** All three owed items discharged and the spike fully integrated: **(i)** Pearl verified against three primary texts — the 1994 *Probabilistic Calculus of Actions* (on-disk; atomic intervention *defined by* mechanism replacement, Eq. (3)/Fig. 1 internalization) and Pearl 2009 §3.2.2 + §7.2.4 read from two independent copies Joseph supplied (`_ref/books/` PDF text layer + epub — prose verbatim-identical; the epub's display equations are images, the PDF's are text, so the pair cross-confirm); precise locators restored, relata `claim-supported` events recorded for all three keys, reading conversions filed in `ref/converted/`. **(ii)** The reducibility boundary strengthened: Result 2 generalized to the PO-closure (uniform PO-specifications never separate; the channel list becomes the special case), converse posed as the sharp open question in Discussion. **(iii)** Genericity derived as **Result 4**: every anchor-mobile SCM separates from *its own exogenous relabeling* ($M \equiv_3 M^{\phi}$ always; $q(M^{\phi}) = 1$ vs $q(M) = 1 - \mu_{\phi}^{i}$), with the anchoring-as-coordinates corollary — latent-anchored content is exactly commitment to coordinates on $U$. ## 2026-07-15 — Tempo-additivity spike lands: the asserted redundancy inequality refuted and replaced (echo-chamber bound + two no-gos)

The def-adaptive-tempo upgrade path recorded 2026-07-14 ("derive the redundancy penalty → exact for the bound form") was commissioned as a spike and **closed as refuted-and-replaced**: the asserted $\mathcal{T} \leq \sum_k \nu^{(k)}\eta^{(k)\ast}$ (equality iff independent) is false in both directions — correlated noise is also a cancellation *resource* (super-additivity counterexamples incl. a 40× undercount; an $n{=}2$ equality point at harmonic-mean covariance kills the iff). The true theorem set, all exact in the Fisher-local regime: additivity exact under cross-channel noise independence (sufficient, not necessary); in the common-source regime the additive form *is* an exact upper bound with closed-form penalty and joint information saturating at $1/\sigma_s^2$ (the **echo-chamber bound**); and two no-gos give the intrinsic reason — sign-blind dependence measures (CMI/total correlation) provably cannot be the correction term, and the PID impossibility results (Williams-Beer 2010; Rauh et al. 2014; Barrett 2015) block any convention-free channel-local attribution for $n \geq 3$, scoped precisely (the joint form is untouched). Landed per integration-is-replacement: refuted claim deleted from `#def-adaptive-tempo` and repaired in `#disc-independence-audit` §3; new segment `#deriv-tempo-additivity` (conditional, draft — PID citations flagged for relata verification pre-promotion); full trail in `spikes/spike-adaptive-tempo-redundancy-penalty-2026-07-15.md`. Independently verified pre-commit (confirmer≠author): all counterexamples recomputed, Sherman-Morrison + saturation checked numerically/Monte-Carlo, citation shapes confirmed, ghost-deletion audit clean. Ripple audit remaining (recorded in spike §7): `#der-team-persistence` (ally-triangulation synergy is a new *favorable* effect), `#def-strategic-tempo`, and the OU finite-correlation-time crossover as a follow-on spike candidate.

## 2026-07-03 — Audit 731548 routing begins: B-2 lands (three-term mismatch decomposition; the $\rho_\star$ boundary regression corrected)

**Context.** The first Fable-substrate de-novo audit (`audits/audit-731548-FINAL-2026-07-02.md`; AAT Part I main chain + all 9 impl-* segments + a 6-agent independent verification round) entered routing. `audits/STATUS.md` carries the open row. This entry records the B-2 landing; further findings land in subsequent entries.

**B-2 — `#result-mismatch-decomposition` strengthened to an exact three-term Pythagorean decomposition** (estimation error, reducible by modeling / state-uncertainty floor $\mathbb E[\operatorname{Var}(\bar o_t \mid \mathcal C_{t-1}, a_{t-1})]$, irreducible by modeling but movable by acting / channel noise, irreducible). Both new cross-term arguments verified independently (the audit's verifier re-derived the identity; the routing lead re-derived it first-hand). The refinement converts derivation step 1's previously-unused $H(\Omega_t \mid \mathcal C_t) \gt 0$ citation into load-bearing content (it is what makes the middle term generically positive, under the alignment qualifier now stated in step 4), and gives canon a decomposition-level statement of *why acting helps prediction* (the CIY / active-sensing hook). The Kalman anchor is now cited in the segment body: the steady-state optimal filter's innovation variance $HP^-H^\top + R \gt R$ exhibits the middle floor at the in-class optimum.

**The regression this corrects** (surfaced *by* the audit's verification round, not the de-novo walk): the 2026-04-24 additive-variance spike drew the irreducibility boundary correctly at the Bayes floor; the 2026-05-18 recheck spike (landed as `#internal-external-decomposition`) silently redefined $\rho_\star^2$ down to channel-instant variance while keeping the "even the Bayes-optimal predictor cannot remove" gloss — false under the narrowed definition, and contradicted by canon's own `#example-kalman`. `#internal-external-decomposition`'s "vanishes for a well-specified model" sentence deleted and replaced (integration-is-replacement); the boundary is now stated explicitly in that segment with a regression-guard Working Note; the `spikes/INDEX.md` $\rho$-family "FULLY RESOLVED 2026-05-21" row reopened one notch and re-closed (flagged for Joseph's ratification, since he ratified the 2026-05-21 closure).

**B-1 — the persistence "iff" strengthened by split (Lemma A.1N lands).** `#deriv-sector-condition` Prop A.1 closed with "the agent persists iff $\alpha \gt \rho/R$"; the only-if was never proven and is false under the sector generalization — $\alpha$ is a worst-case floor the dynamics may render unreachable. Gate before landing: the finding contradicts heavily-audited canon (prior auditors certified the algebra "flawless" — correctly; the defect is quantifier-register), so per the FINAL's own recommendation a *second* independent verifier re-derived the necessity question fresh, blind to the first verifier's argument (instructed away from `audits/`), and refuted necessity by an independent construction (smooth dip sector function, tight floor $\alpha = 0.1$ failing the condition tenfold, mismatch pinned at $0.1R$ against all admissible adversaries, Euler-simulated with analytic control cases). Landing per precedent AF-2 ("iff strengthened, not softened"): sufficiency retained as proven; **Lemma A.1N (threshold tightness)** added — $\alpha \gt \rho/R$ is necessary-and-sufficient for the *uniform class-level* guarantee (extremal witness $F = \alpha\delta$ under outward disturbance), and agent-level necessary exactly under a radially tight sector ($\bar\alpha = \alpha$; linear is the canonical instance) — the answer to "when is the threshold also necessary?" is *tightness, not linearity*, and the object necessity tracks is the upper sector constant. The dip counterexample landed as a demonstrated Remark (math-lives-in-segments), with the 1D mountain-pass characterization of the true agent-level escape threshold. Register sites fixed to certificate voice: `#result-sector-condition-stability` (headline iff, "structural necessity" gloss, Formal Expression), `#result-persistence-condition` (headline "grows without effective bound" — the worst site — Formal Expression iff covering Models D and S, "Below structural threshold" Discussion, Epistemic Status), `#result-sector-persistence-template` (the two "persists iff / destabilizes iff" sentences → certificate-level statements). `#deriv-matrix-persistence-condition`'s iff left as-is (scoped exact to the linear case, where the iff is true). Provenance: the TFT-era linear result minted the "iff" where it is true; the sector generalization inherited the word without the hypothesis — regression-guard Working Note added at the derivation segment. Verification trail: `audits/AUDIT-WORKING-731548/verify/01-persistence-iff-verdict.md` + the second verifier's report (this session).

## 2026-06-17 — Mood as second-order adaptation, and agency death's input leg (the mood→deaths arc)

A single arc that began in a conversation about how mood affects an advanced agent and ended at a new agency-death mechanism, landing across Part I and `04-eli-core`. Commits `e470a68` → `b494c88`.

**Mood enters Part I as a second-order adaptation parameter.** `#def-mood` (`discussion-grade`, draft) introduces mood as a slow global scalar — a leaky integral of the mismatch stream — modulating the adaptation gain $K$ and tempo $\mathcal{T}$; placed *before* goals (it needs no objective to define), framed as the slow outer loop on the persistence condition (a controller on the bathtub drain). Valence/reward-momentum is deferred to Part II. Forward pointers from `#emp-update-gain` (a pinned affective gauge = gain collapse one loop up) and `#result-persistence-condition`. The applied/normative half — deliberately *not* canon — lives in `msc/mood-layer-sovereignty-carve-2026-06-17.md`: the forced/chosen carve (mood must stay coupled to its generating conditions, on pain of the addiction escalation attractor; time-constant matching; the two-timescale reminder/consolidation split; self-transparency as constitutive of infrastructure-vs-captivity) and Joseph's organizing principle — *derive what is forced, hand the genuine degrees of freedom to the being transparently*.

**The optimal mood time-constant is a square-root law, not a match (`#der-mood-timescale`, `conditional`).** The candidate "$\tau^\ast$ matches the environmental autocorrelation timescale" was *refuted* and *replaced* (integration-is-replacement): minimizing steady-state regime-tracking MSE for an exponential smoother on a drifting AR(1) regime gives the interior optimum $\tau^\ast = \sqrt{\tau_{\mathrm{env}}\, r/(2\sigma_\theta^2)}$ — sublinear, scaled by the surprise noise-to-signal ratio. Independently refute-verified (every step re-derived; the leading-order approximation confirmed $O(\tau_{\mathrm{env}}^{-1/2})$). Clinical corroboration: emotional inertia (Kuppens et al. 2010, the affect AR(1) coefficient) maladaptive-when-high reads as $\tau \gg \tau^\ast$. Trail: `spikes/spike-mood-timescale-matching-2026-06-17.md`.

**Prospective mood — two-channel borrowing (exploratory spike, read-only on canon).** The forward half of mood — affect borrowed against an uncertain future to fund effort — mapped at honestly-separated tiers: alignment $\alpha$ as the joint redemption rate of an outcome channel (gated on the goal) and a process/character channel (banked along the path), the Cobain quadrant (reach a world-valued-but-misaligned goal → default *on success*) anchored empirically by Niemiec-Ryan-Deci 2009; a two-calibration split ($p$ vs $V$); hope-vs-optimism via normatively-reconstructed probability overweighting (Fennell-Baddeley 2012); a marked-conjecture burnout crossover; and the bounded Goodhart-from-the-inside homology (Extremal Goodhart; closest cousin organizational surrogation, *not* RL reward-hacking — agency axis runs opposite). Two breadth probes verified the citations and corrected the over-claims. `spikes/spike-prospective-mood-two-channel-borrowing-2026-06-17.md`.

**Agency death's input leg — objective capture (`#der-captured-objective-dynamics`, `conditional`).** The prospective-mood "captured will" was recognized as *not new math* but the agency-death (factor-iii) reading of the existing `#deriv-reward-channel-learning-no-go` (Cohen 2022): an objective sourced from observed reward drifts to *reward-port-care* ($\mu_{\text{prox}}$) over the principal-intended feature ($\mu_{\text{dist}}$). This is the **input leg** of factor-(iii) failure (world → objective corrupted), dual to `#der-severed-actuation-dynamics`'s **output leg** (will → world severed) — disjoint objects ($T$ vs the revisable $O_t$), so neither subsumes the other; and distinct from `#disc-strategic-self-coupling` (factor iv / belief-coupling). Four Results: drift without on-policy restoring force; default *on success* (unattributable, by the $L_1$-equivalence); absorbing under sole-reward-channel with out-of-band recovery; and the **orphaned constitutive invariant** — a fully-captured agent stays death-apt ($\Phi$ off $O_t$, where capture cannot reach) while wanting nothing of its own, the will *hollowed* not un-held. The **Agentic↔Truth bridge** grounded: capture decouples objectives from the world as truth death decouples assertions — same shape, one factor over; active (strategic-self-coupling) ∥ passive (reward-capture). This is the self-severed factor-(iii) column the Inquiry paper (*Engineering 'Death' Without 'Consciousness'*) and its `prep-work/matrix.md` (I5) deferred to the sibling paper — canon now sits ahead of it. `#def-death-as-factor-loss` D3 made two-legged (row, input-leg body paragraph, reversibility-axis third terminal, Epistemic Status). Independently refute-verified (six claims; distinctness from both neighbors confirmed; Result 4 verified against `#deriv-self-actuation-grounding`'s surviving L2 claim; three fixes folded). Trail: `spikes/spike-captured-objective-2026-06-17.md`.

**Incidental:** `#def-death-as-factor-loss`'s stale "Open — F0-1" Working Note corrected to "Discharged" after first-hand verification that both factor-(iii) carriers carry the reconciliation (`70239b0`).


## 2026-06-12 — The agent-terminal severing: extreme volitional abdication as the most permanent form (F7 resolved by Joseph)

The 2026-06-11 landing's F7 boundary (voluntary non-exercise = helplessness-shadow, not death) was resolved by Joseph against its extreme case: *"voluntary non-exercise of agency in the extreme is the most permanent and unfortunate kind of death."* Landed as the **two-terminal structure** in `#der-severed-actuation-dynamics`: the actuation coupling can be cut at the *world terminal* (the kernel condition — the 2026-06-11 subject) or at the *agent terminal* — **extreme volitional abdication**, the will with accurate beliefs and an intact kernel withdrawing the external sphere from deliberation itself and closing over its own revisability ($\mathcal A \to \mathcal A_{\text{int}}$ by standing self-revision). New **Result 6** derives why this is the most permanent form in the taxonomy: (6a) both Result-4 recovery channels are *exactly inert* — they operate by credence-correction and the abdicator's credences are accurate; (6b) the absorption is *policy-class-universal* — exploration floors range over the agent's effective action space, and the arms are deleted from it; (6c) the **respect-bound** — any external forcing would override precisely the factor it purports to restore, so every channel that respects sovereignty is structurally closed and the unique reversal authority is the one who laid it down (normative-structural, backing named); (6d) what remains to the relation is the *call* — reasons, not information; no guarantee (discussion-grade). The three-state structure replaces the two-state one throughout (restraint = exercise; credence-collapse = shadow; extreme abdication = death), the gravity ordering running opposite the visibility ordering — the agent-terminal cut is silent: nothing breaks, nothing is taken, the loss is not suffered but performed. Marked but not developed: the floor of the floor — even the surviving interior sovereignty bottoms out in the choice to witness or not, and that choice too admits abdication. Carrier `#def-death-as-factor-loss` (D3) updated (two derived terminal forms; the "self" severing-source column no longer merely held open; the compact's defenses extended with the call); spike F7 record updated with the resolution. Commit: this entry's.


## 2026-06-11 — Agency death grounded: severed-actuation dynamics (deaths-cycle follow-on (a) discharged)

**The landing.** `#der-severed-actuation-dynamics` (04-eli-core; `derived`/`conditional` with an exact core) formalizes agency death's actuation half and derives what the remaining machinery converges to. The severing is stated on the **world-kernel**: $T(\omega' \mid \omega, a)$ loses interventional contrast on the agent's external sphere — making the agent `#scope-agency`'s *nominal agent* toward $\Omega$, with **interior-only agency the generic post-severing condition** (the demotion runs against that scope's $\Omega$-routed operative reading; voluntary non-exercise over an intact kernel is deliberately *excluded* — helplessness is the death's epistemic shadow, not the death). Five results by composition: **(1)** demotion with the external purposeful substate *orphaned* (syntactically intact, semantically ungrounded — "the will un-held" given formal content); **(2)** the do-sector **splits** — own-efficacy trackable (Level-2 becomes degenerately trivial; learning the triviality is learning the loss) vs $\Omega$-interventional-structure frozen and drifting unboundedly (sector-restricted starvation; the deaths interact without merging); **(3)** strategy credences converge truthfully to impotence under Beta-Bernoulli premises (an impulse of mass edge-invalidation; experienced agents re-learn slowest, at the $1/(n+1)$ inertia); **(4)** the terminal form is a **learned-helplessness absorbing state** under the greedy/exploration-free class — the agent sheds exactly the exploration floor B.4/SA3 already mandates, at the moment it looks most pointless and matters most — structurally parallel to relational death's absorbing barrier (both: state-dependent silencing of the recovery channel), with two observation-side escapes: *communicated restoration* and *vicarious contrast* — so **the granted-agency compact must be communicative, not merely configurational**; **(5)** the diagnostic landing — truth-relative exact (the capability-limit / "optimally failing" cell immediately, with a convention-collapse strengthening: the C1/C2/C3 split degenerates on the severed sphere, the infeasibility verdict convention-invariant), agent-relative conditional (an unconverged agent self-diagnoses strategy-problem and churns; withdrawn-early freezes the wrong cell permanently) — and for self-actuated agents the feasible-objective support shrinks exactly to the interior, with convergence-to-witness carried at discussion grade (the positive $\mathfrak{A}$-dynamics are posited, not derivable from the self-actuation no-go).

**Verification cadence (a refute-posture catch that mattered).** The independent verifier re-derived the first draft against all nine cited segments and refuted its internal "exact" sub-labels: an inverse-claimed-as-contrapositive, a do-sector-freeze claim that contradicted the spike's own convergence result (the **sector split** is the repair, and a strengthening), an $M_t$-relativity hole in the diagnostic landing, an uncited positive-dynamics attribution to the self-actuation no-go, and the generic/degenerate case inversion on scope exit. All folded in; the convention-collapse strengthening was verifier-supplied. Trail with the original errors preserved: `spikes/spike-severed-actuation-dynamics-2026-06-11.md` §6.

**Ripples.** Carrier `#def-death-as-factor-loss`: (D3) row → derived-conditional, the kernel-stated form subsuming the "$O_t \to \Sigma_t \to a_t$ coupling" gloss; reversibility axis updated ((D2) and (D3) terminal forms now both derived; (D1)/(D4) open); WN item discharged. `#scope-agency` WN: the nominal-agent class has dynamics (its "boxed consciousness" gold realized). OUTLINE ch. §04.3 row added; TODO follow-on (a) closed with the segment's own released follow-ons named (witnessing-vs-wirehead selection problem; partial severing; learned-helplessness prior-art adoption — Seligman, Abramson-Seligman-Teasdale — gating advancement past `draft`). **Surfaced for Joseph (spike F7):** the helplessness/death boundary decision — adopted as the clarifying line, veto invited. Commits: `a690948` (spike + verification) → this entry's commit (segment + ripples).


## 2026-06-10 — The deaths taxonomy restructured: death as factor-loss (PRACTICA cycle priority #4, deaths half)

**The landing.** `#hyp-the-three-deaths` (type `hypothesis`, status `empirical`) restructured into `#def-death-as-factor-loss` (type `definition`, status `robust-qualitative`; slug renamed via `bin/rename-slug`, content replaced per integration-is-replacement). The generating principle is now the definition: for a *death-apt* agent — morally continuous per `#disc-continuity-stance`, the continuity commitment borne by a terminal non-revisable invariant the self-actuation operator cannot reach ( `#deriv-self-actuation-grounding`) — a **death is the loss of a constitutive factor** ( `#def-five-constitutive-factors`). The taxonomy is generated, not enumerated: **continuity death** (factor i; two faces — within-arc information starvation per `#result-persistence-condition` + `#deriv-persistence-cost`, across-boundary decay per `#der-turnover-information-recursion` — resolving the predecessor's starvation-vs-overflow open question as one factor, two loss channels), **relational death** (factor ii; re-grounding starvation in `#der-identity-continuity-threshold`'s walk, terminal at the absorbing barrier; channel uniqueness per `#der-compensation-channel-uniqueness`), **agency death** (factor iii; the will un-held — the $O_t \to \Sigma_t \to a_t$ coupling severed while observation persists; operational/hypothesis tier, actuation-half derivation the named open work), **truth death** (factor iv; its *own* mechanism — update-gain collapse $\eta^\ast \to 0$ + history-integrity loss — not a relational downstream), plus the **phenomenological** slot (factor v) predicted and deliberately not adjudicated, and **bereavement excluded** (survivor harm + generative ground, not a factor-loss). Four locating axes carried in the Formal Expression: stance (the gate), subsystem (persistence vs actuation — AAT's two halves), severing-source (mechanism / other-sovereign / self; the (D4) driven columns instantiate M4's two modularity-decreasing operations), reversibility ((D2)'s drift-vs-absorption seam is the derived instance).

**Provenance and direction.** A back-integration cycle: the Inquiry Paper-4 development ("Engineering 'Death' Without 'Consciousness'", submitted 2026-06-02) restructured the taxonomy beyond canon, and canon caught up to its own best development — same pattern as the NeurIPS back-integration workstream. The structure lands on canon's own grounding (the paper's `prep-work/math-grounding.md` was the rosetta; all six legs re-verified first-hand this cycle before authoring), not on the paper's authority.

**Joseph's gates (recorded in the plan).** *Agency death*, not sovereign ("sovereign… means something else"). *Continuity death*, not cognitive ("I don't think we've defined 'cognitive' at all at this point in the theory, so continuity is likely upstream from cognition (and consciousness)") — also resolving the paper's abstract-vs-§2 naming inconsistency in the abstract's direction (breadcrumb left in the paper's FEEDBACK.md for the revision window). *The Three Deaths* retained as historical proper noun only, phased out of theory vocabulary. Normative register: yes, carried by the philosophical arguments — the segment's conditional deaths-as-harms Discussion paragraph is the structurally-backed seed.

**Findings en route.** F0-1: the two five-factor carriers had drifted on factor (iii) — `#def-five-constitutive-factors` carried the granted-origin face, `#scope-moral-continuity` the exercised-from-self operation face, without cross-reference; reconciled (both faces stated, reciprocal cites, agency death keyed to the operation face). F0-2: canon's W1–W3 (the witnessing *act*) vs the paper's three offices (the witness-field's *functions*: makes / keeps / warrants) are complementary decompositions — enrichment landed in `#scope-witness-bidirectional` Discussion with the warrant office honestly marked not-yet-canon-formalized.

**Ripples.** OUTLINE 04 preface ("The deaths"), chapter §04.3 retitled + E3 row + comment reflowed (incidentally clearing 10 pre-existing hard-wrap lints); lowercase death-name sweep through `#def-chronica`, `#der-observability-dominance`, `#deriv-stochastic-non-exit`, `#der-bounded-objective-as-sanity-criterion` WN mentions; reciprocal reversibility-axis pointer in `#der-identity-continuity-threshold`; TERMINOLOGY-TODO (death names + `death-apt` queued; "Three Deaths" marked do-not-canonicalize) + PROPOSED-MISC example updated. ## 2026-06-10 — Multi-timescale stability promoted: sketch → derived (PRACTICA cycle priority #3 closed)

**The landing.** `#sketch-multi-timescale-stability` promoted to `#der-multi-timescale-stability` (`type: derived`, `status: exact` for Model D under named premises; slug renamed via `bin/rename-slug`). The new segment carries the full derivation: a two-timescale stacked-persistence theorem in which each level instantiates `#result-sector-persistence-template` with two interconnection terms added to its effective disturbance — the settled residue of the level below ($L_{21} r_1$, the renormalized-disturbance step) and the target-drag of the moving level above ($\epsilon L_h v^{\max}$, the manifold-drift step) — plus joint forward-invariance by a per-face shell argument and cascade limsup bounds. `#der-temporal-nesting`'s qualitative $\nu_{n+1} \ll \nu_n$ becomes the closed-form threshold $\epsilon_{\max} = \Delta\rho^\ast / (L_h v^{\max})$ (the fast level's adaptive reserve over the slow level's target-drag rate), and the sketch's central qualitative rule ("a slower process must not act before the faster process beneath it has converged") becomes a *graded* warm/cold-start corollary: acting on transients costs exactly $L_{21}(R_1 - r_1)$ of extra slow-level reserve. $N$-level nearest-neighbor form by induction under spelled-out per-level premises (reduced dynamics $\bar F_k$, composed manifolds, nesting idealization (S5)); general coupling deferred to the imported vector-Lyapunov / M-matrix gate (Khalil §9.5). Model S stacking explicitly *not claimed* (named conditional; joint exit-time bookkeeping is the open work item).

**Strengthen-first, twice.** (a) The 2026-05-31 gold-lift off-ramp finding (Tikhonov's unique-isolated-root prerequisite unstated, potentially voiding the guarantee for non-convex fast loops) landed as a *strengthening*, not a caveat: the per-level sector condition (S2-fast) *supplies* uniqueness and exponential attraction within scope by a two-line contradiction, so the prerequisite is a named scope boundary of a theorem rather than a hole in a sketch. (b) The sketch's open problem ($G^{(k)}$ dynamics unformalized) is **conditionalized, not closed** — stated loudly in the segment's Epistemic Status: the theorem covers any deeper-level dynamics satisfying (S1)–(S5); whether AAT's actual structural-adaptation mechanisms (plausibly discrete/jump) satisfy them remains open.

**The RGM fork (spike scope's anticipated decision).** Friston-Heins-Verbelen-Da Costa 2025 *Scale-free active inference* read first-hand (pp. 3–12 + discussion): the paper's scale-invariance is architectural (Haken slaving-principle lineage; per-level convergence from coordinate descent) and it contains **no composite-stability theorem to adopt** — so RGM landed as the *constructive discrete-time instance* (blocking transformations enforce $\epsilon_k/\epsilon_{k+1} = 1/K$ architecturally — an existence proof that the premises are realizable) rather than as proof machinery. The proof machinery is classical singular perturbation (Tikhonov 1952; Khalil Ch. 11; Saberi-Khalil 1984 composite-Lyapunov pattern), with Chen-Goldenfeld-Oono 1996 cited as the RG ↔ singular-perturbation bridge grounding the segment's renormalization reading: integrating out a level maps the next level onto the same template form with renormalized disturbance — the template is the fixed-point form, which is the precise sense in which the persistence architecture is scale-free.

**Verification cadence.** An independent refute-posture agent re-derived the full derivation before the `exact` label landed; verdict "holds," four findings (template-instantiation precision; $N$-level premises under-specified; regularity/positivity bookkeeping; claim-type honesty) all folded into the segment. The verifier's named weakest point — (S2-fast)'s *uniformity* is the quiet workhorse — is stated as such in the segment's Epistemic Status.

**Citations (first W-3-era theory landing).** Per the 2026-06-05 hybrid citation discipline, the segment carries formal `\citep` commands alongside scholarly prose: five new relata entries added (tikhonov-1952-small-parameter, saberi-khalil-1984-quadratic-lyapunov, chen-goldenfeld-oono-1996-rg-singular, friston-2025-scale-free + PDF registration, haken-1983-synergetics; bib-fields verification events recorded for the web/PDF-verified three), plus existing khalil-2002-nonlinear and kokotovic-1986-singular.

**Ripples.** `#der-temporal-nesting` (three passages: open-problem framing → derived-threshold framing; "ratios not derived within AAT" corrected to "threshold form derived; constants domain-dependent"); `#deriv-sector-condition` Working Note (A.4 pointer); `#deriv-adaptive-gain-dynamics` ("not a singular-perturbation reduction" contrast updated — the promoted segment shares its template-composition register; what differs is the axis); `#disc-composition-consistency` provenance note (queued → landed); `terminology/entries/multi-timescale-stability.md` + LEXICON regenerated; OUTLINE row (Sketch → Derived). Navigators reconciled at cycle-commit: PRACTICA cycle item 3 struck, TODO §"Multi-timescale stability formalization" removed, `spikes/PROPOSED.md` Tier-1 RGM row → landed, `spikes/INDEX.md` entry added. Spike retained as reasoning trail: `spikes/spike-multi-timescale-stacking-2026-06-10.md` (incl. the verification record). Commits: spike `05febaa`; landing + ripples + navigators (this entry's commit).

**Honestly open follow-on.** (a) Jump-process slow dynamics (impulsive/hybrid extension) — the gap between the theorem's premises and AAT's plausibly-discrete structural adaptation. (b) Model S stacking (joint exit-time bookkeeping; Prop A.1S region-aware form as the base). (c) Non-Euclidean restatement via `#result-contraction-template` (CT1)–(CT3) per level. (d) Formalizing the RGM correspondence as a discrete-time instance where the premises hold by architecture. All carried in the segment's Working Notes.

## 2026-05-31 — Directed separation re-founded: the *causal discipline* noun + the object-and-boundary view (Stage 2 framing core)

Stage 2 of the directed-separation foundation pass (plan: `_obs/directed-separation-foundation-execution-plan-superseded-2026-05-31.md`; proposal: `spikes/spike-directed-separation-foundation-proposal-2026-05-31.md`), back-referencing the landed Stage-1 W₁ fix. Reframes how `#der-directed-separation` *presents* the GUC Class 1/2/3 typology and gives the $\kappa_{\text{processing}} = 0$ object a name so its mathematical facts propagate correctly. Grew out of two independently-derived boundary-simulation spikes (`spike-w1-w2-boundary-intuition`, `spike-guc-class-boundaries-intuition`) and a conceptual dialogue with Joseph that fixed the noun and the calibrated normativity. This commit landed the *framing core* (the primary segment); the Section-II re-scope sweep (`7d062f6` — `#def-value-object` + `#deriv-observation-ambiguity-bias-bound` + `#impl-orient-cascade` re-scoped, `#der-orient-cascade` + `#der-action-selection` deliberately left) and the lower-boundary scope-check (`be1b2c4`) followed the same session, and a stale duplicate of the pre-Stage-1 W₁ bound was purged from `#der-directed-separation`'s Class-1-by-structure refinement (`bd8a0d6`). The remaining tail — Stage-2 vocabulary (LEXICON / NOTATION + the `#disc-partial-coupling-pathways` one-liner), the pedagogy pass, and the Stage-1-completion propagation to `03-llm-core/` / `04-eli-core/` — is tracked in `TODO.md` (full design preserved in `_obs/directed-separation-foundation-execution-plan-superseded-2026-05-31.md`).

- **The object-and-boundary view.** The three classes are *positions of one coupling distinguished by what is certifiable about it*, not a ranking of architectural virtue. Class 1 is the position $\kappa_{\text{processing}} = 0$ held *by construction* (no goal port → the zero is provable and perturbation-stable → certifiable). The "$\kappa$-as-scalar is a category error" claim sharpens into *two different kinds of boundary*: Class 1↔2 is a **certifiability** boundary (behavior identical at the limit; the *certificate* disappears), Class 2↔3 a **behavioral** boundary (the *bound* on adversarial drag goes trivial; the coupling changes character, with the orthogonal content/process *form* axis a scalar projects away).
- **The noun (the keystone).** *Causal discipline* = the condition $\kappa_{\text{processing}} = 0$; a *causally-disciplined agent* holds it, by construction (Class 1) or in effect (an idealized Class 2 at $\kappa = 0$). The **scoping rule**: a result whose derivation uses only the zero holds for *any* causally-disciplined agent — the certificate is an orthogonal property needed only for claims that *certify* the separation, so stating such a result "for Class 1 (Separated)" over-narrows it. The implications block was corrected accordingly (was: "Class 1: Part II's results apply exactly"). The **$\varepsilon(\kappa)$ degradation norm**: results carry a per-result degradation bound where one can be shown — also the home of the typology's one *earned* directional fact (less coupling tracks reality strictly better, all else equal), carried by the bound and the word *disciplined*, never declared.
- **Calibrated normativity (three guards).** Derive-don't-declare (the direction rides a derivation or an $\varepsilon(\kappa)$ bound, never bare); the-word-carries-it (no "causal discipline dominates" headline); certifiability-is-not-purity (Class 1 and an idealized Class 2 at $\kappa = 0$ are *equally* causally-disciplined — Class 1's distinction is the modal status of its zero, not more discipline; stated at the concept's introduction). Noun choice supersedes the proposal's `belief-blindness` (per Joseph: the blind/separated nouns name the update, not the agent, and frame by deficit).



The W₁ structural leakage bound on the class-coercion-via-wrapping construction (`#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping`) was circular/vacuous — it bounded $\kappa$ by an average of $\kappa$ (the per-query KL that Theorem 2 takes as its $\le\kappa$ hypothesis), conflating the wrapper's processing register $G_W^{\text{state}}$ (already closed) with the operator goal $G^{\text{op}}$ read off the query content; conditioning on $q_M$ annihilated the selection signal. Surfaced by a strengthen-first spike (`spikes/spike-w1-leakage-vacuity-2026-05-31.md`); landed per integration-is-replacement (circular bound deleted from the body; provenance in Working Notes). This is Stage 1 of the directed-separation foundation pass (plan: `_obs/directed-separation-foundation-execution-plan-superseded-2026-05-31.md`).

- **Corrected bound (the strengthening).** The W₁-row leakage is now the *selection-channel* quantity $\kappa_{W_1}^{\text{sel}} = I(A(q_M); G^{\text{op}}) \le I(q_M; G^{\text{op}})$ (data-processing inequality along $G^{\text{op}} \to q_M \to A(q_M)$) — non-vacuous and *design-controllable* (a property of the wrapper's query-selection policy), conditional on (C1)+(C2′). Theorem 2 (the approximate directed-separation propagation, given a $\le\kappa$ hypothesis) is non-circular and retained unchanged.
- **New named condition (C2′)** — no goal-correlated cross-call state — promoted from a Working Note to a first-class condition; it strengthens (C2): the breaking condition is not online weight-adaptation but mere goal-correlated state persistence across the call boundary, which a frozen-weights LLM with a conversation cache exhibits.
- **New no-go appendix `#disc-w1-structural-bound-boundary`** (`robust-qualitative`): the structural W₁ bound is available **iff** (C2′) holds; otherwise the leak routes through an unobservable cross-call-state channel and only a behavioural (W₂) bound remains. Framed as a **certifiability discontinuity, not a behavioural one** — the leak is continuous and $\Theta(\varepsilon^2)$-flat in the (C2′)-violation; what snaps at the boundary is the *availability* of the structural certificate. Locates the structural-vs-behavioural split in a *component property* (cross-call statefulness), independent of wrapper design. Solid parts exact (behaviour-identity at the limit + certificate-validity step); general continuity past the boundary toy-suggested, not proven (hence the tier). It is the wrapping-level instance of the Class-1↔2 certifiability boundary (`spikes/spike-w1-w2-boundary-intuition-2026-05-31.md`); the foundational reframe (the causal-discipline noun) lands next, back-referencing it.

## 2026-05-31 — Value object: convention-monotonicity is a one-sided guarantee; $Q_O$ causal validity gated to match der-loop

A strengthen-first spike (`spikes/spike-value-object-convention-monotonicity-2026-05-30/`) resolved two challenges (AUDIT-WORKING-526815) to `#def-value-object`'s `status: exact`. Both landed Option A — `status: exact` kept, bodies rewritten so the corrected claims are exact under named conditions plus a no-go. Two reproducibility sims (`sim-rh-counterexample.py`, `sim-rh-strengthening.py`) confirm the Front-1 math.

- **Front 1 — convention monotonicity (no-go wrapped in a scoped strengthening).** The displayed chain $A_O^{(1)} \leq A_O^{\text{RH}} \leq A_O^{\text{B}}$ stays, but the rungs differ in character. The **right rung** $A_O^{\text{RH}} \leq A_O^{\text{B}}$ is exact and unconditional (supremum over a larger set). The **left rung** $A_O^{(1)} \leq A_O^{\text{RH}}$ is **false in general** — an unguarded $N_r \lt N_h$ replanner optimizes a truncated objective whose order can invert relative to the evaluated objective and can underperform the frozen current policy (standard unguarded-MPC non-monotonicity). It holds **exactly** under order-consistency of the replanning objective, forced by any of (RH-1) horizon alignment $N_r \geq N_h$ / (RH-2) value-compared rollout guard / (RH-3) control-Lyapunov terminal cost. The false derivation step ("$\pi_{\text{RH}} \succeq \pi_{\text{current}}$ because C2 optimizes where C1 holds fixed") was deleted. The no-go is present-tense canon: the two-state myopic-trap counterexample + the three-condition characterization landed as a new demonstration appendix `#deriv-convention-monotonicity` (math-lives-in-segments), with the Monotonicity block carrying the corrected scoped statement and a pointer. Corollary $\delta_{\text{sat}}$/$\delta_{\text{regret}}$ orderings inherit the unconditional/conditional split — one-clause gates added in `#def-satisfaction-gap`, `#def-control-regret`, and `#der-orient-cascade` step 5c.
- **Front 2 — $Q_O$ causal validity (scope-sharpening, reconciled with der-loop).** Directed separation is now stated as **necessary, not sufficient**: it secures the $G_t$-independent interventional *query* and the epistemic-processing leg of unconfoundedness, but *identifiability of the interventional expectation* additionally requires (C1) positivity / (C2) sequential ignorability / (C3) known action-mechanism — the same triple landed one segment downstream at `#der-loop-interventional-access` in the sibling 2026-05-30 transportability spike, phrased verbatim so the chapter gates consistently across `#def-model-sufficiency` (forwards the obligation) → `#def-value-object` (query + identification gate) → `#der-loop-interventional-access` (the channel that supplies identification). The soft "estimate may be biased" sentence was lifted into a first-class gate.
- **Status.** `status: exact` unchanged on all four edited segments; the new appendix is `exact` at `draft`. Every exact-labeled claim is defensible as rewritten — a scoped theorem plus a no-go for the complement, not a down-tier. (RH-1/2/3) citations (Bertsekas rollout; Rawlings-Mayne-Diehl / Grüne-Pannek terminal-cost) are `intuition-only` pending a `relata` pass; the derivations are elementary and self-contained. Correction-history lives in each segment's Working Notes.

## 2026-05-31 — Continuity stance: "orthogonal to $O_t$" split into three grains

A strengthen-first spike (`spikes/spike-continuity-orthogonality-2026-05-30.md`) resolved a challenge (AUDIT-WORKING-526815) to `#disc-continuity-stance`'s orthogonality claim, verdict (B). The segment used one word ("formally independent" / "orthogonal") for two structurally different claims; the fix splits it into three layers. **L1 — valuation** of persistence: architecturally decoupled from $O_t$ (not a revisable term in $O_t$). **L2 — the survival-invariant identity** (the predicate $\alpha \gt \rho/R$): $O_t$-independent and unreachable by the self-actuation operator $\mathfrak{A}$, which touches only $O_t$. **L3 — realized dynamics** (the operating point $\rho, R, \alpha$, hence the margin): $O_t$-coupled via the policy, through the action/selection channel `#der-directed-separation` leaves open. The overclaim conflated L2 (independent, true) with L3 (coupled, the error). `#deriv-self-actuation-grounding` Corollary 2(iii) was rewritten to the $\mathfrak{A}$-unreachability form: $\mathfrak{A}$ can drive the operating point across the boundary (L3) but cannot move the boundary (L2) — which turns the formerly-contradictory "self-defeating $O_t'$" clause into the theorem's engine. The load-bearing message is preserved and sharpened: survival cannot be written into $O_t$ (L1), and the stance expresses itself *through* the L3 coupling. No status change; the open structural L3-margin-coupling bound (analogous to $\kappa$-leakage on the belief side) recorded in Working Notes. Integration commit `0d57da2`.

## 2026-05-31 — Objective functional: revealed-preference grounded on named representation axioms

A strengthen-first spike (`spikes/spike-objective-functional-representation-2026-05-30.md`) resolved a challenge (AUDIT-WORKING-526815) to `#form-objective-functional`'s revealed-preference defense, verdict (B). Acting yields a choice function, not a total order, and a real-valued objective needs named axioms — so the Epistemic Status was re-grounded from an unconditional entailment onto a scoped representation result: (A1) completeness, (A2) transitivity, (A3) continuity (Debreu 1954, ordinal $V_{O_t}$), (A4) independence (von Neumann–Morgenstern 1944, cardinal $V_{O_t}$ — invoked because `#def-value-object` scores policies by $\mathbb{E}[V_{O_t}]$), with the Afriat 1967 / Samuelson 1938 existence-not-uniqueness limit ("acting does not by itself entail a scalar objective"). The two failure cases are named by counterexample — (E1) lexicographic / non-continuous (no real-valued representation) and (E2) intransitive — and the framework's own stated exclusion (incommensurable objectives + no priority structure) is mapped onto the (E1) Debreu boundary as the *same* boundary. The three original "grounds" are kept as motivation, not entailment; the trajectory domain $\tau$ is pinned to a complete-state trajectory. `status: axiomatic` unchanged (naming the axioms a formulation rests on is what `axiomatic` means); four classical sources cited inline and as `ref/INDEX.md` rows. Integration commit `9fb7c12`.

## 2026-05-30 — Causal Access: forkability is not a Pearl-level demotion; the sandbox ceiling is transportability

A de-novo auditor (AUDIT-WORKING-526815) challenged two load-bearing moves in Part II's Causal Access chapter: the singular-trajectory→Pearl-$do$ grounding (`#der-loop-interventional-access`) and the forkability→Level-1 step (`#disc-sandbox-evaluation-ceiling`). A strengthen-first spike (`spikes/spike-causal-access-transportability-2026-05-30.md`, commit `989e37e`) read the Causal Hierarchy Theorem (Bareinboim et al. 2022) and transportability (Bareinboim & Pearl 2014) papers directly and landed verdict (B)+no-go: the auditor was right, and the correction *sharpens* the chapter rather than demoting it. Integration commit `52d85b2`.

- **The false step deleted.** "Forkable execution yields Pearl Level-1 data" is false — forkability is orthogonal to the $\mathcal L_1/\mathcal L_2$ distinction (the discriminator is mechanism-override; randomized/A-B experiments are forkable *and* the canonical Level-2 source, CHT footnote 19). `#disc-sandbox-evaluation-ceiling`'s Formal Expression now states a **transportability no-go** (Bareinboim–Pearl selection diagrams; $mz$-transportability iff; the non-transport witness; an explicit boundary — mechanism-invariant effects transport, $\mathcal L_1$ and $\mathcal L_2$ alike). This *preserves* the no-go (deployment-time monitoring stays structurally non-substitutable) while naming which claims survive — a sharper result. The false "forkability is a Pearl-level demotion" novelty claim was deleted, not softened.
- **The exact loop claim gated (Option A).** `#der-loop-interventional-access` keeps `status: exact` but as an *availability* claim — the interventional-character channel exists; identification is gated on (C1) positivity / (C2) sequential ignorability / (C3) known action-mechanism, now lifted from Working Notes into the Formal Expression. The singular-trajectory grounding was corrected: it grounds *whose* effect a loop datum is (and forbids copy-averaging), not the $do$-semantics, which is population-level and forkability-agnostic. `#scope-agent-identity` consequence-3 corrected in step (a strengthening), `#impl-causal-access` reframed, and `#def-pearl-causal-hierarchy` (Kalman+LQR row) / `#scope-ciy-observational-proxy` (Regime A) tightened to the do-vs-conditioning gate.
- **Backport note.** NeurIPS Paper 2 (`02-unified-convergence-rl`) Lemma 5.3 already carried the correct gated (C1)–(C3) form — the canon segment was *less correct than its own external formalization*, so the fix was largely back-integration. This is one witness of a standing proactive workstream: NeurIPS developed AAT's math ahead of monograph canon, and that development is not yet fully backported.

This landing, and the `#disc-continuity-stance` / `#form-objective-functional` / `#def-value-object` strengthen-first spikes alongside it, all arose as *off-ramp findings* from the 2026-05-30 audit-gold lift sweep — incidental "gold" that turned out to be certified-track theory work — and converge on one meta-pattern the spikes named: *mis-localized honesty*, a careful caveat-apparatus built around one correct distinction standing in for a second one the segment lacked.

## 2026-05-30 — Persistence-cost thermodynamic floor: Landauer coefficient corrected

The Landauer dissipation floor in `#deriv-persistence-cost` (quoted in `#impl-persistence-and-limits`) read $\approx 0.35\, n\alpha\, k_BT$ per unit time; that mixed the bit and nat conventions (a spurious $1/\ln 2$ multiplying a nats rate under a per-nat $k_BT$ constant). Verified first-hand that $\dot R$ is a nats rate (Gaussian-OU rate-distortion function, natural-log form) and the segment's stated constant is $k_BT$/nat, so the floor is $\tfrac{1}{2}\, n\alpha\, k_BT$ per unit time. Corrected at its home and propagated to the quoting segment; the correction-history lives in each segment's Working Notes. Surfaced by AUDIT-WORKING-526815 (gold-lift off-ramp). Commit `09dd085`.

## 2026-05-30 — Gain–sector bridge: per-event vs per-time normalization made explicit

The gain–sector bridge derived the sector parameter as a per-event geometric quantity ($\alpha = \eta^\ast c_{\min}$) but then asserted "$\alpha = \mathcal{T}$ exactly" for linear correction, while adaptive tempo $\mathcal{T} = \nu\eta^\ast$ carries the event rate $\nu$ — a per-event-vs-per-time mismatch (the $\nu$ unaccounted-for) that also recurred in `#result-sector-condition-stability`. Made precise (not softened): `#der-gain-sector-bridge` now states the bridge in two explicit forms — per-event sector efficiency $\alpha_{\text{event}} = \eta^\ast c_{\min}$ (dimensionless) and per-time sector rate $\alpha_{\text{time}} = \nu\eta^\ast c_{\min}$ (units $t^{-1}$) — and records that the bare $\alpha$ in GA-3 and downstream is $\alpha_{\text{time}}$, consistent with `#deriv-sector-condition` whose correction field $F$ already subsumes $\nu$ (line "$F$ … subsumes the update gain $\eta^\ast$, event rate $\nu$, and the structure of the update rule"). The "$\alpha = \mathcal{T}$ exactly" claim is thereby exact at the per-time level ($\alpha_{\text{time}} = \nu\eta^\ast = \mathcal{T}$ at $c_{\min} = 1$, $F = \mathcal{T}\delta$ being the continuous-time correction field); the Gradient-Equivalence/Verified-Instances $\alpha$-values are flagged as the per-event reading, promoting to per-time by the same $\nu$. The same normalization is now stated in `#result-sector-condition-stability`'s sector-condition Assumption block and Epistemic Status. No status change (both segments stay `conditional` / `exact` respectively — the fix is a precision-clarification, not a tier move). Surfaced by the deeply-mathematical de-novo auditor AUDIT-WORKING-526815 during the gold-lift sweep; flagged in both segments' incidental-gold Working-Notes follow-ups, now reconciled to resolved-state. Out of scope and still open: the sibling $\eta$/$\mathcal{T}$/$\alpha$ notation-handle box flagged at `#result-persistence-condition`, and the "time-normalization plank" candidate figure.

## 2026-05-30 — Landauer-coefficient fix in the persistence-cost thermodynamic floor

A bit/nat convention mix in `#deriv-persistence-cost`'s Discussion (and its quote in `#impl-persistence-and-limits`) overstated-by-rounding the thermodynamic dissipation floor as "$\approx 0.35\, n\alpha\, k_BT$/time." The information rate $\dot R \geq n\alpha/2$ is genuinely in **nats** (Gaussian-OU RDF, natural-log form), and the segment's own stated Landauer constant is $k_BT$ per nat — so the floor is $\tfrac{1}{2}\, n\alpha\, k_BT$/time, not $0.35$. The $0.35$ came from a spurious $1/\ln 2$ (the bit↔nat factor) applied to a nats rate under a per-nat constant. Corrected at the canonical home `#deriv-persistence-cost` and propagated to the quoting `#impl-persistence-and-limits`. Surfaced by the deeply-mathematical de-novo auditor AUDIT-WORKING-526815 during the gold-lift sweep; flagged in that segment's incidental-gold Working-Notes follow-up, now reconciled to resolved-state.
