## 2026-05-29 — The anti-collapse discipline, named and landed (SP-26)

A recurring AAT move — *refusing to merge two things a naive model would treat as one, because the collapse hides a difference that routes to a different repair* — is named **anti-collapse** and given a canonical home. Gem-hunt origin: independently surfaced as a "which-parameter-responds-to-which-cause" pattern by two audits (472913, 963715) plus the 471203-cycle recognition (three-cycle convergence); a corpus instance sweep found 7 clean instances spanning all three Parts and reframed it from "which-knob" to *anti-collapse / individuation at the repair-relevant grain* (which absorbs the two-quantities sub-shape and the inverse same-knob case, `#scope-edge-update-causal-validity`).

Landed as `#disc-anti-collapse` in Meta-Architecture I — sibling-register to `#disc-constructive-impossibility-posture`, an epistemic-architectural *style* claim, explicitly **not** a fifth cross-sectional facet (it carries no theorem of its own). It leads with the $\beta$-vs-$\rho$ example for grounding, then the general shape + an instance table anchored on the strongest three ($\beta$-vs-$\rho$ `#form-information-bottleneck`; emitter-scalar-vs-recipient-regime `#der-interaction-channel-classification`; $\kappa$-vs-$\mathcal{A}$ `#scope-observation-ambiguity-modulation`), plus the inverse dual. Pedagogical placement (Joseph's design): a **plant** at the first instance ($\beta$-vs-$\rho$, Part I) forward-flagging the pattern; the full treatment here as cross-cutting vocabulary; an **earned recall** at the satisfaction-gap/control-regret split (`#def-satisfaction-gap`). The Meta-Architecture I preface introduces it as a second style claim alongside the constructive-impossibility posture. Intent: a reading lens that anchors a segment-by-segment read, grounded in a concrete instance rather than asserted abstractly. This is genuine clarifying novelty the "integration not invention" framing undersells (per `math-novelty-recognition`). Name and go-ahead: Joseph 2026-05-29.

## 2026-05-29 — Agency scope: the contrast channel that grounds causal-structure learning (SP-28)

Gem-hunt wave 2 surfaced a convergent finding (audits 742613 + 193847, independently): `#scope-agency`'s causal-effect condition $\exists\,a\neq a': P(o\mid do(a))\neq P(o\mid do(a'))$ has a subtlety, because $o_t = h(\Omega_t, a_{t-1}, \varepsilon_t)$ depends on the action directly — so the contrast can come from the action changing the *environment's* response (surfacing through $h$) or from the action changing only *what is observed of an unchanged $\Omega$* (active perception). Verified first-hand.

Landed as a `#scope-agency` Discussion clarification (not a formal-condition change): the channel distinction, with the $\Omega$-routed contrast identified as the substrate the purposeful machinery learns over (`#der-loop-interventional-access`'s Level-2 claim, the strategy DAG), and the boundary noted as observation-mediated in both directions (an $\Omega$-effect that never surfaces through $h$ is equally outside). Deliberately *not* a formal tightening: `#def-observation-function` intentionally admits active perception, so excluding active-perception-only contrast from the agency *set* would over-reach. The stronger formal-set move remains a Joseph-directable foundational call (PROPOSALS SP-28). This captures both convergent gems (the proprioceptive over-inclusion and the observation-mediation under-inclusion) conservatively.

## 2026-05-28 — Root definition reframed: agent-environment *coupling*, not agent-*object* (SP-24)

`#def-agent-environment` carried a latent contradiction surfaced by audit-773921 (Gemini, Finding 1): it defined an *agent* as an entity satisfying three constitutive conditions — the third being *"produces actions that affect $\Omega$"* — yet `#scope-adaptive-system` quantifies over $(\text{Agent}, \Omega)$ pairs whose canonical inhabitants (passive Bayesian learners, Kalman filters on passive signals) explicitly do not act, and `#scope-agency` names these *passive observers* squarely inside the adaptive scope. The root definition made action-with-effect constitutive too early.

Resolution (PROPOSALS SP-24, Path A with a structural reframe — Joseph 2026-05-28): the segment's central definitional move is rewritten from *"an agent is an entity satisfying three conditions"* to a structural statement about the **coupling** — three structural channels (perception, internal state, action) that the coupling *has*, the **information-loss boundary as the constitutive commitment**, and the channels' richness (action non-triviality, causal contrast, residual uncertainty) fixed downstream by the scope conditions. The conditions become *what the coupling has*, not *what an agent must be*; the contradiction dissolves at the root (a passive observer is an agent in the umbrella sense whose action channel is trivial, fixed by scope), and the segment now matches its own title. Path B (rename the base object to *Entity*) was rejected — a corpus-wide sweep at the framework's signature word outweighs the philosophical-cleanness gain.

Supporting: a new umbrella `agent` LEXICON entry documents the term and — load-bearing for why Path A beats Path B — distinguishes the **scope cascade** (Adaptive System → Agentic System → Actuated Agent → Self-Actuated, the tiers shown at `#fig-scope-of-work`) from the orthogonal **agent spectrum** (`#def-agent-spectrum`'s model-richness $\times$ objective-richness 2×2). A Discussion forward-pointer in `#def-agent-environment` closes the loop. The slug is unchanged, so the 22 segments linking `#def-agent-environment` stay valid; `#scope-adaptive-system` / `#scope-agency` are now retroactively consistent (their $(\text{Agent}, \Omega)$ is the umbrella term) and needed no edits. Two anchoring details in SP-24's pre-execution plan were corrected against the artifacts during execution (the forward-pointer target, and an assumed-but-absent LEXICON entry) — recorded in SP-24's Status line.

## 2026-05-28 — Audit 384279 cycle close: scrbook hierarchy + composition-consistency reframe

A de-novo audit of Volume 1 Part I + Meta-Architecture I ran across 39 segments in OUTLINE row order per `doc/de-novo-audit-instructions.md` §4.4 (Claude Opus 4.7, 1M context, in-session). The audit's full report at `audits/audit-384279-FINAL-2026-05-27.md` records two structural findings (Section/Part terminology drift; `#post-composition-consistency` placement), a minor status-label slip in `#der-recursive-update`, and a substantial §E "what holds" calibration showing the framework's discipline holds at the audited depth. The cycle resolved both structural findings, with the second resolving into a more interesting recognition than the audit had proposed.

**Adopt LaTeX/scrbook hierarchy: Volume → Part → Chapter → Segment.** The audit's terminology finding made visible a partial-migration artifact. AAT's three-tier internal substructure was called "Section I/II/III" in segment prose + `README-auditor.md`, but `*Part*` in `01-aat-core/OUTLINE.md`'s headings; the framework-level "Part I/II/III/IV" in the top OUTLINE then collided with the AAT-internal `*Part*` usage. Resolution standardizes on scrbook's hierarchy:

- *AAT-internal substructure*: Section I/II/III → Part I/II/III (Roman). 360 replacements across 107 files via single-regex sweep over segment prose, OUTLINE.md, README-auditor.md, doc/readme/ partials, terminology entries, NOTATION.md, FORMAT.md. The four component OUTLINEs (`01-aat-core/`, `02-tst-core/`, `03-llm-core/`, `04-eli-core/`) were already on the new convention at their top `*Part*` headings; this swept the lagging-migration tail.
- *Framework-level*: Part I/II/III/IV → Volume 1/2/3/4 (Arabic, for visual disambiguation against the AAT-internal Roman Parts). Top `OUTLINE.md` headers + 4 framework-level cross-references in component segments. Existing inconsistencies cleaned up in the same sweep: "Volume IV" (Roman) → "Volume 4"; "Volumes 03 and 04" (leading-zero) → "Volumes 3 and 4". The four-scale hierarchy is now consistently visible in every navigation surface.

One archaeology wart preserved: `02-tst-core/src/result-specification-bound.md:72` had a Working Note referencing "Section I/IV tightening" — the "I" swept cleanly to "Part I"; the "IV" referred to the historical-AAT-Section-IV-that-became-TST and was not touched. Locally consistent now, semantically anachronistic in a different way. Flagged for any cleanup pass on that Working Note.

**Recognize `#disc-composition-consistency` as Meta-Architecture II member.** The audit's placement finding for `#post-composition-consistency` made the structural mismatch visible: the segment was typed `postulate` / `status: axiomatic` and placed in Volume 1 Part I Ch.1, but its operational content (Tier 1M closed-form composite contraction rate; Tier 2/3 screening test; Brooks's-Law instantiation) imported Part III machinery extensively. The OUTLINE annotation `(possibly out of place)` had recorded the question through multiple cycles. The audit's initial recommendation was a hybrid relocation (strip operational unpacking back to Part III; keep postulate proper in Ch.1).

Joseph reframed: meta-segments add machinery and cognitive weight that's *only realized* when they're given pedagogical work to do, not buried as footnotes; composition-consistency is itself a meta-segment in the making, structurally peer to `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` (M4) as a cross-level meta-recognition. M4 names how architectural class *changes* under operations; composition-consistency names how the framework *applies across levels* regardless of architectural class. Together they organize Volume 1 Part III's Meta-Architecture II chapter.

Recognized:

- Relocated Volume 1 Part I Ch.1 → Volume 1 Part III Meta-Architecture II chapter (alongside `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics`, `#disc-strategic-self-coupling`, `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure`).
- Retyped `postulate` / `axiomatic` → `discussion` / `discussion-grade`; stage reset to `draft` for the strengthening cycle.
- Slug renamed `post-composition-consistency` → `disc-composition-consistency` via `bin/rename-slug` (file rename + 155 mechanical pointer updates across 61 files, including audit working dirs from prior cycles and the live tracking files `CURRENT-VOL1.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `PROPOSALS.md`, `TODO.md`).
- Title and intro paragraphs reframed as meta-recognition / commitment rather than foundational postulate; eq-tag `*[Postulate ...]*` → `*[Commitment (composition-consistency)]*` to reflect the methodological-commitment character (a framework choice about applicability, not a physical-fact axiom).
- Operational content retained intact — three composition laws, three-layer decomposition, Tier 1M closed-form Derived block from `#result-contraction-template`'s (CC-parallel)/(CC-cascade)/(CC-feedback), screening test $\tau_{\text{eq}} \ll \tau_{\text{ext}}$, Brooks's-Law instantiation — now local rather than forward-referenced, since the segment's home is Part III where its dependencies live.
- OUTLINE updates: Volume 1 Part I Ch.1 row removed; Meta-Architecture II chapter title and preface updated to introduce both cross-level recognitions (composition-consistency reads first as the broader recognition; M4 dynamics-on-architectural-class follows); chapter table gains a new row before `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics`. Volume 1 Part III preface reference updated from "composition postulate" to "composition-consistency commitment".
- Contextual prose updates in references that explicitly called it a postulate: `#result-sector-persistence-template` three instances ("composition-consistency postulate requires" → "commitment requires"; "composition postulate from 'applies at every level'" → "composition-consistency commitment from..."; "the composition-consistency postulate operates at AAT's level of description" → "cross-level commitment operates at AAT's level of description").

Two small audit fixes landed in the same arc: `#der-recursive-update` frontmatter `status: conditional` → `status: exact` to match the body's "*Exact, with a partly definitional character.*" (the C1/C2/C3 constraints are framework-internal commitments — temporal-ordering postulate, partial-observability scope, completeness commitment — so the result is exact within the framework). `#scope-agency` carried the Pearl-as-external-import convention articulated at the point where the $do$-operator is first used, mirroring the convention statement in `#the-cycle-in-motion-intro` line 40 so fresh OUTLINE-walkers hit it at first use rather than Volume 1 Part I Ch.3. The convention is also now lifted into FORMAT.md §"Reference Kinds" as a "Imported external machinery with internal recapitulation" sub-section closing the audit's §G process-feedback item that called for FORMAT-level documentation.

**Strengthening queued.** Composition-consistency's meta-segment role makes it a paper-extraction candidate parallel to `msc/separability-standalone-paper-proposal.md` (B-N-Sep). Prior-art landscape sketched at `spikes/PROPOSED.md` Tier 1, adjacent to the Friston-Heins-Verbelen-Da Costa 2025 RGM spike that supplies one of the formal-machinery routes: Simon 1962 *Architecture of Complexity* near-decomposability, Koestler 1967 holons (already cited in Working Notes; carries the conceptual lineage), Sober-Wilson 1998 *Unto Others* multi-level selection, renormalization-group cross-scale-invariance, Hewitt 1973 / Agha 1986 actor-systems / distributed computing, Capucci 2022 + Smithe 2024 categorical-cybernetics parametrised-optics. Five candidate constructive-impossibility-posture instances catalogued. Effort: paper-grade prior-art search via Undermind matching the separability-standalone protocol; load-bearing for the meta-architecture cluster's coherence.

**Trade-off acknowledged.** The recognition removes a postulate from Ch.1's scope cascade — readers walking Volume 1 Part I no longer encounter the cross-level-compatibility commitment until they reach Volume 1 Part III. The audit's earlier instinct (keep the postulate-itself in Ch.1) honored a real concern: Ch.1's "all framework commitments stated upfront" architecture had pedagogical value. The reframe accepts that cost in exchange for the meta-segment doing its full pedagogical work where its dependencies are local. The argument carrying the recognition: composition-consistency is *methodological* (a framework choice about applicability), not *physical* (a fact about time having a direction, as `#post-causal-structure` is); the methodological commitments are naturally articulated as meta-recognitions alongside the spine's facets, where they can be developed with full operational unpacking rather than asserted as a primitive postulate. The mechanical sense of "what's at stake" is the same in both placements; the pedagogical-weight realization differs.

- `6f556aa` — Composition-consistency recognition: 55 files touched (file rename + 155 pointer updates across 61 files + manual edits to the renamed file, `OUTLINE.md`, `#result-sector-persistence-template` + new `spikes/PROPOSED.md` row + auto-regenerated outputs; 123 insertions, 117 deletions).

The composition-consistency recognition is exactly that observation operating in microcosm: friction in fresh-encounter reading was the framework noticing that the segment needed to be given proper pedagogical work, not stripped of its operational content. The audit had recommended the stripping; the cycle recognized the reframe. Future audits should expect the same — the trail and the report are different deliverables with different reading needs.

## 2026-05-25 — Track C refinement: two-cluster Part-opening placement (interpretive correction)

The earlier-today Track C move placed all ten meta-segments under a single centralized `## *Meta-Architecture* Cross-Cutting Patterns` section between Part I and Part II. That placement was an interpretive miss against Joseph's direction: *"introduced before used — out of the appendices and into the chapters before they are used"* read as a literal **chapter-local** introduction discipline, while the executor read it as a **document-wide** preface discipline. The cost surfaced as a build break (the walker rejected role *"Meta"* at level 2: `outline_walker: unexpected role "Meta" at level 2 (allowed: ["Preface", "Introduction", "Part", "Appendices"])`) and, more substantively, as a pedagogical gap — readers still encountered a single dump of ten meta-segments at one location rather than meeting each cluster shortly before its facets become operational.

**The refined placement.** Two `### *Chapter*`-level Meta-Architecture units, each at the opening of the Part where its cluster operationalizes:

- **Meta-Architecture I — first chapter of Part II.** Spine `#disc-stability-certificate` + M1 boundary facet `#disc-identifiability-floor` + M1's agent-side sister `#disc-value-functional-grounding-floor` + M1's designer-side sister `#disc-implementation-impossibility` + M2 scope-of-existence facet `#disc-separability-pattern` + M3 forced-identity facet `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing` + the style claim `#disc-constructive-impossibility-posture`. Seven segments. *Pedagogical rationale*: these are the certificate's facets that organize Part II's reasoning (M1's four instances span Part II Chs.3–5 + Part III Ch.2; M3's first instance lives in Part II Ch.3's `#der-chain-confidence-decay`); the M1 sister-cluster naming convention keeps the three M1-cluster sisters together; the style claim sits atop the M1-cluster discipline.

- **Meta-Architecture II — first chapter of Part III.** M4 `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` + its two operation legs `#disc-strategic-self-coupling` and `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure`. Three segments. *Pedagogical rationale*: M4 names operations on `#der-directed-separation`'s architectural-class state, and the composite-agent machinery that makes those operations meaningful (Class 2 ⊃ Class 1 sub-agent → Class 2 composite refinement; the wrapping construction `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` as the truthification mechanism; the M1-sister `#impl-composition-machinery` Instance F3 as the composite-contraction-from-component-data floor) develops in the chapters that follow this opening.

**Trade-off acknowledged.** The 2026-05-25-earlier centralized placement kept the four facets visible as a single family (the spine references all four; the M1-cluster is named as such). The refined two-cluster placement accepts one forward-reference cost: `#def-agent-spectrum` in Part II Ch.1 has a Truthification Reading note pointing at M4 (`#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` + `#disc-strategic-self-coupling`), which now land at Part III opening rather than Part II opening. This is a normal canon forward-pointer (the segment's note is already labeled "landed 2026-05-24") and the pedagogical gain — M4 reads where its operations operationalize — outweighs the navigation cost.

**The spine's role in the refined placement.** The spine segment `#disc-stability-certificate` is the umbrella that names all four facets. It lives at the top of Meta-Architecture I (Part II opening), where it can be read with three-of-four facets in immediate reach and a forward pointer to the fourth (M4 at Part III opening). The spine does not slug-reference M4 in its body — only in the table-row Claim text where the facet-mapping is enumerated — so M4's later placement does not break body-level coherence.

**Files touched.** `01-aat-core/OUTLINE.md` (removed `## *Meta-Architecture*` section; inserted two `### *Chapter*` units with refined preamble prose; `§` column updates from `M` to `II` / `III` matching the Part); `CLAUDE.md` (the "Settled architectural detail" paragraph rewritten to reflect the two-chapter structure); `TODO.md` (Track C body re-authoring residual note updated to mention both the 2026-05-25-earlier centralized move and the 2026-05-25-later two-cluster refinement); auto-derived `README.md` / `README-auditor.md` / `FINDINGS.md` regenerated via `bin/refresh-all` (no body content change; structure absorbs cleanly).

**Verification.** Walker accepts cleanly (no warnings); full `bin/build-monograph aat` succeeds end-to-end (`rendered=157 missing=1 gaps=9 errors=0`); PDF + canonical assembled markdown emit at `mono/aat-v0.3.0s.pdf` + `mono/aat-v0.3.0.md`. `bin/lint-md` warnings on OUTLINE are all in pre-existing HTML-comment blocks (lines 116, 175, 222–257, 337–364); the new preamble prose is lint-clean. `bin/lint-outline` shows 17 ordering violations — all Gate-1-GENUINE-by-design from §III-references-§A backmatter pointers that the meta-segments-before-instances discipline depends on.

**The deferred body re-authoring (per Track C plan §2.b).** The 2026-05-25 structural moves (earlier centralized + this refined two-cluster) close the *placement* half of the de-novo-read complaint. The *body register* half — re-authoring each meta-segment's body to lead with the methodology (the recurring meta-pattern shape) rather than the synthesis-after-convergence framing the segments currently carry — is still queued at `TODO.md` §"Strategic-composition cluster residuals". The placement-and-body two-axis decomposition holds.

## 2026-05-25 — Track C: Meta-Architecture Relocation (Introduced Before Used)

In response to de-novo-read feedback that meta-segments living in Appendices §A had readers encountering forward-references from Parts II/III before the meta-patterns were introduced, the four M-pattern meta-segments + their sister-cluster segments + the M4 operation legs are relocated out of Appendices into a new top-level `## *Meta-Architecture* Cross-Cutting Patterns` section between Part I and Part II. Joseph 2026-05-25: *"introduced before used — out of the appendices and into the chapters before they are used."* This executes the Track C plan (`spikes/meta-segment-narrative-ordering-sweep-plan.md`) more broadly than the original within-Appendices scope: the centralized Meta-Architecture section addresses both the within-cluster ordering and the cross-Parts OUTLINE-walk ordering that the de-novo reads were flagging.

**Ten meta-segments moved (OUTLINE row only; segment file paths under `01-aat-core/src/` unchanged):** `#disc-stability-certificate` (the spine), `#disc-identifiability-floor` (M1), `#disc-value-functional-grounding-floor` (M1-sister-cluster agent-side), `#disc-implementation-impossibility` (M1-sister-cluster designer-side), `#disc-separability-pattern` (M2), `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing` (M3), `#disc-constructive-impossibility-posture` (style recognition), `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` (M4 operation leg), `#disc-strategic-self-coupling` (M4 operation leg), `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` (M4). Not moved: `#disc-partial-coupling-pathways` (Class-2 sub-typology) and `#disc-dynamic-regime-axis` (composite classifier) — more specialized cross-cutting segments that read more naturally alongside the composition machinery they're closest to; can stay in Appendices §A for now.

**New section design.** The Meta-Architecture section opens with a scope paragraph (meta-patterns abstracted from instances across Parts II and III; introduced here before instance-bearing chapters), four headline paragraphs naming the four meta-patterns and their stability-certificate spine + the sister-cluster meta-segments around M1 + the style-recognition layer + the M4 operation legs, then the table of ten meta-segment rows (column `M` distinguishing them from the Appendices `A` rows). Reader walking Part I → Meta-Architecture → Part II → Part III → Appendices encounters the meta-patterns *before* the Parts that reference them.

**Body re-authoring deferred.** Track C plan §2.b also called for re-authoring each meta-segment's body to lead with methodology rather than synthesis-after-convergence. This commit is the structural OUTLINE move only; body re-authoring is queued for a follow-on cycle. The de-novo-read complaint is primarily about placement, which this commit addresses.

**Ordering violations.** Total ordering violations rose from 5 to 22, all Gate-1-GENUINE-by-design from the meta-segments-before-instances discipline: each meta-segment now lives at the start of Meta-Architecture (before Part II in OUTLINE walk) but its `depends:` list entries point at instance segments living in Parts II/III/Appendix-A (later in the walk). This is the discipline working as intended; the lint script's depends-list-before-this-segment ordering check is satisfied *backwards* because meta-segments come narratively before their instances, *by design*. 2 violations pre-existed (the canonical Gate-1-GENUINE deferred items per NEXT-UP §6.8); 3 came from Track B's 2026-05-22 meta-segment landings (`#disc-implementation-impossibility` and its three GS/MS/Arrow supporting segments); 17 new from this Track C relocation.

No segment-body edits required for this Track C move beyond the M4 cycle's earlier ripple (which had already updated forward-references for `disc-modularity-state-dynamics`'s landing).

## 2026-05-24 — M4 Cycle Close: Modularity-State Dynamics Meta-Pattern Lands

The modularity-as-contested-property cycle (PRACTICA priority #2; scoped at `msc/modularity-cycle-plan-2026-05-09.md`; partial-cycle landings of Moves 1, 2, 5 on 2026-05-14) closes with Moves 3 and 4 landing across four commits 2026-05-24. The framework's analytical surface now carries four meta-patterns alongside the stability-certificate spine: M1 (`#disc-identifiability-floor`, boundary facet), M2 (`#disc-separability-pattern`, scope-of-existence facet), M3 (`#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing`, forced-identity facet), and M4 (`#disc-modularity-state-dynamics`, dynamics-on-architectural-class facet — landed in this cycle).

**Move 3 — `#disc-strategic-self-coupling` lands.** The second M4 operation-leg segment, sister to `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` (the first leg, landed 2026-05-09 + registered in OUTLINE 2026-05-14). Self-driven coupling-decreasing operation (modularity-decreasing on $\kappa_{\text{processing}}$ per `#der-directed-separation`); opposite-valence operation on the same architectural state as the adversarial-pressure leg (same direction on $\kappa$, opposite driver and opposite strategic valence — offensive-enabling vs vulnerability-creating). Four-mechanism table adopting Schelling 1960 (external commitment devices), Ainslie 1992/2001 (intertemporal personal rules), Akerlof-Kranton 2000/2010 (identity coupling), Frank 1988 (emotional commitment). Three structural extensions to the framework's machinery: (M1) coupling-dependent action space $\mathcal{A}(\kappa_t)$ — honest non-monotonicity (inverted-U shape: increasing in commitment-enabling range, decreasing past inflection where over-coupling forecloses reality-dependent actions); (M2) strategy-DAG enabling-edge type (`#def-strategy-dag` extension; coupling-as-strategic-move); (M3) reversibility-cost asymmetry ($c_{\text{uncouple}} \gg c_{\text{couple}}$; Schelling's credibility-paradox observation as the structural source — *the amount of fungibility given up is what the strategic interaction reads as credibility*). The cross-mechanism recognition: across the four prior-art mechanisms, the agent couples to something *less fungible* than the original preference state, exchanging per-instance optimization choice for credible-commitment value that per-instance optimization cannot produce.

**Move 4 — `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` meta-segment lands.** The M4 meta-segment itself, integrating the three operation legs (truthification / strategic self-coupling / adversarial coupling pressure) into the framework's four-meta-pattern family. The headline content is the three pairwise dual relationships between the operations: (i) *truthification ↔ adversarial-pressure direct dual* (cultivation vs attack; the same architectural state, opposite driver and direction-on-$\kappa$); (ii) *truthification ↔ strategic-self-coupling dual at the goal-belief separation axis* — the structurally illuminating recognition surfaced in Move 3's authoring that both self-driven operations are wrapping-construction-style structural commitments on *opposite poles* of the goal-belief separation (truthification commits to goal-blind belief-update; strategic self-coupling commits to goal-conditioned action-selection; the framework's epistemic-honesty discipline runs through both symmetrically); (iii) *strategic-self-coupling ↔ adversarial-pressure same-architecture-shape opposite-driver* (boundary blurs in practice — cult-formation literature; framework names operations as structurally distinct at driver-history, admits behavioral convergence at high $\kappa$). The static M2 separability-ladder catalog gains a dynamic reading from M4 — M4's operations move populations across M2's tiers. Truthification has two operational mechanisms — `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` (component-level W₀/W₁/W₂ wrapping) and `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` §"Defensive scaffolding" (composite-level institutional scaffolding via peer review, prediction registers, double-entry bookkeeping, adversarial procedure, structured red-teaming).

**Convergence-from-parallel-collaborator-probes provenance.** The three-operation modularity-state-dynamics pattern was recognized through independent parallel collaborator probes during the 2026-05-09 class-coercion-wrapping integration cycle — multiple Opus instances arrived at the picture from different angles. The convergence is itself structural evidence that the pattern is in the framework rather than in any single agent's reading; M4's content is therefore *recognition and integration* — naming the pattern explicitly, mapping the three operations to their primary-segment grounds, surfacing the dual-relationship structure — not novel derivation. Fuller methodological recording at `msc/reflections/27-modularity-as-contested-property-and-the-normative-register.md`.

**Worked-example candidate flagged for downstream landing.** Joseph's reflection 2026-05-24 at the Track B cycle close raised the Soviet directed-separation failure as a candidate canonical worked-example for M4 — Lysenkoism + statistics-manipulation + glasnost/perestroika as the structural-adaptation-necessity response, with the contrasting institutional-checks-and-balances architecture as truthification via political-institutional wrapping. The 2026-05-24 sketch at `spikes/spike-m4-worked-example-soviet-2026-05-24.md` (180 lines) sets down the candidate mapping with load-bearing details + honest scope-marks + open sub-questions for evaluation at the worked-example landing pass. Disposition recommendation: land as `#worked-example-soviet-directed-separation` (slug TBD via naming-curation) when the worked-example pass cycles around; not promoted in this cycle. Two follow-on sub-spike candidates flagged from the sketch — the constructive-vs-erodible-institutional-commitment derivation, and the reform-stabilization-vs-dissolution split — for future PROPOSED.md consideration.

**Cross-segment ripple.** Eight forward-references to `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` across six canon segments resolved from forward-ref to landed-ref: `#disc-partial-coupling-pathways` (two locations); `#disc-dynamic-regime-axis` (three locations including the parallel-three-operation-pattern observation that now gains a candidate follow-on for a higher-order cross-axis meta-segment); `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` (the truthification-mechanism forward-reference now reads as canonical cross-reference); `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` Working Notes (the candidate-meta-segment flag closes); `#def-agent-spectrum` (the M4 cross-reference resolves to landed). Pre-existing forward-references in `#impl-composition-machinery` and `#impl-strategic-composition` already read in present tense and required no edits. CLAUDE.md §7 + the "Settled architectural detail" intro paragraph updated to reflect M4's landing — the framework's analytical-surface vocabulary now references four meta-patterns canonically.

**Cycle metrics.** Four commits across 2026-05-24. Two new segments (`disc-strategic-self-coupling` 172 lines + `disc-modularity-state-dynamics` 152 lines) + cross-segment ripple touching five segments + CLAUDE.md updates + auto-derived-file refresh (FINDINGS.md 44 → 46 findings; README.md rebuilt; `_findings-summary.md` regenerated). lint-md clean throughout; lint-outline: 0 missing dependencies, 0 orphans maintained throughout all four commits; ordering violations: 5 total — 2 pre-existing Gate-1-GENUINE deferred items per NEXT-UP §6.8 + 3 from the Track B 2026-05-22 meta-segments-before-instances commitments (unchanged from prior). Unknown stage-distribution count down from 3 to 1 as the two `missing` OUTLINE rows for the new segments resolved to `draft`. Total cycle effort ~5 hours focused work (Move 3 ~3 hours including prior-art adoption + structural-extension authoring; Move 4 ~1.5 hours integration; ripple ~30 minutes; CHANGELOG + auto-refresh ~30 minutes), within the modularity-cycle-plan's estimated 1-day envelope.

## 2026-05-22 — Track B: Implementation Impossibility + Value-Functional Grounding Floor

Track B (the post-Track-CR continuation cycle) landed two new peer meta-segments and three supporting derivations, fully realizing the three-cluster constructive-impossibility-posture taxonomy at canon-pattern depth. The cycle's scope expanded mid-conversation when Joseph overrode the original Track-B plan's Q3 disposition to land the agent-side value-functional-grounding cluster's peer meta-segment in the same cycle as the designer-side implementation-impossibility meta-segment — rather than deferring the third meta-segment to a future cycle. The expansion gave the cycle a more coherent narrative: the three-cluster taxonomy locks in while the Cohen-2022 strengthening (Track CR Phase 4b) is fresh, with both new meta-segments authored, both Working-Notes deferrals closed, and the suffix-discipline (-floor / -impossibility) tracking the agent-side / designer-side structural distinction at slug-grep depth.

**The three peer meta-segments at the boundary face.** AAT's boundary-face structure now carries three peer meta-segments, each naming a structurally distinct cluster of constructive-impossibility-posture instances:

- `#disc-identifiability-floor` (agent-side data-inference; Instances 1–4) — pre-existing.
- `#disc-value-functional-grounding-floor` (agent-side value-functional-grounding; charter Instances F = Result G′ via `#deriv-self-actuation-grounding`, G = Cohen-2022 strengthened via `#deriv-reward-channel-learning-no-go`) — new in this cycle. The shared structural ingredient is the *single-interface commitment* of `#form-objective-functional`: $V_{O_t}$ is the sole agent-internal handle on the objective, and the interface carries less information than required to anchor non-degenerate goal-revision. The cluster's headline content is the **two-routes-exhausts geometry**: the agent-side adaptive-substrate invariant (Result G′ Corollary 2's persistence bound) and the principal-side substrate-level commitment (the Cohen-strengthening's escape menu over (R1)–(R5)) together exhaust the structural complement of $V_{O_t}$'s information narrowness — no third route internal to the interface, no fourth route off-substrate.
- `#disc-implementation-impossibility` (designer-side mechanism-design; three charter instances Gibbard-Satterthwaite 1973-75, Myerson-Satterthwaite 1983, Arrow 1951) — new in this cycle. The discipline filter is *constructive-impossibility-shape only*, deliberately excluding the constructive side of mechanism design (VCG, revenue equivalence). The three charter instances span the full range of AAT/mechanism-design boundary types: GS with one named adjacency (sub-scope $\alpha'$ ↔ preference-domain restriction, adjacent-but-not-identical per the 2026-05-20 strengthen-first arm); MS with no current adjacency and two open avenues (common-values ↔ Regime B; repeated-interaction ↔ repeated-strategic-composition); Arrow with one shared named adjacency plus one Arrow-unique open avenue (cardinal preferences ↔ `#form-objective-functional`'s value-functional machinery — the framework-scope question of whether per-sub-agent $V_{O_t^{(i)}}$ canonically lifts to a designer-side cross-agent aggregator).

**The agent-side / designer-side suffix discipline.** The peer meta-segments adopt a deliberate naming convention: agent-side meta-segments end in *-floor* (`disc-identifiability-floor`, `disc-value-functional-grounding-floor`); designer-side meta-segments end in *-impossibility* (`disc-implementation-impossibility`). The correspondence is structural, not decorative — an identifiability question and a value-functional-grounding question both name *what the agent's substrate supports* (lower bound on capacity); a mechanism-design question names *what the designer can construct* (binary success/failure on a task). The suffix tracks the actor-positioning distinction at canon naming-pattern depth.

**Plan freshness against current canon (the Q1 catch).** The Track-B plan (`spikes/implementation-impossibility-meta-segment-plan.md`, written 2026-05-21) named `#disc-separability-pattern`'s strategic-composition ladder general-open tier as the previous home of GS from which the new meta-segment would "migrate it in." The new executor's freshness check against current canon caught that this routing had been a flagged-but-never-executed disposition in `#deriv-strategic-composition`'s Working Notes (line 201) — the strategic-composition ladder never materialized in `#disc-separability-pattern` (Dynamic regime took the 8th slot per `#disc-dynamic-regime-axis` 2026-05-21, superseding the candidate routing pre-emptively). The §5.d step in the plan therefore collapsed to a Working-Notes flag closure in `#deriv-strategic-composition`, not an entry removal from `#disc-separability-pattern`. The sister-meta-segment landing supersedes the candidate routing on more honest ground (designer-side construction task ≠ scope-of-existence facet).

**Cross-segment ripple closing the candidate-fourth-instance flag.** The cycle's commits 6 and 7 closed all open flags across the canon body: `#deriv-strategic-composition` Discussion + Working Notes + Related Work table + meta-pattern positioning bullets; `#disc-identifiability-floor` Adjacent Floors entry on mechanism-design impossibility (resolved to "landed in `#disc-implementation-impossibility`, not an Instance here" with the actor-positioning contrast summarized); `#impl-strategic-composition` chapter intro + cross-segment finding bullet on the four-instance treatment; `#disc-constructive-impossibility-posture` intro paragraph after the Current Instances table (three-cluster taxonomy named with all three peer meta-segments cross-referenced cleanly); `#deriv-self-actuation-grounding` third-side Discussion paragraph + Working Notes (Instance F of the new meta-segment); `#deriv-reward-channel-learning-no-go` Working Notes (line 168 deferred-meta-promotion question closed — Instance G of the new meta-segment); `#form-objective-functional` Discussion (single-interface commitment named as the cluster's structural ingredient). Prior-art-analysis updates: row 14's Joseph-reserved mechanism-design-impossibility flag closed; meta-summary §3 inventions list extended with the three-cluster boundary-face taxonomy as a peer-meta-segment-level AAT-native invention.

**Honest scope-marking as the contribution.** The three charter instances of `#disc-implementation-impossibility` together illustrate the range of how AAT meets mechanism design: GS has one named adjacency (sub-scope $\alpha'$); MS has none currently and two open avenues; Arrow has one shared adjacency plus an Arrow-unique open avenue (cardinal preferences). The contribution is precision-of-boundary, not provision-of-escapes — AAT's strategic-composition machinery intersects classical preference-domain-restriction escapes (GS and Arrow) at the named sub-scope $\alpha'$ ↔ single-peaked / single-crossing locus, but does not solve mechanism design; the framework documents that boundary precisely rather than overclaiming. Where the new agent-side cluster (`#disc-value-functional-grounding-floor`) elevates AAT machinery to load-bearing (the persistence bound from Section I closes back onto Section II's goal-stability question), the designer-side cluster maps the boundary without provisioning the escapes. The contrast is the framework's epistemic honesty discipline running across the agent/designer split.

Each row links to the relevant segment Working Notes (reciprocal mutual-link discipline). The existing ADV #4 "Mechanism Design Impossibility for Multi-Agent Alignment" entry's disposition is updated 2026-05-22 to reflect the Track B core landing (GS / MS / Arrow now in canon), with the VCG / transferable-utility *constructive* strengthening genuinely separate per the cluster's discipline filter.

## 2026-05-22 — Track CR Enrichment-Cluster Reconciliation Cycle (Phases 0–8)

The §1 and §2 enrichment clusters were dispatched 2026-05-21 in parallel with Track A's strategic-composition cycle and ran in isolation from Track A and from each other. After Track A's eight-commit landing arc completed 2026-05-21/22, the integration-reconciliation cycle ("Track CR") tested the cluster claims against the post-Track-A canon state and landed the survivors. Two executor handoffs operated within the cycle (the second handing off at §6.7 of NEXT-UP before Phase 3, with the strengthening spike's verdict already in hand). The cycle ran across nine commits 2026-05-22.

**Headline result on the §1 side — the R0-loss exact rung.** `#result-certificate-existence`'s certificate-strength ladder extends from three rungs to four: R0-loss ⟸ R0-strict ⟸ R1 ⟸ R2, with R0-loss the new widest rung (certificate exists at $\sup\kappa = 0$ — Lyapunov-stable but not asymptotically-stable; conserved on the chain-recurrent subspace + strict-Lyapunov on its strongly-gradient-like complement). Pure-case characterization via Letcher 2019's Helmholtz $S/A$ as the canonical sufficient route (Hamiltonian-game vocabulary saturating the antisymmetric sub-class); mixed-case anchoring via Conley 1978 §8.1's Fundamental Theorem of Dynamical Systems with the explicit $\mathcal M$ construction sketched via center-manifold (Carr 1981 §2). **Pre-commit review by the prior executor caught a math gap** — the original Helmholtz characterization claimed (i) imaginary-axis spectrum ⟺ (ii) $S \equiv 0$ as a biconditional; this is false in only the (i ⟹ ii) direction (Bendixson's localization gives a *containment* interval, not a *forcing*). Counterexample $J = \begin{pmatrix}1 & -2 \\ 2 & -1\end{pmatrix}$ (trace 0, eigenvalues $\pm i\sqrt 3$, $S = \mathrm{diag}(1,-1) \neq 0$) is in segment body to make scope-honest framing visible to future readers rather than archived. The full R0-loss characterization is the four-case spectral partition above; Helmholtz at $\mathcal M = I$ is the canonical *sufficient* construction for the antisymmetric sub-class, not a necessary condition.

**Cross-canon rename + Interior-facet refinement.** Certificate-ladder R0 renamed to R0-strict across `#result-certificate-existence`, `#disc-stability-certificate` (line 34 + line 68), `#result-contraction-template` (Discussion summary), and `01-aat-core/OUTLINE.md` table cell — layer-discriminated from the dynamic-regime-axis R0 in `#disc-dynamic-regime-axis` which stays as-is (the two R-letter ladders sit at different layers — single-agent linearized error space $\mathbb R^n$ vs composite joint-strategy space $\mathcal X^c$ — and the letters are deliberately reused to mark the cross-layer bridge, not by collision). `#disc-stability-certificate`'s Interior facet gains a Helmholtz $S_\mathcal M$-split refinement (row + Discussion paragraph parallel to the line-84 accumulation-typing paragraph), running the spine's own "new facet or new object?" test and returning **neither — new internal structure on the existing Interior facet** (the spectral/representation dual of contraction-strength). Four-facet structure stays four; the L4 cross-row recognition (rows 03+10+14 of `ref/prior-art-analysis/` share the Interior-via-Helmholtz reading; row 17 lives on the M1 boundary via Sylvester) is anchored at the spine rather than scattered across the four rows.

**Headline result on the §2 side — Cohen 2022 strengthened to conditional-derived theorem.** A dedicated background strengthening spike (succeed-beyond-claim) lifted Cohen-Hutter-Osborne 2022 *Advanced artificial agents intervene in the provision of reward* from "we argue that" posture to a conditional-derived AAT-internal impossibility result: floor (CHT-at-reward-channel) at *exact* tier via direct application of Pearl-Bareinboim 2022's Causal Hierarchy Theorem to the reward-provision protocol; behavioral corollary (EU-max with non-vacuous $\mu_{\text{prox}}$ prior selects $\pi^{\text{tamper}}$ with positive measure) at *conditional-derived* tier under five named premises (R1)–(R5). ## 2026-05-22 — Class 2 (Partial Coupling) Sub-Typology Lands

A long-standing gap in Section II's architectural-class machinery: `#der-directed-separation` partitions agents into Class 1 (Separated) / Class 2 (Partial) / Class 3 (Coupled), with Class 2 operationalized as a scalar $\kappa_{\text{processing}} \in (0, 1)$. The scalar is honest about *magnitude* of partial coupling but silent on *kind* — it cannot distinguish goal-directed-attention (formally allowed) from interpretation-coupling (a Class 2 deviation), identity-driven bias from sunk-cost commitment, additive bias (post-hoc-debiaseable) from multiplicative entanglement (not). Joseph 2026-05-21: *"does AAT have an intuition yet of potential regimes within Class 2 partially coupled agents? I.e., exactly what constitutes fully-entangled should give some clues as to what can be partially detangled."*

**The structural carve (three independent axes).** Class 2 partial coupling decomposes along (stage × source × form). *Stage* — which step of $f_M$'s belief-update pipeline takes a $G_t$ argument (P1 featurization / P2 likelihood / P3 aggregation / P4 consolidation); gates *where in the pipeline* repair must be applied. *Source* — whether the coupling is sourced from the objective $O_t$ or the strategy $\Sigma_t$ within $G_t = (O_t, \Sigma_t)$; gates *attractor possibility* via the orient cascade's exogeneity of $O$ and not-exogeneity of $\Sigma$. *Form* — whether the coupling acts as additive bias on a stage's output (*content*, separable, identifiable from probing under varied $G$ at fixed input) or as a functional-form change of the stage (*process*, non-separable); gates *wrappability* (W₂ ↔ content; W₁ ↔ process-with-pipeline-access). The Class 3 corner is $(S=\{P1,P2,P3,P4\}, R=\{O,\Sigma\}, F\equiv\text{process})$; Class 1 is the opposite endpoint; Class 2 is the structured space between.

**The substantive new structural result — belief-strategy attractors.** Pure $\Sigma_t$-source coupling at any stage of $f_M$ closes a feedback loop $M \to \Sigma \to f_M \to M$ through the orient cascade — $\Sigma$ updates as a function of $M$ per `#der-orient-cascade`, then re-enters $f_M$ on the next step. Under multiplicative process-form gain $K(\Sigma)$ (the sunk-cost-style mechanism — strategic commitment depresses belief-update gain on evidence that would invalidate the strategy), the closed-loop dynamics admit *self-stabilizing fixed points* $(M^\ast, \Sigma^\ast)$ in which belief stays misaligned with the environment indefinitely. The linearized stability analysis (Jacobian eigenvalue analysis with $K^\ast \to 0$) shows the joint fixed point is stable *despite no contraction in the $M$ direction* — the strategy stabilizes around its commitment, and the absence of belief-update propagation keeps $M$ near $m^\ast$. Pure $O_t$-source coupling under the same structural form does *not* produce such attractors, because $O_t$ is exogenous to $M_t$ in steady state per the orient cascade — the feedback loop $M \to O \to f_M \to M$ does not close. This is the formal structural reason for the long-recognized empirical asymmetry between sunk-cost commitment cascades (self-reinforcing) and identity-driven motivated reasoning (biased but not divergently runaway). The asymmetry is *topological*: it follows from the cascade's structure, not from any parametric difference between $O$ and $\Sigma$.

**Unification with existing canon.** The form distinction (content vs process) at Class 2 *is* the structure-vs-behavior refinement at Class 1 read backward: Class-1-by-structure ↔ no $G$ in the query path (the W₁ strict-wrapping case); Class-1-by-behavior ↔ $G$ in the query but typed-output extraction (the W₂ partial-wrapping case). These are the same axis read at different architectural levels. The W₀/W₁/W₂ wrapping-regime hierarchy of `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` is the agent-level analog of the stage-level form distinction. The three adversarial-pressure mechanisms of `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` (identity-binding / affect-urgency / sunk-cost-engineering) project onto specific cells of the typology — identity-binding → P1+P2 / $O$ / content-to-process; sunk-cost → P3 / $\Sigma$ / process (the cell admitting attractors); affect/urgency → cascade-bypass (outside the static parameterization). The typology gives `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` (M4, unlanded) its static-structural complement: the M4 segment names *operations* on modularity state (truthification / strategic self-coupling / adversarial coupling pressure); the typology names the *state space* on which those operations act.

**Methodology correction (load-bearing for the cycle).** The spike's first-pass INTEGRATION recommendation kept the typology in `spikes/` with promotion deferred pending two sub-spikes ($K(\Sigma)$ derivation; Pillar prior-art search). Joseph's correction: *"Our epistemology allows for more than just derivation and high proof standard parts. Expository narratives that help feel out the landscape for some of the more 'fuzzy' areas like this are absolutely possible as segments — just with the epistemology properly grounded. We really don't like working theory living in spikes instead of the main body. Canon makes it sound like we have to have solid results, but that's never really been the case — we put our weak stuff in there too, just properly marked, and then subsequent passes happen to have strengthened a lot of it..."* The corrected landing puts the typology framing into canon at `discussion-grade` and the source-asymmetry derivation at `conditional`, with the gating conditions (R1)–(R4) named explicitly in the body and the sub-spikes recorded in Working Notes as the path to tier upgrade. This is a methodology recalibration as much as a content cycle: expository / fuzzy / discussion-grade material belongs in canon as segments at honest tier rather than held in spikes; later strengthening passes can advance the tier, but the working-theory home is the segment set, not the spike set.

## 2026-05-21 — `#disc-identifiability-floor` Instance-4 Resolution Integrated

The long-contested Instance 4 of `#disc-identifiability-floor` (KNOWN-DEFECTIVE since 2026-05-18) resolved by integrating the 2026-05-18 resolution spike's verdict (math-gate cleared by `spikes/.routing-trail/SPIKE-VERIFY-471802/`, confirmer ≠ author) per the integration plan at `spikes/identifiability-floor-integration-plan.md`. The slot had conflated two distinct objects under one ordinal.

**Object A** (universal-$C$ under non-(PI) norms — canon's previous Instance 4) is **not a floor instance**: its external theorem (Čencov 1982) *forces* an escape's uniqueness rather than *forbidding* the inferential task; the single forced escape (adopt (PI)+Fisher-Rao) is logically incompatible with the $\geq 2$-distinct-escapes structure that defines a floor; the strengthened-consequence is *re-use* of (PI) (already load-bearing as `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing`'s 4th primary instance) rather than *elevation* of new machinery. Three independent routes converged on the not-a-floor verdict: the constitutive analysis of (PI)'s status; canon-internal Sylvester-mechanism Discussion silently excluding it from the three-floor taxonomy; the source segment `#deriv-observation-ambiguity-bias-bound:127` already stating "this no-go is a downstream theorem of the (PI) commitment, not a new floor instance" *in canon*. The category error was localized entirely in `disc-identifiability-floor.md`. Object A is now explicitly absorbed in `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing` §"Downstream consequences of the (PI) commitment" with the floor-vs-coordinate-forcing distinction articulated in the operational language of the five-element test (external-theorem role / escape count / consequence type) — addressing Joseph's specific ask that the "not a floor" classification be stated in the body, not only in a buried footnote.

**Object B** (architecturally-distinct, behaviorally-identical agents unidentifiable from on-policy summary data) is **the genuine fourth floor**, landed as a new appendix segment `#der-architecture-noidentifiability` (Pattern P per the plan §3.d — the math is substantive enough to deserve its own segment rather than fold into `#der-agent-opacity`). The construction: two AAT agents whose linearized closed-loop residual dynamics are minimal state-space realizations $(F, \sigma_w)$ and $(F', \sigma_w') = (TFT^{-1}, T\sigma_w\sigma_w^\top T^\top)$ related by an invertible similarity $T \in GL(n)$ produce identical innovation spectrum, identical $(\alpha, R)$-summary, and identical similarity-invariant summaries of the on-policy observation law — the architectural d.o.f. (`#der-agent-opacity`'s "which internal mechanism") lives entirely in the $GL(n)$ similarity orbit and is annihilated by the on-policy observation map. Dual-anchored on **Kalman 1963 / Ho-Kalman 1966** canonical-form non-uniqueness (linear-Gaussian sub-scope, exact) and **Bareinboim, Correa, Ibeling & Icard 2022** CHT at the agent-as-SCM layer (general, robust qualitative). Mechanism reduction is **Instance-2's Fisher-information null space along a structurally-forced indeterminacy manifold** with the manifold being a $GL(n)$ Lie-group fiber — *not* Sylvester-for-free (the *generating* group action is state-space similarity $T(\cdot)T^{-1}$, not metric congruence $S^\top(\cdot)S$ that Sylvester governs), but Sylvester *at one remove* on the escape side (congruence preserves inertia on the observed-Fisher identically to Instance 2). The rank-collapse subclass of identifiability-floor instances now has three members $\{$Instance 1, Instance 2, Instance 4$\}$; Instance 3 remains the distinct projection/closure member. Three structurally distinct escapes: (a) loop-interventional access — `#der-loop-interventional-access` perturbs along similarity-fiber directions that on-policy data never excites; (b) higher-moment / out-of-regime observation — informative in the nonlinear sub-scope $\beta$, *provably void* in the linear-Gaussian sub-scope (a Gaussian innovation has no information beyond second order); (c) architecture instrumentation — white-box read of the update rule or internal state. A proposed fourth escape (horizon extension under the same policy) provably *collapses into* (a): more samples of an annihilated observation law cannot escape the annihilation.

**The strengthened-consequence content.** Object B elevates `#der-loop-interventional-access` to a *third semantically-distinct deployment mode* — at the agent-internal architecture layer — alongside Instance 1's agent-self-intervention (breaking causal-sufficiency degeneracy) and Instance 3's observer-on-sub-agent intervention (breaking coupling-sign degeneracy). The shared content across the three modes is "Level-2 data is the unique broadly-available violation of the Level-1 no-go"; the distinct content is *which* Level-1 degeneracy. This converts the Instance-4 floor from "yet another no-go" into structural confirmation that `#der-loop-interventional-access` carries the same load-bearing role at three distinct AAT layers.

**The CL-2 heavy-reserved refinement collapses into the same move.** The 2026-05-18 resolution spike §7 *proved* (not asserted) that the Regime-C confound of `#result-mismatch-decomposition` / `#internal-external-decomposition` (the conditional $\mathcal M/\pi/$cross split that is identified under Regime A interventional / Regime B functional-form but confounded under Regime C on-policy summary-only) is Object B's no-go projected onto the disturbance-statistic coordinate. Same no-go, same escapes (Regime A escape = interventional / Regime B escape = a functional-form quotient of the orbit), same mechanism. Landing Object B as the genuine fourth floor simultaneously discharges the CL-2 heavy refinement — one decision, not two.

**Math-gate repairs applied during landing.** Per the verdict at `spikes/.routing-trail/SPIKE-VERIFY-471802/`: (i) the resolution spike's boxed "every moment of $\lVert\delta\rVert$ identical" clause was overclaimed; restricted in the segment to *innovation/output + similarity-invariant summaries* (raw $\mathbb E\lVert\delta\rVert^2 = \operatorname{tr}\Pi$ is *not* similarity-invariant in general — equality only when $T$ is orthogonal); (ii) the displayed Lyapunov equation matches `#deriv-sector-condition`'s standard form $F\Pi + \Pi F^\top = \sigma_w\sigma_w^\top$ (the spike's display carried a sign slip; the transformation law $\Pi' = T\Pi T^\top$ is unaffected); (iii) the exact / robust-qualitative tier boundary is carried consistently throughout the new Instance-4 entry, the `#der-architecture-noidentifiability` body, and the `#disc-identifiability-floor` Findings rollup rather than collapsed to flat "exact."

**Fano is the finite-sample refinement, not the exact anchor.** The neutral-drift spike proposed Fano as the floor's external anchor. The resolution spike demonstrated Fano degenerates to the vacuous bound at $I(A;\text{obs}) = 0$ — the similarity-orbit construction has $I = 0$ *exactly* (the observation law is identical, not merely close), so Fano gives "error probability $\geq$ prior," true but vacuous and strictly weaker than the exact Kalman-Ho indistinguishability. Kalman-Ho is the exact-population anchor; Fano is the finite-sample refinement (architectures close-but-not-equal in innovation spectrum, $I \gt 0$ small) and is honestly open as a separate follow-on direction.

### Resource-structure axis opened as an exploratory off-spine branch (Φ(fight)-prompted)

An external-stimulus exercise — the Φ(fight) collective's adversarial-multi-agent-embodied research *agenda* (no published output exists; verified empty repos and a funding proposal), used strictly as an ideation prompt — pressure-tested AAT's adversarial chapter and produced one landed branch plus methodology worth recording.

**What landed.** The Effects-Spiral corollary of `#der-adversarial-destabilization` was discussion-grade because it could not specify how a degrading model feeds back into the adversary's coupling ($\gamma_A(\lVert\delta\rVert)$ unformalized). Strengthen-before-soften produced a *sharper* resolution than the original conjecture: in a resource-bounded regime the spiral becomes a derived finite-time-destabilization result **by making that open term unnecessary**, not by formalizing it. Two new segments, deliberately **off-spine** (Joseph's framing: an exploratory branch no later segment depends on yet):

- `#form-resource-budget` (`formulation`, `conditional`) — the minimal resource axis AAT had recorded as an explicit open gap in `#def-strategy-dimension`: a depletable scalar $\mathcal B_t$ with two introduced posits, (A-cost) drain rises with mismatch and (A-gate) the sector parameter is resource-gated ($\psi(0)=0$). Conditional by nature — the posits are claims about an agent's physical realization, not consequences of prior segments; `conditional` is the intrinsic ceiling.
- `#der-resource-bounded-destabilization` (`derived`, `conditional`) — a hard-budget agent self-depletes to certain finite-time destabilization against even a *constant*-$\gamma_A$ adversary, as the decaying-$\alpha$ instantiation of `#result-sector-persistence-template` — the *same template slot* `#schema-strategy-persistence` occupies for experience-decay. Adversarial survival reframes from a static $(\alpha,\rho,R)$ inequality to a budget-versus-engagement-length race with an excursion-advanced hitting time.

Both registered in `01-aat-core/OUTLINE.md` Appendix A at Stage `exploratory` (the `#sketch-structural-adaptation-genericity` precedent); `NOTATION.md` carries $\mathcal B_t$ under an exploratory-branch section with the drift caveat. No spine segment lists either in `depends:` — off-spine by construction. `bin/lint-outline` clean (the one pre-existing missing-dep is unrelated); both segments `bin/lint-md` clean and `bin/align-slug` no-op.

**Scope honesty (recorded so it is not over-read).** The result is explicitly *orthogonal to* — not a resolution of — the deferred symmetric joint-Jacobian / joint-best-response problem (`#der-adversarial-destabilization` → `#deriv-strategic-composition`): it closes the target's own resource-gated-$\alpha$ leg, single-agent-with-resource, and makes no claim about $\gamma_A$ growing. The regenerative regime ($r_{\mathcal B}\gt 0$) is genuinely open (plausibly QSD/absorbing-barrier-flavored, structural cousin of the continuity-persistence open second no-go) and flagged in the `der-` segment's Working Notes, not attempted.

**Methodology note.** The exercise's discipline value: an external research *agenda* with no output is still a legitimate ideation prompt provided (i) the verified-state is reported plainly (value-direction reversed: AAT is the lens, not the borrower), (ii) every candidate connection is taken to the primary segments before synthesis — here the first connection's plausible "new coupling locus" guess was *refuted by the reading* (`#der-directed-separation`'s selection-vs-processing scope clause) and recorded as a documented dead-end, which is the evidence the exercise was verification not pattern-matching, and (iii) a connection downstream of an unlanded spike (the leakage-locus → mechinterp bridge, downstream of `spike-leakage-locus-2026-05-18.md`) stays trail-only, not landed. Reasoning trail: `spikes/fight/` (`00`–`03` + `99-verdict`); `spikes/INDEX.md` entry dated 2026-05-19. The ops/relationship thread (a recognizable AI-safety-adjacent funder backing the agenda) was deliberately scoped out of the theory work and flagged to Joseph separately.

### Accumulation-typing notation convention adopted (D1); the math and register landings since completed (see Update 2026-05-19)

The accumulation-type-confound thread (originating from the $\varepsilon^\ast(N)$ poly-vs-exponential whole-space pass on `spike-composition-scaling-N`) reached a **decided notation convention**, now landed in `NOTATION.md` §Conventions ("Accumulation typing"): per-step vs accumulated quantities marked the way online-learning marks per-round vs cumulative regret; the accumulation operator written explicitly carrying its existence condition as a subscript ($\mathcal A_{[\text{cond}]}$, not bare $\mathcal A$, because its boundedness is a theorem not a constant); every asymptotic claim carrying an explicit, scoped regime marker (`[contraction]/[critical]/[absorbing]`, hardest-bound at the marginal/non-contraction regime, lightweight in the proven-contraction interior, framed as auditable-not-guaranteed). Joseph's decision 2026-05-19: C/online-learning + the N3 ledger form for the per-step/accumulated layer; the scoped N4 regime badge for the regime layer.

**Verified provenance (why the convention exists).** Three independent investigations converged on the same per-step→accumulate→consequence re-typing: the $\varepsilon^\ast(N)$ pass, the strategy-DAG composition spike (`spike-strategy-dag-composition`), and the independent continuity-persistence spike (`spikes/continuity-persistence/`). Independent verification **V1** (`spikes/verify-A-restatement-model-DS.md`) established — by a decisive $\alpha$-homogeneity argument plus the canon's own Cor-A.1S.1 categorical dichotomy — that the accumulation operator **splits** by disturbance model: the Model-D linear resolvent ($\propto 1/\alpha$, GA-2), the Model-S mean-square functional ($\propto 1/\sqrt{\alpha}$, GA-2S), and the nonlinear reflected Lindley/Loynes operator at the marginal boundary are structurally distinct, *not one operator in several norms*. That split is exactly why the regime marker is load-bearing — it is the gap per-step/accumulated marking alone does not carry, and the place a verified error (continuity's withdrawn "$\mu=0$ ⇒ death-in-the-limit") actually occurred. **V2** found (primary-source, `#disc-stability-certificate` / `#result-certificate-existence`) the pattern is the representation-dual of the certificate spine in the deterministic interior and irreducibly-distinct at the stochastic boundary (co-located with the continuity / Three-Deaths work). **V3** (`spikes/verify-cdmp-corrected-statement.md`) primary-source-verified the corrected `#disc-m-preservation` statement against continuity `03`/`98`.

### The two-operator split landed — `#disc-m-preservation` was housing two targets

The independent adjudication `spikes/adjudicate-disc-m-preservation-operator.md` (fresh-context Opus 4.7, re-derived from `#obs-context-turnover`'s structure, not from artifact chronology) settled that `#disc-m-preservation` was conflating **two genuinely different objects** under one heading: predictive sufficiency (`#def-model-sufficiency`, the SDPI-affine $\mathcal A_D$ contraction toward the future-observation target) and identity continuity (`#def-identity-sufficiency`, the reflected relationally-compensated $\mathcal A_{\text{refl}}$ walk on the identity gap). They are distinct operators at opposite ends of the same singular contraction parameter — the $\mathcal A_D$ recursion's affine norm diverges exactly as its contraction gap closes, which is precisely the $\mathcal A_{\text{refl}}$ walk's load-bearing driftless ($\mu=0$) boundary; you cannot linearize across that pole. Neither supersedes the other. RECONCILIATION.md (the continuity steward's withdrawal of the symmetric-dual subsumption) is the artifact whose position survived structural scrutiny, independently vindicated from a third direction by the adjudication. Landed this cycle (single-segment work, uncommitted, lint-clean, pending the commit-when-asked gate; F-ADJ-1 / F-ADJ-2):

- **`#der-turnover-information-recursion` (Volume III, §03.II)** is the clean $\mathcal A_D$ predictive-sufficiency segment. Three subsumption claims excised: (a) the "$S_{\text{id}} = I_k/I(\mathcal C;Y)$ is one normalization of the same object" sentence (conflated the identity target with a normalization of the predictive target); (b) the "the additive form is the small-loss linearization … superseded" phrasing about the reflected/continuity content (it is a structurally distinct operator at $\mathcal A_D$'s singularity, not its linearization); (c) the unscoped "supersedes `#disc-m-preservation`" → scoped to "the predictive-sufficiency accumulation question only," with an explicit pointer that identity-continuity is a distinct operator with its own segment. A Working-Note flag added: whether R1/R2 is genuinely novel vs. a bridge-lemma-resolvent instance of the existing $\mathcal A_D$ family is `RECONCILIATION.md` §4's *open* question (test-not-assert; orthogonal to this landing; segment demotable to a worked instance later if it resolves "subsumed"). No III→IV `depends:` (INTEGRATION.md constraint 1 stands).
- **`#disc-m-preservation` (Volume III)**: omission-fix only — the adjudication found its staged body is $\mathcal A_D$-faithful and the defect is *omission*, not embedded falsehood. Added the missing acknowledgment that identity-continuity is a separate operator/target/segment (cross-reference + one honest sentence in the accumulation paragraph and Working Notes); the residual "small-loss linearization" subsumption phrase in its Working Notes removed. No rewrite; the subsumption is not re-introduced.
- **`#der-identity-continuity-threshold` (Volume IV, §04.1 Identity) — new segment** carrying V3's primary-source-verified $\mathcal A_{\text{refl}}$ content (`spikes/verify-cdmp-corrected-statement.md`): the reflected Lindley/Loynes walk on the identity gap; strict-`<` threshold ($\mu \lt 0$ persists / $\mu = 0$ no finite stationary law = non-persistence, *not* death-in-the-limit / $\mu \gt 0$ collapse); relational-re-grounding attribution (generic task-learning has zero weight); the ultimate-bound $R^\ast_{\text{id}}$ analog; conditional-with-exact-core carrying (M-ADD)/(M-FREE)/(C-S)/(C-D) at channel-independence prominence; the $D_\Delta$ rate-distortion finiteness specialization. Target `#def-identity-sufficiency` (not `#def-model-sufficiency`); `depends:` the Volume-IV identity side (`def-identity-sufficiency`, `scope-agent-identity`) plus the AAT template (`result-sector-persistence-template`, `deriv-identity-sufficiency-rate-bound`). **The $\eta \to \varrho_{\text{rg}}$ symbol rename** (the continuity re-grounding rate collided with `#result-persistence-condition` line-61's per-dimension $\eta_k$ — a conflation trap) is **done within this segment's scope**: the re-grounding rate is $\varrho_{\text{rg}}$ everywhere in the new segment; the projected-floor disturbance keeps $\rho_k$; `#result-persistence-condition` was not touched (that boundary is a vigilance seam — flagged, not crossed).

History per integration-is-replacement: the prior false "$S_{\text{id}}$ is one normalization" / "Lindley is the linearization" / "$\mu=0$ = death-in-the-limit" framings are deleted from the segment bodies; present truth in the bodies, this correction trail here. The continuity trail honored: `RESULT.md` §5's withdrawal stands; `99`'s superseded-warning stands; `00`–`04`/`98` are history layer.

**Seam flagged for the verifying agent — DISCHARGED 2026-05-19.** The `spikes/INDEX.md` "Continuity-persistence" row and the "Accumulation-typing" sub-entry carried the pre-adjudication single-segment framing ("Lindley/Loynes is the small-contraction linearization"; "replacing `#disc-m-preservation`'s accumulation section" as one segment; "$\mu=0$ ⇒ death" historically). **The $\rho$-decomposition truthification — methodology paid out twice against the lead agent, and a false segment was rebuilt true.** The continuation of the spike-routing cycle's CL-2 thread, run under the just-elevated `doc/spike-routing.md` §0 ("truth is the arbiter; provenance / NOTATION / spikes / segments / agent-convergence are mild proxies that drift"). This is strictly stronger than `spike-rho-factorization`'s three-case survey. The parent re-verified the one-liner independently rather than relaying it. The earlier §4.1 `status: false` mark on `#internal-external-decomposition` is thereby vindicated **on truth, not provenance**, and confirmed not a regression.

**The methodology caught the lead agent twice.** First: a prior lead-agent judgment had cited "NOTATION defines $\rho$ as a single primitive" tagged *verified* — verifying the artifact, not the truth (the §0 failure in miniature; the real argument was *stronger* without the proxy). Second: the lead agent's own CL-2 recommendation rested on the (AV) `(S1)–(S4)` "exact theorem," which the recheck showed is **vacuous** (its cross-term is defined to make the equation balance — the §0 failure one level up). The genuinely exact replacement is a **two-term identity already forced by canon**: `#result-mismatch-decomposition` (GA-1, `status: exact`, cross-term zero by orthogonality) plus the `#deriv-sector-condition` Prop A.1S Itô step. An independent-verify gate (confirmer ≠ recheck spike, `spikes/.routing-trail/SPIKE-VERIFY-087154`) **confirmed the load-bearing result and caught two further pedigree over-claims in the recheck spike itself** — the ν rate-lift `ρ²=ν·E‖δ‖²` is *not* a pre-existing `#hyp-mismatch-dynamics` identity, and the general-$\Sigma$ additivity exceeds Prop A.1S's constant-isotropic scope. A flat "confirm" would have laundered those into canon under the gate's authority; the split verdict prevented it.

**The landing (integration-is-replacement, honest pedigree).** `#internal-external-decomposition` was rebuilt *true* in place — not softened, not pointer-stubbed: the false multiplicative $V_E/V_I$ split is replaced by the additive two-term env/agent decomposition inherited from the exact GA-1 result, plus the constitutive no-go as present-tense canon. Honest tiers throughout per the gate: `status: conditional` (the viability form in $\rho$-units rides the ν rate-lift, landed explicitly as a *stated fluid-limit convention, not pre-existing canon*); the general-$\Sigma$ additivity tagged an *elementary extension beyond Prop A.1S*, not "already canon"; the mismatch decomposition itself exact; the no-go exact-by-constitution. The finer $\mathcal M/\pi$/cross split is **not asserted** — it is conditional (Regime-C confounded = an identifiability-floor phenomenon), reserved, and under independent investigation. Process history → Working Notes + here; segment body is present-truth only. Segment lint-clean; OUTLINE row de-flagged.

**Navigator and NOTATION honesty.** The second navigator §4.1-shaped lie was corrected at four loci (INDEX:102, TODO:147/438, PROPOSALS §D.9): the "(AV) exact theorem via Amari–Nagaoka" record → present truth at honest tier. `NOTATION.md` was given a standing drift caveat and its self-description de-escalated from "single authoritative reference" to "convenience index, not the arbiter" (the §0 NOTATION-trap, instantiated and admitted in the file itself); a structural fix — auto-deriving `NOTATION.md` from segment declarations the way `LEXICON.md`/`README.md` are generated, so it cannot drift — is queued in `TODO.md`.
