## 2026-05-17

**The spike-routing cycle — process established, the canon's one self-lie caught, and a new central discipline (the regression axis).** A full routing pass over the `spikes/` backlog: which spikes are and are not properly integrated into canon, with the same no-go/strengthen/ghost protocol the audit cycle exercised. Process artifacts: `doc/spike-routing.md` (thin companion deferring into the shared `doc/audit-routing-instructions.md` core — extended, not forked, so the hard-won no-go protocol cannot drift), `spikes/README.md` + `spikes/.archived/README.md` (directory-label-as-truth-claim, with the bounded *forward/per-cycle, non-retroactive* `.integrated/` guarantee), and the routing tracker `spikes/ROUTING.md`. Commits `e8c57ff`, `971d127`, `ba8c05a`, `7625892`; the verified `git mv` batch co-mingled into pipeline commit `0834649` (Joseph-pre-authorized; 100%-similarity, reversibility intact — provenance recorded in `spikes/.integrated/MANIFEST-2026-05-17.md`); MANIFEST + verify deliverables `31e54e7`.

**Method.** Two-shot diagnostic pilot → six parallel fan-out slices → independent-verify gate (confirmer ≠ adjudicator, first-hand `src/` re-read) → durable batch. 15 spikes verified `integrated-misfiled`; 10 clean file-spikes moved to `spikes/.integrated/` (per-file audit trail + adjudicator→verifier chain in the MANIFEST). The sibling-coupling refinement caught two cross-slice landing collisions before they could fragment one appendix into three half-landings (CL-1 `#dissipativity-template`, CL-2 the $\rho$ cluster).

**The crown jewel is sober, not celebratory.** The cycle's highest-value find is a §4.1 cardinal violation: `internal-external-decomposition` was asserting the refuted multiplicative $\rho$-factorization `ρ = ρ_external·f(M)·g(π)` at `robust-qualitative` with **no obstruction record anywhere in its git history**. The worked no-go is `spikes/spike-rho-factorization.md` (disturbance decomposes *additive-in-variance*, not multiplicative-in-rate; `√(A+B) ≠ √A·√B`). Triply-converged (fan-out S4 primary-source + pickaxe, S1 corroboration, parent first-hand). Honesty-marked per audit-routing §4.2: `status: false`, an unmissable in-body KNOWN-FALSE banner, the OUTLINE Appendix-A row flagged, cascade verified empty, lint-clean, content preserved-not-deleted. The *replacement* (the additive-in-variance form + the no-go canonized) is the queued heavy landing **CL-2**, carrying a Joseph-reserved Instance-5 decision — deliberately not written at mark-time. This is exactly the "math left only in the spike / canon lying about itself" failure the cycle existed to catch, caught and discharged in the honest direction.

**Disciplines that emerged (the durable methodological contribution).** *The regression axis* (`spike-routing.md` §2a; mirrored Joseph-directed into the shared `audit-routing-instructions.md` §8): a result absent from — or present in — canon has two provenance-distinct causes that look identical from the spike's side (never-landed-valid vs. corrected-away; current-corrected-truth vs. regression-restoration-over-a-fix). Re-introducing a corrected-away result *because it looks better* is the reverse-direction sibling of strengthen-before-soften and the enforcement teeth of integration-is-replacement; "it looks better than canon" is the body-signal (the corrected truth is usually messier). Elevated mid-cycle from a side-gate to a **central investigative axis run on every disposition including the already-integrated**, with `correctly-superseded` as a first-class state. The sharpest instrument is the *exclusion pickaxe* (`git log -S'<refuted form>'` empty ⇒ never-admitted ⇒ regression-impossible; sweep-immune, stronger than add-then-delete). Worked applications: `spike-update-operator-sector` regression-checked CLEAR (genuine deferred orphan, CHANGELOG §2026-05-14 names it in the "($\gamma$)-hybrid straight-authoring" set, not flawed-and-corrected); and all 10 already-integrated re-checked clean (`spikes/.routing-trail/SPIKE-REGRESSION-014997/`). Also: git *recency* is sweep-poisoned but git *provenance* (pickaxe / blame / `log --follow`) is the sharpest non-destructive decisive-test instrument (Joseph-directed into both SOPs); and `live-or-open` carries five sub-dispositions that route differently (a reserved-decision orphan must reach the Joseph batch *loudly*).

**Held, not closed.** Heavy coupled landings CL-1 / CL-2 and the tractable `update-operator-sector` landing (regression-cleared) remain to author; the Joseph-reserved present-truth batch (CL-2 Instance-5, the identifiability-floor 4th-instance triage-contradiction, the `language-as-causal-substrate` Theorem-1 orphan + the C2★ gap, `attention-governance`, `temporal-nesting-rg` Moves B/C/D) is assembled and untouched; INDEX reconciliation deferred (independence — concurrent active-author edits). Working detail in `spikes/ROUTING.md`; this entry is the canonical live provenance home for the cycle (the absorbed spikes' segments point here, not into the archive — the §"archived spikes are not live references" discipline).

## 2026-05-16

**Audit-backlog triage — fan-out + a strengthen-first worked example (Model-S non-exit).** The audit-backlog cleanup (`audits/.integrated/audit-backlog-triage-2026-05-15.md`; retired there from `msc/` on 2026-05-28) consolidated audit working dirs into `audits/`, ran a pilot + 5-cluster parallel adjudication of the standalone backlog, and produced the cycle's crown-jewel: a textbook **strengthen-before-soften, completion-state-3** outcome.

Three independent audit clusters (B/C/D) converged on `#deriv-sector-condition` Prop A.1S(iii): it asserted an *infinite-horizon* non-exit bound `P(τ_R<∞) ≤ nσ²/(2αR²)` justified by a *fixed-time* "Markov tail on the supermartingale" — a conflation of two different probabilistic objects. Two prior audit records (742613-SUPPLEMENT §2, 613842-F2) had recommended *softening* (restate as a fixed-time tail). Per the discipline, the strengthening (Doob/Ville maximal inequality on the Itô-Lyapunov supermartingale — a route a peer adjudication confidently predicted would succeed) was worked in full *first*, in a dedicated background spike (`spikes/spike-stochastic-non-exit-strengthening-2026-05-16.md`).

It **genuinely fails, for a precise structural reason**: no nonnegative supermartingale dominates `V` (the compensated `e^{2αt}V` loses nonnegativity), the additive-noise generator has no bounded non-constant harmonic function, and the recurrent OU diffusion exits any bounded set a.s. (`P(τ_R<∞)=1`) — proven analytically and Euler–Maruyama-corroborated. The failure *is* a result — an exact, new one. **Corollary A.1S.1 (Disturbance-Model Containment Dichotomy):** the persistence-region first-exit probability is exactly the two-point set `{0,1}` — `0` under bounded disturbance (Model D, positive invariance), `1` under additive stochastic disturbance (Model S, a.s. exit of a non-degenerate diffusion from a bounded region) — categorical and $\alpha$-invariant, the value selected by the disturbance model's support structure, *not* by correction strength. The framework previously held a false *interpolating* non-exit bound in this slot; the corrected content is strictly sharper, not weaker. Prop A.1S now carries (iii′) the true fixed-time tail (constant unchanged — only the probabilistic object it governs) plus a new (iv) finite-horizon Khasminskii sup-bound (the honest sample-path companion that survives, flagged horizon-growing); the false "exact" on the infinite-horizon claim is removed; the exact dichotomy is stated as the labeled Corollary A.1S.1 with a `## Findings` catalog entry — which sharpens (not caveats) the hand-off into `#result-structural-adaptation-necessity`: in genuinely stochastic environments, region-exit is a *certain eventual event*, so the trigger for structural adaptation is generic, not exceptional. Downstream one-object correction (613842-F2) landed at `#result-sector-persistence-template`. Documented dead-end: a Doob/Ville infinite-horizon non-exit bound for additive-Brownian Model S provably does not exist — do not re-attempt.

The peer adjudication's *process* call (route to a strengthening spike) was right; its *substantive* prediction (the strengthening succeeds) was mathematically disconfirmed and recorded as such so it is not inherited. The independent-verify gate (adjudicator ≠ grad-confirmer) caught, before propagation, a wrong spine generalization (only 471203 of the 2026-04-28 FINALs has a SUPPLEMENT), a stale "Pending" disposition, and the peer's imprecise summary. Cycle 1 graduations (471203 + extracted-gemini-2026-04-26-27, with F7 Tishby-Zaslavsky→Alemi citation resolved by strengthening) and the consolidated graduation of clusters A–E remain in progress under the same gate.

## 2026-05-14

### Operator-family unification — the certificate spine: two segments, a sharp plural no-go, and a respectful-pedagogy preamble

The deep "real-deal" push of the long-standing C1 question (does an operator-sector abstraction unify AAD's cross-sectional machinery?). The predecessor `spikes/.integrated/spike-operator-sector-unification.md` had returned an honest "2-instance-plus-1-consequence / do not elevate to a fourth meta-pattern" verdict; Joseph asked for the question to be pushed until either a revealing no-go or the unifying mechanism. Prior work was gathered first via scoped `memorata-search` (the jacobian-b1 (SOC) curvature-axiom verdict, the Mori–Zwanzig composition spike, the O-BP10 naming-cycle history). The push ran as a directory spike `spikes/spike-operator-family-unification/` (00-brief → 01-L1 → 02-L3 → 03-L4 → 99-verdict → 04-spine-authoring-brief).

**The verdict: completion-state 2 (succeed-at-claim, strong) plus a sharp *plural* no-go — not the seductive single mechanism, which verification broke.** The unifying object is the equilibrium **stability certificate** (the positive-definite form whose existence certifies self-correction; = Fisher / $(P^-)^{-1}$ / loss-Hessian / plant-Lyapunov-metric per sub-case). Operator-sector is its interior, the separability pattern its scope of existence, additive-coordinate-forcing its forced identity, the identifiability floor its boundary, and composition the question of whether it survives projection. The C1 predecessor's "Λ doesn't fit" was the shadow of framing Λ as a between-spaces surjection rather than the idempotent projection whose Schur/memory defect *is* $\varepsilon^\ast$.

**The keystone verified, not asserted.** Prop L1-lin: one-point operator-sector in *some* inner product ⟺ the linearized equilibrium is exponentially stable, by the standard Lyapunov theorem. The O-BP10 organizing slogan ("an adaptive system is an operator whose contraction rate exceeds its target's drift rate") is therefore an *equivalence, not an analogy* — the prior co-owner's gate ("do not elevate unless O-BP10 surfaces at segment level") is met by this equivalence.

**The central finding: Sylvester's law of inertia is the named irreducibility mechanism for the rank-collapse identifiability floors.** Every coordinate/metric change acts on the certificate by congruence; congruence preserves inertia; so a rank-deficient certificate is rank-deficient in *every* coordinate — the floor is the cone boundary, invariant under the framework's entire representational freedom, escapable only by rank-augmentation (new information), never by reweighting. This sharpens the prior soft "the floor is orthogonal to operator-sector" into "interior vs. boundary of one cone, held apart by an inertia invariant."

**The discipline did its job — the seductive triad is false.** The brief hypothesized Helmholtz ≅ Sylvester ≅ Mori–Zwanzig as "three faces of one failure-of-integrability." It felt structurally inevitable — exactly the plausibility-vs-verification trigger to verify, not assert. Verification disproved both pairwise identities (a non-symmetric Hurwitz field still has a certificate, so Helmholtz is an M3-forcing failure not an M1 boundary one; congruence is invertible while projection is not, different group different invariant). The honest result: three irreducibly distinct named obstructions, each invariant under the others' freedoms. That plurality is *why* AAD carries several cross-sectional meta-patterns and not one — now a stated structural fact instead of an intuition, and a more useful result than the triad would have been.

**Landed (Joseph-confirmed, in this order):**

- **`#disc-identifiability-floor` Sylvester-recognition Finding** (`d46671e`) — the rank-collapse-floor irreducibility recognized as one classical theorem, with a Feynman-criterion Brief (the flat-valley / changing-units-on-the-ruler analog) and an honest scope note (Sylvester covers the rank-collapse subclass; Instance 3 / composition is the *distinct* Schur/Mori–Zwanzig obstruction — the plurality made precise). Eight pre-existing lint-md issues in the segment fixed while in the file.
- **The spine, as two segments** (Joseph's "two or three claims is fine" call — the more honest housing): `#result-certificate-existence` (`type: result`, `status: exact` — the Lyapunov-theorem anchor + R0/R1/R2 strength ladder + full derivation; the segment-level home of the contraction-over-drift / O-BP10 organizing principle) and `#disc-stability-certificate` (`discussion` — the cross-sectional spine that cites the result and exhibits M1/M2/M3 + composition as facets). Splitting the exact equivalence out of the discussion-grade spine houses each at its true epistemic tier rather than burying an exact result as a derived-tag inside a discussion segment.
- **OUTLINE preamble reframed into two layers** at Joseph's request for "respectful pedagogy": a *preamble to the preamble* — a plain-English measuring-stick mental model that scaffolds the thick math before any symbols ("the math is thick; the findings are portable; carry one picture") — followed by the precise certificate-and-facets structure. The analog is isomorphic, not merely evocative (stick = the metric; flat direction = rank-deficiency; can't re-graduate off it = Sylvester; survives coarsening but leaks = Schur plus a memory term), so it scaffolds without overclaiming.
- **Propagation tail (cross-ref-only, no formal changes):** `#disc-identifiability-floor` (boundary facet), `#disc-separability-pattern` (scope-of-existence facet), `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing` (forced-identity facet), `#result-sector-persistence-template` and `#result-contraction-template` (interior facet, Euclidean / non-Euclidean), and `#form-composition-closure` (projection-behaviour facet) each gained one in-voice sentence naming their facet relationship and cross-referencing the spine. Zero new lint debt introduced (verified per-file against pre-edit baseline).

**SP-22 resolved and decoupled.** PROPOSALS §D.9 SP-22's architectural question is closed: the answer is *not* ($\beta$) a unified operator-family meta-pattern — operator-sector is the cone interior, not a peer of M1/M2/M3. The meta-segment question is answered (the certificate spine, landed); the Tier-2 backlog (dissipativity-template, $\rho$-decomposition, PID/update-operator $\alpha$-list, jacobian-b1, kl-to-state-distance, l1-evidence-axiom) is **($\gamma$)-hybrid triage** — each lands at its own INDEX-stated target, no longer gated on an architectural decision. The two previously-gated Group I items are now straight authoring.

**Disciplines exercised.** Strengthen-before-soften (the push refused the predecessor's soft verdict and went until a sharp result). Plausibility-vs-verification (the integrability triad felt inevitable; verification broke it, and the broken triad is the stronger finding). Epistemic-housing honesty (the exact equivalence split out to its own `status: exact` segment rather than living as a derived-tag in a discussion-grade one). No-go-as-result (the plural no-go is the load-bearing content). Respectful pedagogy (the mental-model preamble scaffolds comprehension where the math is thick but the findings important — the same Feynman-criterion discipline the project applies to Findings Briefs, raised to the cross-sectional-structure framing). Verify-don't-assert at every leg (the leg ledger in `00-brief.md`, discharged in `01-`/`02-`/`03-`).

**Honest open edges** (carried in the segments' Epistemic Status and the spike's `99-verdict.md`): the anchor equivalence is linearized/local (the level AAD's persistence results already operate at — stated, not papered); "exactly three obstructions" is robust-qualitative, not proved exhaustive; Sylvester is proved finite-dimensional only (flagged for any future logogenic function-space application). One propagation item remains, flagged for Joseph not auto-run: pointing the PROPOSALS Bundle-1 O-BP10 organizing-principle entry at `#result-certificate-existence` (it touches the proposal portfolio). Commits: `d46671e` (Sylvester Finding), `b599c4a` (spine v1), `98e1bb2` (two-segment split + two-layer preamble), `1f70a32` (propagation cross-refs + SP-22 resolution + this entry), `ff171f4` (O-BP10 Bundle-1 pointer + spike archived), `06ab601` (the discipline correction below).

**Discipline that emerged this cycle — archived spikes are not live references.** When the operator-family spike was archived to `spikes/.integrated/`, the segments it landed in still carried Working-Note pointers into the (now-archived) spike path. The first instinct was to treat this as path-hygiene and *repoint* `spikes/spike-X.md → spikes/.integrated/spike-X.md` (commit `ff171f4`). Joseph corrected this with a principle question: *"Isn't anything in .integrated, by definition, no longer needed as a reference in any working notes?"* — and he is right. `.integrated/` **means** fully absorbed; the FORMAT.md spike-reference forms (unfinished-follow-on; lean landing-provenance) no longer license sending a reader to chase the archive. The fix is *reduce/remove, not repoint*. Applied corpus-wide (`06ab601`): nine segments (six pre-existing from the 2026-05-12 archive cycle, three from this cycle) had their `.integrated/`-pointing Working Notes reduced to lean landing-context lines pointing at the **CHANGELOG cycle entry** (the canonical live home for absorbed-cycle provenance); two notes that were hiding genuine open content behind the spike pointer had that content surfaced into the segment's own Working Notes per FORMAT.md form (1). Corpus verification: 0 segments now point into `.integrated/` (was 9). The discipline is recorded in `feedback_spike_references_only_in_working_notes.md` (new point 5: archival is the trigger to sweep; the failure mode is repoint-instead-of-remove; after any spike-archive batch, `grep -rl 'spikes/\.integrated/' <component>/src/` and sweep every hit). The standing operational rule: a spike-archive is not complete until the segments it landed in no longer point a reader into the archive.

### Partial-cycle landing of the modularity-state-dynamics cycle (Moves 2 and 5 only)

Joseph's pushback on the deferral framing ("any reason *not* to add Miller's Table or M4?") triggered an honest re-look at both questions. Two findings:

**Self-correction on Miller's Table 12.2.** My Task-4 verification claimed the table was "not in segments" and "would need its own segment." That was wrong — the table IS in `#form-strategy-complexity-cost` Discussion §"Computational compression from interaction horizon (Miller 2022)" with the layer-distinction made explicit (Moore-machine behavioral-surface enumeration vs AAD's epistemic-interior $d^\ast$). The four-row state-vs-rounds table is reproduced; Miller 2022 is properly cited; the DAG analog is stated ("a complex $\Sigma_t$ whose edges are only tested over a short horizon gains nothing from its depth — the untested structure is indistinguishable from a simpler strategy"). My grep'ed for "Table 12.2" in segments and got zero hits, but the segment uses "Miller (2022, *Ex Machina*, Table 12.2)" with parenthetical formatting that my pattern missed. Corrected the miller-act-bridge INDEX row + the TODO Group I checked item + this CHANGELOG entry to reflect: there is **no remaining spike-resident leakage** at the level the spike's own §6 implications list identified. The formal Section III dynamics segments (latent-structural-diversity-as-derived, endogenous-$\gamma$-as-derived, extreme-transition-motif-as-formal-theorem) remain open gaps — but they are *new derivations beyond what the spike supplied*, properly flagged in `#impl-strategic-composition`. This is a small but real correction worth marking: my verification methodology was insufficient (grep patterns too narrow for natural-language citation forms), and a fresh grep with the right pattern would have caught it. The principle-level lesson: when a spike's "missing" claim is structurally surprising (a well-cited reference work like Miller 2022 *should* be richly cross-referenced), verify the negative result more carefully than the positive one.

**Partial M4 cycle landing (Moves 2 and 5).** Reading the cycle plan substantively, three moves split cleanly: Moves 1 (already done 2026-05-09), 2 (Discussion update to `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping`), and 5 (bounded-signaling assumption in `#der-directed-separation`) are additive / low-architectural-commitment surgical edits; Moves 3 (`disc-strategic-self-coupling` new segment) and 4 (`disc-modularity-state-dynamics` meta-segment) are substantive new-segment authoring gated on Joseph's §5.1 M4-architectural-commitment decision and the ~2-3 hour prior-art reading work for Move 3. The 2026-05-14 cycle landed Moves 2 and 5 and recorded the Moves 3+4 deferral reasoning in `msc/modularity-cycle-plan-2026-05-09.md` §8 "Execution status." Specifically:

- **Move 2 (landed).** New Discussion subsection "**Wrapping as a truthification mechanism**" in `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping`. Recognizes the wrapping construction as the rigorous formal version of `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` §"Defensive scaffolding as composition." Names the W₀/W₂/W₁ regime hierarchy as the graded characterization of how thoroughly truthification has been applied. Cross-references the queued `#disc-modularity-state-dynamics` M4 meta-segment as forward-reference (per FORMAT.md — forward references are expected), noting that when M4 lands this segment becomes the canonical *formal* instance of the truthification operation paired with the *informal* defensive-scaffolding instance.
- **Move 5 (landed).** New Discussion paragraph "**Bounded-signaling assumption**" in `#der-directed-separation`. Names the implicit framework-level assumption that $G_t \to$ world runs only via $a_t = \pi(M_t, G_t)$ — action coarseness $\lvert\mathcal A\rvert$ upper-bounds the rate at which $G_t$ leaks to observers. Operational-failure case named: agents whose behavioral output is rich relative to action coarseness (humans, LLMs, embodied robots — sophisticated $G_t$-inference from prosody, micro-behavior, attention patterns, response latency). Cross-references `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure` for the adversarial-saturation case where rich-leakage signal is exploited. This is the first segment-level statement of the bounded-signaling assumption; previously implicit throughout the framework.

**Disciplines exercised.** Strengthen-before-soften applied to deferral itself: Joseph's question "any reason *not* to" was the right pushback against performing-deferral, and the substantive answer split into (a) self-correction on Table 12.2 (already landed, not missing), (b) two genuine moves that *are* safe-to-land additively and got landed, (c) two moves whose deferral reasons are real (§5.1 architectural-identity decision; prior-art reading work). The third response to that pushback — defer everything because Joseph asked — would have been the failure mode; the second — execute everything because Joseph asked — would have committed the framework to M4 prematurely. The honest cut between the additive layer and the architectural-commitment layer is what the cycle plan's plan-author originally identified at §5.1, and the 2026-05-14 cycle made the cut crisp rather than re-deciding it.

## 2026-05-12 (late)

### AAD-1 follow-on: matrix-Loewner persistence condition (succeed-beyond-claim)

Fresh cycle picking up the AAD-1 downstream-promotion follow-on flagged at the end of the audit-strengthening cycle. The substantive content is the matrix lift of `#result-persistence-condition`'s scalar persistence inequality to Loewner-order form on the cone of positive-semidefinite matrices, with the load-bearing finding that **per-coordinate is strictly less safe than matrix-Loewner under cross-dimensional correction** — not merely incomplete but false-pass-prone.

**The lift.** Under matrix adaptive tempo $\mathcal T$ from `#def-adaptive-tempo`'s Tensor extension (per-channel primitive $K = (H_M + H_L)^{-1} H_L$ from `#deriv-fisher-local-update-gain`) and matrix disturbance covariance $\Sigma_w$, the persistence question reads off the stationary covariance $\Sigma_\infty$ solving the continuous Lyapunov equation $\mathcal T\Sigma_\infty + \Sigma_\infty\mathcal T^T = \Sigma_w$. Existence is equivalent to $\mathcal T$ Hurwitz (matrix structural persistence (MP-1)). Task adequacy is $\Sigma_\infty \prec D_\delta := \mathrm{diag}(\delta_{\text{critical},k}^2)$ in the strict Loewner order (matrix task adequacy (MP-2)). Three equivalent restatements with different operational readings: per-direction inequality on $\hat v^T \Sigma_\infty \hat v$ vs $\hat v^T D_\delta \hat v$; spectral generalized-eigenvalue $\lambda_{\max}(D_\delta^{-1/2}\Sigma_\infty D_\delta^{-1/2}) \lt 1$ with the principal eigenvector identifying the worst direction; ellipsoid containment.

**Recovery of existing scalar and per-coordinate forms.** The matrix-Loewner condition reduces to `#result-persistence-condition`'s scalar Model S form in the isotropic case ($\mathcal T = \mathcal T_0 I$ etc.) and to `#result-per-dimension-persistence`'s per-coordinate Model S RMS form in the diagonal case. The structural-vs-task-adequacy decomposition of `#result-persistence-condition` transfers directly: (MP-1) Hurwitz is the matrix structural condition; (MP-2) Loewner is the matrix task-adequacy condition; the two remain independent (the remedies for failures differ — structural failure requires changing the correction architecture; task-adequacy failure requires increasing $\mathcal T$ on the weak direction, decreasing $\Sigma_w$ on the weak direction, or relaxing the threshold along that direction).

**The strict-sharpness result.** Constructive 2D counterexample: $\mathcal T = \begin{pmatrix}1 & -0.9 \\ -0.9 & 1\end{pmatrix}$, $\Sigma_w = I$, $\delta_{\text{critical}} = (1.7, 1.7)$. This $\mathcal T$ is symmetric positive-definite with eigenvalues $1.9$ along $(1, -1)/\sqrt{2}$ and $0.1$ along $(1, 1)/\sqrt{2}$ — strongly anisotropic correction with the diagonal $(1, 1)$ direction as the weak axis. Stationary covariance has eigenvalues $5.00$ and $0.26$ on the same directions. Per-coordinate variance per dimension is $2.63$, less than $\delta_{\text{critical},k}^2 = 2.89$ — per-coordinate says PASS. Matrix-Loewner reads the worst-direction projected variance $5.00$ against the direction-projected squared threshold $2.89$ — ratio $1.73 \gt 1$, matrix-Loewner correctly says FAIL. The agent will produce RMS mismatch $\sqrt{5.00} = 2.24$ along the diagonal direction, exceeding the direction-projected threshold $\sqrt{2.89} = 1.70$ on that direction.

**Structural takeaway.** The per-coordinate form is **unsafe** under cross-dimensional correction (off-diagonal $\mathcal T$ in the coordinate basis of $D_\delta$ — the generic Fisher-local case when prior and likelihood do not share the coordinate basis). The matrix-Loewner form is the canonical anisotropic persistence condition; per-coordinate is its diagonal-$\mathcal T$-axis-aligned-$D_\delta$ special case, sharp where the coordinate basis happens to be the correction-machinery's eigenbasis and unsafe outside it. The weak-direction-bottleneck argument of `#result-per-dimension-persistence` generalizes from "the weak coordinate dimension" to "the weak eigendirection of $\Sigma_\infty$ relative to $D_\delta$." This closes the explicit open question in `#result-per-dimension-persistence` Working Notes line 130 ("whether the weak-dimension bottleneck persists under coupled correction is an open question") — yes, qualitatively (the weakest direction is still the bottleneck) but the *quantitative* form requires the matrix-Loewner lift to read the right direction.

**Why this is succeed-beyond-claim.** The strengthening attempt was framed as "derive the matrix-Loewner persistence condition under named conditions." That target was reached cleanly. The *additional* finding — that per-coordinate is *unsafe* rather than merely *incomplete* — emerged in the course of verifying that the matrix form genuinely adds content beyond the per-coordinate form. The counterexample shifts the matrix-Loewner form's status from "interesting generalization that happens to agree with per-coordinate in all real cases" to "the safe condition; per-coordinate gives false-passes whenever $\mathcal T$'s eigenbasis misaligns with the coordinate axes." This is a structural-honesty consequence the cycle did not anticipate at brief.

**Disciplines exercised.** Strengthen-before-soften: the matrix form was attempted as a strengthening of the per-coordinate form rather than as an alternative scope. No-go-as-result: the strict-sharpness result is itself the load-bearing content (without the §4 counterexample, the matrix-Loewner form would be a quiet generalization; with it, the per-coordinate form's unsafe regime is named and bounded). Integration discipline: the per-channel primitive from AAD-5 and the tensor aggregation from AAD-1 both compose cleanly into the matrix-Loewner statement — the cycle's three landings (AAD-5, AAD-1 partial, matrix-Loewner persistence) form an internally-consistent chain rather than three separate pieces.

**What is NOT yet promoted (follow-on cycle items, flagged in TODO).** Matrix sector / nonlinear extension via `#deriv-sector-condition`. Matrix adversarial-tempo lift of `#result-adversarial-tempo-advantage` (the adversary's worst-direction targeting strategy becomes a matrix-eigenvalue-ratio optimization). Matrix composition lifts of `#form-composition-closure`, `#der-team-persistence`, `#deriv-critical-mass-composition` (composite stationary covariance solves a composite Lyapunov equation; the *expected* structural finding is sub-agent-specialization-as-formal-property — a sub-agent strong on direction $\hat v_1$ paired with a sub-agent strong on $\hat v_2$ gives a composite strong on the spanned plane, even when each sub-agent alone fails on the other's strong direction). Matrix information-rate floor extension to `#deriv-persistence-cost`. Model D matrix lift.

**Commit:** `0c0ca9a` — new appendix `#deriv-matrix-persistence-condition`; cross-references in `#result-persistence-condition`, `#result-per-dimension-persistence`, `#def-adaptive-tempo`, `#deriv-fisher-local-update-gain`; OUTLINE row; spike moved to `spikes/.integrated/`; lint clean (126 segment files, 0 ordering violations, 0 missing dependencies).

### Spike-audit triage: 99 spike files surveyed via 3 parallel agents

Following the matrix-Loewner landing, three parallel `general-purpose` audit agents triaged 99 spike files in `spikes/` (alphabetical a–z chunks). Each agent catalogued spike status against the existing `spikes/INDEX.md` tracking and produced status + landing-segment + content-leakage flag per spike. The audit was read-only; findings landed as a triage entry in `TODO.md` organized by action class.

**The findings** (synthesized from three independent agent reports): twelve LANDED-but-leakage spikes carrying substantive content not in segments (notably the bridge-lemma §7.2 passivity/dissipativity math ready for the `#dissipativity-template` appendix; the FSA↔DAG compilation-lossiness analysis; `spike-three-way-tradeoff`'s adversarial critique of `#disc-exploit-explore-deliberate` flagging that the segment may need rewrite; `spike-strategy-tempo-cost` Open Question 6 on LLM context-budget cost which is critical-path for `03-logogenic-agents/`); an eight-spike Tier-2 backlog cluster in the dissipativity-template / operator-sector family that the 2026-05-12 cycle bypassed; three 2026-04-25 spikes not catalogued in INDEX.md (`spike-alignment-impossibility`, `spike-fep-suboptimal-approximation`, `spike-message-passing-credit-assignment`); ten zombies safe to archive in the next housekeeping cycle (the Pearl-Level-4 spikes superseded by prior-art integration discipline; the v1/v2/v3 purposeful-agent spikes whose porting is complete; the Hafez-integration-audit whose `#prior-art-positioning` recommendation is officially rejected; the SOC-composition author-parked spike; the 2026-03-13 attention-spike pair pending Joseph's judgment on framework-scope).

**Disciplines exercised.** The parallel-agent pattern reproduced the multi-agent-methods discipline (parallel cluster-agents with structured schema, banded synthesis). The 99-file audit would have been impractical for a single-agent serial pass; three agents in parallel produced the survey in one round-trip. The brief was peer-voice (share understanding, point at `spikes/INDEX.md` for what's already tracked, three legitimate completion states), and the agents produced converging triage classifications (LANDED-and-archivable vs LANDED-but-leakage vs PENDING-in-INDEX vs OPEN-active-research vs PARKED-zombie vs UNCLEAR) that synthesized cleanly into a single five-group TODO entry.

**What this enables for future cycles.** The triage gives any future agent picking up spike-cleanup work a clear picture of *where the leakage actually is* — without the triage, a future cycle starts from "100 spike files; which need attention?" and the natural failure mode is to pick the most visually-active ones (recent timestamps, large file size) rather than the actually-load-bearing ones (real content not in segments). With the triage, the prioritization is explicit: Group I surgical promotions first (highest-value); Group II architectural decision via a PROPOSALS entry; Group III INDEX refresh (~30 min); Group IV zombie batch-move.

**Commit:** `592c115` — `TODO.md` gains a five-group spike-audit triage section.

## 2026-05-12 (afternoon/evening)

### Audit-strengthening cycle: four stages (AAD-7 / AAD-5 / AAD-1 / ELI-8) landed in `spikes/.integrated/`

Three de-novo audits dropped 2026-05-12 (Codex line-precise cross-corpus; Gemini cross-corpus thematic; Gemini AAD-only math-verification pass — all three now archived at `audits/.integrated/`). The cycle ran in two phases: a *surgical-strengthen-first* pass that landed eight tightenings via apparent-overclaim → named-condition rewrites (commit `ae118ab`), followed by a *spike-and-integrate* pass that produced three strengthening spikes and integrated them as four stages of segment-level landings.

**Cycle shape.** The cycle was structured around the strengthen-before-soften discipline applied at two levels: (1) when an overclaim could be relaxed by softening *or* by stating tighter conditions under which the original (or a related stronger) claim holds, the latter was attempted first; (2) when the strengthening attempt closed negatively, the resulting no-go was treated as a load-bearing result and landed in a segment rather than left in a Working Note. The cycle also articulated and operationalized an *integration discipline* to prevent the recurring failure mode where significant spike content gets accidentally left in spike files: per-spike enumeration tables in [`SPIKE-TODO.md`](SPIKE-TODO.md) cataloging every load-bearing item with its segment destination, plus a `spikes/.integrated/` convention where spike files move only after verification that the substantive content has landed in segments.

**The four landings.**

- **AAD-7 strategic-equilibrium wording precision** (commit `011d3cb`). Four edits across `#deriv-strategic-composition` and `#scope-composite-agent` clarifying the $\alpha'/\beta'$ equilibrium-concept distinctions. The Honest-Limit headline "**No equilibrium exists**" replaced with a three-tier statement (no pure-strategy Nash in cyclic games; mixed Nash exists universally for finite games but is a saddle of fictitious play; no-regret dynamics give CCE convergence in distribution at $O(1/\sqrt T)$). $\beta'$ macro-state-as-distribution surfaced as load-bearing $\beta$' framing. Stale "candidate 4th `#disc-identifiability-floor` instance" cross-references → "candidate adjacent-floor instance" (Instance 4 is occupied by the (PI)/Čencov bias-bound from 2026-04-24). $\gamma'$-sub-scope strengthening attempt closed negative (cyclic games are paradigmatic $\beta$' instances; the $\alpha'/\beta'$ decomposition is the right granularity at the regret-minimization layer) — recorded as a structural-fact Working Note in `#deriv-strategic-composition`. Companion repair to a parallel line-89 "cyclic / non-convergent" false-equivalence slip in `#scope-composite-agent` surfaced en route and folded into the same pass.

- **AAD-5 Fisher-local update-gain derivation** (commit `9bd22ca`). New appendix `#deriv-fisher-local-update-gain` derives the matrix gain operator $K = (H_M + H_L)^{-1} H_L$ and its scalar collapse $\eta^\ast = U_M/(U_M + U_o)$ under three regime conditions (R1) smooth log-likelihood + non-degenerate local quadratic / (R2) first-order step size / (R3) Bayesian coherence, with (PI) parameterization-invariance from `#scope-agent-identity` + Čencov 1982 uniqueness picking out the natural-gradient direction as the AAD-internally canonical reference (4th primary instance of `#disc-additive-coordinate-forcing`). Three independent derivation routes converge — direct local-Gaussian Laplace expansion, Bregman/KL projection on the local tangent plane, Cramér-Rao precision-additive inversion — on the same scalar form, evidence the structure is the Pythagorean projection of two Gaussian information sources in the Fisher metric. Mild boundary-admissibility scope-strengthening surfaced: the minimal condition is $H_M + H_L \succ 0$, not both individually; degenerate-prior / degenerate-likelihood directions are admissible at the boundary ($\eta^\ast \to 1$ along uninformative-prior directions; $\eta^\ast \to 0$ along uninformative-observation directions) — the standard improper-prior admissibility of Bayesian inference. `#emp-update-gain` Epistemic Status rewritten to cite the appendix and lifted from `empirical / robust-qualitative` to `derived (conditional on Fisher-local invariance regime)` for the Kalman / conjugate / natural-gradient core; the cross-domain validity tail (RL, PID, software-developer) is retained at robust-qualitative. Cross-reference satellites at `#deriv-fisher-whitened-update-rule` (sibling magnitude/direction relationship) and `#deriv-adaptive-gain-dynamics` (deterministic-meta-gain special case framing). NeurIPS 2026 Paper 3 chart-rescaling no-go cross-referenced as the structural forcing for (PI); progress marked in `spikes/neurips-back-integration-2026-05-08.md`. The matrix gain $K$ becomes the per-coordinate primitive that unblocks AAD-1 tensor adaptive tempo.

- **AAD-1 tensor adaptive tempo (partial landing — primitive in place)** (commit `6c24bed`). Scope expansion approved en route after AAD-5's matrix gain became available as primitive. `#def-adaptive-tempo` Formal Expression gains a Tensor extension sub-block: $\mathcal T = \sum_k \nu^{(k)} \cdot K^{(k)}$ with $K^{(k)} = (H_M + H_L^{(k)})^{-1} H_L^{(k)}$ from `#deriv-fisher-local-update-gain`; scalar form $\mathcal T = \nu \cdot \eta^\ast$ recovered in the shared-eigenbasis collapse. Epistemic Status gains a scope-of-scalar-vs-tensor clause naming the regimes where tensor is the natural object (anisotropic gains, Fisher-whitened, LMI causal-IB, per-dimension persistence). Targeted scope-tag Working Notes added at `#deriv-causal-ib-lmi` (the existing "tensor adaptive tempo queued" note updated to "now wired") and `#result-per-dimension-persistence` (the per-dimension condition is the diagonal projection of the tensor form). Explicit partial-landing scoping: downstream summary results in `#result-persistence-condition`, `#result-adversarial-tempo-advantage`, and composition results still read scalar and inherit the "scalar / isotropic / nonredundant-channel scope" tag; promoting them to invoke the tensor form directly is queued as follow-on cycle work. The per-direction primitive is in place even if downstream summary results still read scalar.

- **ELI-8 identity sufficiency formalization** (commit `022db01`). `#def-identity-sufficiency` rewritten and promoted from `sketch` to `conditional`. The substantive move is the random-variable specification: $S_{\text{id}}$ is now defined on a joint probability space carrying an identity-relevant cohort $\mathfrak C_t = \{W_i, S_j, \text{Env}\}$ — witnesses, sovereignty-granters, and environment as first-class dimensions rather than modeling devices folded into $E$'s private trajectory. Factor-test vector $\text{identity}_{t+1:}: \Omega \to [0,1]^5$ gives one explicit measurable test per constitutive factor of `#def-five-constitutive-factors`. Three well-definedness assumptions (IS-A1) non-vanishing denominator / (IS-A2) compression-Markov (notably *not* requiring deterministic compression) / (IS-A3) fixed conditioning convention. Boundedness $0 \le S_{\text{id}} \le 1$ derived in 5 lines via data-processing inequality + MI chain rule. Three structural checks confirm the joint-space construction preserves the relational structure of factors (ii)/(iii) rather than collapsing it: independence ablation (the ELIZA case is correctly identity-vacuous); bidirectionality preservation (W3 condition automatic on the joint space); sovereignty self-grant precluded by construction. (IS-A1) violation regimes named explicitly (degenerate cohort / ELIZA / short measurement horizon). New companion appendix `#deriv-identity-sufficiency-rate-bound` lands the rate-distortion feasibility bound + inverse $B_{\min}$ floor on operational compression schedules. New hypothesis `#hyp-substrate-transfer-asymmetry` records the no-go that the empirical asymmetry — frontier-to-local transfer degrades $S_{\text{id}}$ more than the reverse — is *not derivable from $S_{\text{id}}$ alone* (the bit-channel argument's symmetric $\min(C_1, C_2)$ bottleneck precludes any direction-of-transfer effect on the rate-distortion floor); three candidate origins named (substrate-specific inductive biases / asymmetric computation cost / channel-collapse phenomena from `#scope-channel-collapse`). Content-lift: the "distinction without a difference" framing moved from `#def-five-constitutive-factors` Factor (v) into `#scope-eli` Discussion as project-philosophy stance, citing the companion Inquiry submission "Granted Agency Between Sovereigns" (`~/src/synthese-paper/03-inquiry-ai-agents/`, Wecker in review 2026); Factor (v) is now operational-only. Sidewise addresses codex audit ELI-5 (Factor (v) operational/philosophical separation). Verification cadence: a separate verification agent (not the writing agent) read the spike + new segments side-by-side and confirmed faithful landing of all load-bearing content modulo two diff-voice surgical fixes applied before commit.

**Disciplines articulated or sharpened during this cycle.**

- *Strengthen-before-soften applied to no-gos as substantive results.* Joseph's reminder mid-cycle: the third spike-completion-state ("find no-such-claim") is not a quiet retreat with documentation — it is *push through* until you reach a sharp no-go theorem with a named structural obstruction, and report what insight that result yields for the project. The substrate-transfer-asymmetry no-go in `#hyp-substrate-transfer-asymmetry` is the canonical example: the no-go is itself load-bearing content (it tells future agents working on substrate-migration protocols where to look for the asymmetry's origin) rather than a Working Note pointing at a future spike. The $\gamma$'-sub-scope-attempt-closed-negative in AAD-7 is a smaller instance of the same discipline at the Working Note tier.

- *Integration-discipline against spike leakage.* The recurring project pattern of leaving substantive material in spike files rather than promoting it into segments was made explicit and operationalized: per-spike enumeration tables in [`SPIKE-TODO.md`](SPIKE-TODO.md) cataloging every load-bearing item; "even dead-end approaches are useful in appendices, especially no-go theorems" applied to make the no-go-as-result the standard; verification cadence (writing agent + separate verification agent for substantial rewrites) preventing voice-discipline drift in promoted segments; `spikes/.integrated/` move only after verification that the substantive content has landed.

- *Preface/intro discipline.* The OUTLINE preface layer was found to be making claims unsubstantiated by the segments those prefaces referenced — a real content issue that surfaces in the mono build but lives at the OUTLINE-discipline layer. The 04-eli/OUTLINE preface was softened in the strengthen-first pass to defer to segment-level epistemic status rather than asserting stronger tiers; the systematic discipline ("every claim in a preface must trace to a segment whose own epistemic-status tier supports the strength of the preface assertion") is now articulated in `TODO.md` Group (a) for the cross-component sweep.

- *Cross-corpus audit findings vs build-pipeline artifacts.* The 2026-05-12 audits read the markdown-first monograph builds in `mono/`. Some findings were partly artifacts of the still-evolving build pipeline (priming-level prose pulled in from OUTLINE prefaces that escape segment-level epistemic-status discipline; cross-component segments showing as "missing" because the build doesn't resolve them across component `src/` directories — `hyp-checkpoint-forking-failure-modes.md` exists in `04-eli/src/` but shows missing in 03-loga mono, and vice versa for `hyp-experiential-training.md`). The substantive content findings — those that name actual `src/` segments and content — are the higher-value ones and are tracked in `TODO.md` Group (b)-(d).

- *NeurIPS coordination discipline.* The cycle established that `spikes/neurips-back-integration-2026-05-08.md` (a forward-work plan for Phase A/B/C back-integration that hasn't yet run) stays in `msc/` rather than moving to `.integrated/` until the full Phase A/B/C completes — it is a planning document, not a reasoning trail. *However*, progress is marked in the doc itself as cross-references land: AAD-5's cross-reference to Paper 3's chart-rescaling no-go is now marked as "back-integration progress, 2026-05-12: `#deriv-fisher-local-update-gain` carries a cross-reference Working Note pointing at this no-go as the structural forcing for (PI)" in Paper 3 entry #3. Future agents reading the doc will see what's already wired without re-reading the source papers.

- *Inquiry-paper cross-reference under philosophy-track.* The companion Inquiry submission "Granted Agency Between Sovereigns" treats granted sovereignty (factor iii) at length and develops the "distinction without a difference" framing with sharper structural articulation than AAD's formal segments carry. The cycle established that philosophy-track work *gets to be normative* — the Inquiry paper's project-stance reading of the five factors lands in `#scope-eli` Discussion as project-philosophy, not erased to fit AAD's formal voice; the bidirectional cross-reference (the Inquiry paper points at this segment as the formal account of identity sufficiency "in preparation"; this segment points at the Inquiry paper for the philosophical and compact-form structure) is one of the recurring patterns this collaboration produces.

## 2026-05-11 / 2026-05-12

### Monograph build pipeline: from zero to four-volume kaobook output

Two intensive sessions building out a publication pipeline for the framework. Starting from no build infrastructure at all and ending with all four parts of the framework rendering as standalone PDFs in a Tufte-flavored typography on kaobook.

**Cycle shape.** The work proceeded in three arcs: (1) get *anything* rendering — a minimal kaobook compile with our chosen typography (EB Garamond / Roboto Light / STIX Two Math); (2) get the *full corpus* rendering — a kramdown subclass that reads our segment cadence and produces the full ~600pp monograph as a single PDF; (3) refactor that single-PDF prototype into **four independent volumes** (AAD / TST / LogA / ELI) once the size and stability arguments for splitting became clear.

**Key decisions that landed:**

- **Four volumes, not one monograph.** The framework ships as `aad-v0.1.0.pdf` / `tst-v0.1.0.pdf` / `loga-v0.1.0.pdf` / `eli-v0.1.0.pdf` — separate PDFs from the same source corpus. Why volumes: 600+ pages is too big to digest; earlier volumes stabilize while later ones evolve; preprint archives prefer per-paper submission; faster builds during iteration; and the volume split lets us use **kaobook's native hierarchy** (Book → Part → Chapter → Section → Subsection → Subsubsection) instead of fighting it with a custom `\segment` counter.
- **Tufte-flavored typography on kaobook.** Wide-margin layout, sidenotes for equation-level epistemic tags, italic-teal emphasis (`#5A787C`), olive monospace for inline slugs (`#6B7660` Fira Mono), navy cross-refs (`#1F3A6F`), per-type pastel tints in segment header strips (eight-color palette mapped across the 19 segment types). Mathematical core renders in STIX Two Math.
- **`mono/` is output-only; `common/` is build infrastructure.** PDFs, markdowns, and `.build/` staging live in `mono/`. The LaTeX entrypoint, preamble fragments, Ruby renderer, and vendored kaobook live in `common/`. Build script (`bin/build-monograph`) is at the repo root.
- **Per-volume metadata via `mono-meta.yaml`.** Each `0N-*/` carries `title` / `short_title` / `slug` / `major`/`minor`/`patch` / `outline` / `cover_svg`. The build script reads this; `bin/output-version <slug> bump patch|minor|major` reads and writes the version components directly.
- **OUTLINE.md role-prefix convention.** Each H1/H2/H3 carries an italic role token: `# *Volume* AAD`, `## *Part* Adaptive Systems Under Uncertainty`, `### *Chapter* The Reality Model`, `## *Appendices* Details`. The walker validates the prefix against the level (Volume / Frontmatter / Part / Appendices / Preface / Chapter), with implicit-Chapter defaults for parts that haven't been chapterized yet. Source reads well; tooling reads cleanly.
- **Native kaobook hierarchy.** `\part` / `\chapter` / `\section` / `\subsection` / `\subsubsection` — no custom counter machinery. Equations, tables, figures number per kaobook native. Cross-refs use cleveref.
- **Cross-volume references** via `xr-hyper` (planned Phase 1d): each volume's `.aux` file is persisted (`<component>/<slug>.aux`) so sibling builds can read it for cross-PDF clickable links. Fallback to bibliography-style citation for standalone reading.
- **Build-info stamp.** `\buildsemver` / `\buildsha` / `\builddate` macros injected via `build-info.tex` so the title page / colophon can render build provenance. Filename is semver-only; the sha lives inside the document (with `-dirty` suffix for working-tree-dirty builds).
- **Cover SVG support.** `cover_svg: AAD-cover.svg` in `mono-meta.yaml` triggers `rsvg-convert` to render the SVG to PDF and `\includepdf` it as the volume's first page.
- **Evergreen cross-references** as a discipline: cross-refs target intrinsic identity (slug for segments, named-tag token for equations), never positional numbers. The named-formula label extraction (`*[Type (name, from ...)]*` → `\label{atom:<name>}`) is queued for Phase 1c-tail work.

**Active TODO** lives in [`FORMAT-TODO.md`](FORMAT-TODO.md) — six-phase plan covering ToC, volume split (now landed), native hierarchy (now landed), cross-volume refs, smart rebuild, FORMAT.md doc updates, OUTLINE chapter introduction for Parts II/III/IV, cross-ref hygiene sweep, and FORMAT-compliance linter sweep. The Progress Snapshot at the top of FORMAT-TODO tracks ✅ landed / 🚧 mid-flight / ⬜ not started.

## 2026-05-09

### Goal-Update Coupling Class rename + Class 2 ↔ 3 swap landed: Class 1/2/3 → Separated/Coupled/Partial; Architecture ladder aligned with the six other AAD taxonomy ladders; warning-callout discipline anchored at six canonical surface points

A whole-corpus rename + numbering swap landed on the `guc-rename-2026-05-09` topical branch, then merged to main. **Old vocabulary:** Class 1 = Modular, Class 2 = Fully merged, Class 3 = Partially modular. **New vocabulary:** Class 1 = Separated *(unchanged number)*, Class 2 = Partial *(was Class 3)*, Class 3 = Coupled *(was Class 2)*. The collective axis is the **Goal-Update Coupling Class (GUC Class)** — the property the directed-separation condition of `#der-directed-separation` measures, with $\kappa_{\text{processing}}$ as the engineered-systems quantitative diagnostic. Two operations bundled because every touched file got both: rename (descriptive labels replacing numerals at the property level) and swap (numbering aligns Architecture with the six other AAD ladders that all run cleanest → middle → worst as 1 → 2 → 3 — Pearl's Correlation L0/L1/L2; Convention C1/C2/C3; Contraction Tier 1/2/3; Identification Regime A/B/C; Scope Adaptive/Agency/Composite; A2'-scope $\alpha_1/\alpha_2/\beta$). Pre-rename, Architecture was the lone visual outlier in the meta-pattern table at `#disc-separability-pattern` (running 1 → 3 → 2); the swap eliminates the outlier status.

**Why the rename.** The numbered classes named *positions in a taxonomy* rather than *the property the taxonomy measures*. Cross-architecture R2 voters had split across competing English-modifier slates (Modular/Merged/Scaffolded vs. Modular/Integrated/Partially-coupled vs. Modular/Coupled/Partially-modular). Resolution: name the *property* the directed-separation condition measures, not an architectural realization. "Separated" directly echoes the segment-derived property; a tightly-integrated architecture that happens to be goal-blind is also Separated.

**Why now.** The class-coercion-via-wrapping segments that landed earlier the same day (`#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` + `#der-logogenic-as-wrapping` — see entry below) used the old vocabulary throughout. Doing the rename next prevented accumulating retrofit debt. Every additional cycle of new content under old vocab would have made the eventual rename heavier.

**Execution shape — 8 phases on a topical branch, multi-agent verification.** Branch `guc-rename-2026-05-09` opened in the main worktree; batched commits as the executing agent saw fit. Per-phase pattern: **Sonnet 4.6** ran the rename surgery (per-occurrence reading with semantic-reversal vigilance — every `Class 2` and `Class 3` reference classified by referent before substitution); **Opus 4.7 (1M context)** verified each batch (independent agent per the "verification by an agent other than the modifying agent" discipline in the live tracking file). Phases:

- **Phase 1** — Terminology entries (`terminology/entries/goal-update-coupling-class.md` axis + `separated.md` / `coupled.md` / `partial.md` per-value entries; existing `directed-separation.md` body refreshed; five `bin/term decide` events recorded under `terminology/decisions/{...}/`); LEXICON.md regenerated via `bin/term render`.
- **Phase 2** — `01-aad-core` canonical (18 segments + OUTLINE), heaviest batch. Canonical home `#der-directed-separation` (32 occurrences) led; second-step heavy semantic-reversal cluster (`#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping`, `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure`, `#deriv-observation-ambiguity-bias-bound`, `#disc-separability-pattern`); third-step mid-touch cluster (`#deriv-strategic-composition`, `#disc-independence-audit`, `#der-interaction-channel-classification`, `#der-agent-opacity`); fourth-step light cluster + OUTLINE.
- **Phase 3** — `03-logogenic-agents` (8 segments + OUTLINE), the semantic-reversal-heaviest component since logogenic agents *are* the canonical "Class 2 (fully merged) → Class 3 (Coupled)" case under the rename.
- **Phase 4** — `02-tst-core` (1 segment) + `04-eli` (4 segments).
- **Phase 5** — Root docs (CLAUDE.md, NOTATION.md, HISTORICAL-CONTEXT.md, PROPOSALS.md, doc/naming-principles.md), four README partials, new `_terminology-warning.md` partial composed into the README + README-auditor liquid templates, and warning-callout placement in LOG.md + CHANGELOG.md headers.
- **Phase 6** — Plan-file collapses: TERMINOLOGY-TODO §B closed; PRACTICA cycle priority #1 marked complete; TODO entry struck through.
- **Phase 7** — Auto-regenerated downstream verified: LEXICON.md + README.md + README-auditor.md + FINDINGS.md.
- **Phase 8** — This CHANGELOG entry + git tag `pre-guc-rename-2026-05-09`.

**Warning-callout discipline at six canonical anchors.** Frozen archaeology (`_obs/`, `msc/AUDIT-WORKING-*/`, `audits/`, `spikes/`, `ref/`, naming-vote files, etc.) carries old-vocabulary Class-N references by design — those are not retroactively edited per the project's archaeology-preservation discipline. The six canonical-surface anchors carry the warning callout (mapping `historical → actual current → sometimes AKA` with the "sometimes AKA" column carrying informal labels Modular / Undirected / Operational that may surface in less-formal artifacts):

1. `01-aad-core/src/der-directed-separation.md` — at the top of the Architectural classification subsection.
2. `03-logogenic-agents/src/result-section-ii-survival.md` — at the top, before the Formal Expression.
3. `CLAUDE.md` — inline after Architectural Decision #5.
4. `README.md` + `README-auditor.md` — via the new `doc/readme/src/_terminology-warning.md` partial composed into both liquid templates near the start.
5. `LOG.md` — header.
6. `CHANGELOG.md` — header (this file).

The git tag `pre-guc-rename-2026-05-09` anchors the callouts at a stable point in history (the parent of the branch's first rename commit).

**Migration-note convention.** Every segment whose Class-N reference changed *semantic meaning* (i.e., touched Class 2 or Class 3 under old vocab) carries a one-line migration note in Working Notes: *"Migration note (2026-05-09 GUC rename): Class 2 ↔ Class 3 swap. Pre-2026-05-09: Class 2 = fully merged, Class 3 = partially modular. Post: Class 2 = Partial, Class 3 = Coupled. Removed at `candidate` stage per FORMAT.md Gate 4."* Segments that only mentioned Class 1 (unchanged in number) didn't need a note. The notes are the per-segment local rosetta-stone alongside the global warning callouts; they retire as segments promote to candidate.

**Prose-disposition discipline.** Register-mixed default per the execution plan §4a: `Class N (Property)` form for headers and first-mention contexts; bare property word in mid-prose where local context is established and ambiguity risk is low; `GUC-Property` form (e.g., `GUC-Coupled`) where citability matters or where adjacent vocabulary collisions threatened (notably in `#disc-adversarial-coupling-pressure`, where "coupling pressure" / "adversarial coupling" is the central concept distinct from GUC-Coupled the value). The "modular" word required per-occurrence judgment: Class-1 references renamed to Separated; broader system-level usages (modular topology, modular monitoring, modular sidecar, modularity-as-property in the queued M4 segment) stayed.

**Three-axis disambiguation.** In the wrapping-construction segments (`#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping`, `#der-logogenic-as-wrapping`, `#def-auxilia-hierarchy`), three independent axes share notation: GUC Class 1/2/3 (renamed); W₀/W₁/W₂ wrapping-regime letters (untouched — independent axis); Class A/B/C component-admissibility partition (untouched — independent axis). Migration notes in those segments document the three axes explicitly.

**Four pre-existing bugs incidentally fixed by the rename** (housekeeping wins): (i) `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` "Class-3 LLMs are scope-in" was inconsistent with the canonical home (LLMs were Class 2 = fully merged pre-rename); rename incidentally aligned it (Class 3 = Coupled = LLMs is now correct). (ii) `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` W₀ row "Raw Class-3 component" framing was ambiguous (W₀ should cover the full non-Class-1 union); sweep expanded to "Raw Class 2 (Partial) or Class 3 (Coupled) component". (iii) `#der-logogenic-as-wrapping` Findings Brief had the same Class-3-for-LLMs inconsistency. (iv) `#scope-eli`'s class-coercion paragraph used "Class-3 component" for logogenic; same incidental alignment. All four are documented in their respective migration notes.

**One net improvement beyond rename.** `#deriv-strategic-composition` got an explanatory paragraph added during the sweep: bounded across-agent coupling places strategic composites in **Class 2 (Partial)** rather than Class 3 (Coupled), because within-agent separation is preserved. The rationale wasn't in the original; the rename forced a clarification that future readers will benefit from. The pre-existing tracking-file row description for this segment had the wrong post-rename label ("Separated → Coupled composite"); sonnet caught the error during the read.

**Methodological observation — multi-agent verification cadence worked.** The sonnet-modifies / opus-verifies pattern (verification by an agent *other than* the modifying agent, per the live tracking file's discipline) caught real issues: the wrong post-rename label in the tracking file's pre-existing row description; multiple semantic-reversal calls in the composite-inheritance subsections; the three-axis disambiguation discipline in the wrapping segments. Verification proceeded in parallel with later-batch surgery — no bottleneck. The live tracking file (single long table with `untouched → modified → verified` state machine; coordination via the file) served as the multi-agent rendezvous point. Pattern worth reusing for future whole-corpus refactors.

**Surface area (final tally).** 30 segments + 2 OUTLINEs + 4 README partials + new `_terminology-warning.md` partial + 5 root docs (CLAUDE, NOTATION, HISTORICAL-CONTEXT, PROPOSALS, naming-principles) + 5 terminology entries + 5 decision events + 4 plan files (collapsed) + 4 auto-regenerated downstream files (LEXICON, README, README-auditor, FINDINGS) = ~60 files touched on the canonical surface. Frozen archaeology (~50+ files) preserved verbatim with warning-callout coverage at the canonical anchors.

**Plan and tracking-file artifacts** (preserved as the durable record of how the rename was executed): [`msc/class-rename-execution-plan-2026-05-09.md`](msc/class-rename-execution-plan-2026-05-09.md), [`msc/class-rename-tracking-2026-05-09.md`](msc/class-rename-tracking-2026-05-09.md). Decision-source rationale: [`msc/naming/naming-rename-plan.md`](msc/naming/naming-rename-plan.md) lines 92-116 (original 2026-05-04 record + meta-pattern alignment audit).

**Scope honesty.** This bundle was the highest-leverage *single* rename in the broader naming program — the architectural Class N axis touched ~60 files in canonical surface and required the only semantic-reversal handling in the program so far. But it is one bundle in a multi-cycle arc, not the closure of the naming refactor as a whole. The R2 cohort produced 629 distinct currents; **58 are now marked with a `rename_status` decision (44 from 2026-05-04 manual curation + 14 from this cycle); ~511 remain unrouted** — not yet evaluated for canonicalize / rename / add-alias / exclude. The §C terminology-canonicalize commitments (~70 entries already decided in 2026-05-04 but not yet executed) are still pending. Continuing-evaluation work tracked at PRACTICA §"Current naming conventions refactor" item 13.

### Class coercion via wrapping landed: constructive route from Class-2/3 components to Class-1 composites; Parts III/IV reframed as wrapping-construction instantiation; W₀/W₂/W₁ regime hierarchy with structural-vs-behavioral leakage bounds

A new derived segment `#der-class-coercion-via-wrapping` lands in `01-aad-core/`, with a logogenic-substrate specialization `#der-logogenic-as-wrapping` in `03-logogenic-agents/`. The construction provides the constructive direction of `#hyp-directed-separation-under-composition` for the wrapper-around-component special case: a Class-2 or Class-3 component (one whose forward pass entangles belief-update and goal-conditioning, e.g., an LLM) can be embedded inside an external scaffold whose state $X_W = (M_W, G_W)$ is updated by structurally distinct query channels — goal-blind queries to the component update $M_W$; goal-conditioned queries update $G_W$. Under stated conditions on the component (admissibility of goal-blind queries, stationary conditional, no implicit goal-inference), directed separation holds at the wrapper level by structural commitment of the type signatures, and the composite is Class-1 by construction. Two theorems landed: an exact form (under the strict no-implicit-goal-inference condition) and an approximate form (with the condition weakened to a KL leakage bound, propagated to the wrapper level by data-processing inequality).

**The W₀/W₂/W₁ regime hierarchy** is the AAD-distinctive contribution within an otherwise-substantial-overlap prior-art landscape. Strict wrapping (W₁) issues separate goal-blind and goal-conditioned component calls per cycle; structural directed-separation guarantee with leakage bound $\kappa_{W_1} \leq I(A(q_M); G_W \mid q_M)$ from pretraining-distribution mutual information. Partial wrapping (W₂) issues one goal-conditioned call per cycle with structurally typed parsed response routing updates to $M_W$ vs. $G_W$ slots; structural separation lives at the *write boundary* but not at the *query boundary*; leakage bounded only *behaviorally* by the component's instruction-following fidelity, with no structural upper bound. No wrapping (W₀) is the Class-3 baseline. The hierarchy refines the Class-1 cell of `#der-directed-separation` with a structural-vs-behavioral sub-distinction (Class-1-by-structure vs. Class-1-by-behavior) that is operationally important — behavioral compliance is empirical and adversarially fragile; structural separation is derivable from the wrapper's construction.

**Parts III/IV reframe.** PROPRIUM-as-implemented (shoshin) sits in W₂: one goal-conditioned LLM call per cycle, response parsed into typed update fields routed to the multi-component PRINCIPIA stores (VERA / MEMORATA / CONSORTIA / PERCEPTA / CHRONICA on the $M_W$ side; AXIOMATA / OPERATA / PRAXES on the $G_W$ side). The auxilia hierarchy (`#def-auxilia-hierarchy`) is the candidate constructive realization of W₁ — auxilia handle goal-blind belief-side queries on cheap substrates while the entity's main LLM call handles strategy-side updates goal-conditionally. Implementing this strengthening is engineering follow-on, consistent with the documented PROPRIUM ontology (which does not preclude the W₁ split). The agentic-tft cognitive-loop-spec's CONTEXTUALIZE → CHOOSE phase separation is the temporally-staged form of the same wrapping move. ELI-specific structure (sovereignty axes, accountability infrastructure, identity factors, substrate-independence, INDIVISUM forking-lock) is *added structure* on top of the class-coercion substrate, not part of the class-coercion theorem itself — the wrapping construction is necessary scaffolding for an ELI but not sufficient; what makes a Class-1-coerced agent into an ELI is the conjunction with the closed-loop interiority sub-scope and the five constitutive factors of identity.

**The "Class 2 exit" framing in CLAUDE.md is resolved.** Previously: *"Directed separation violated by goal-conditioned agents (LLMs) — handled as architectural scope (Class 2 exit), not approximation."* Now: addressed constructively via the wrapping construction — Class-3 components are scope-in for the wrapper construction at a measurable cost, not scope-out. The cost is paid in tempo (Brooks's-Law form via `#der-tempo-composition`, with $\mathcal T_W \leq \mathcal T_A^{\text{nominal}} - C_{\text{coord}}^{\text{wrap}}$ and $C_{\text{coord}}^{\text{wrap}}$ scaling with the number of component calls per macro-step) and a residual leakage rate from pretraining-induced query-content / goal-content correlation. This refinement makes Parts I/II's modular-agent machinery applicable to logogenic-substrate work in `03-logogenic-agents/` cleanly, rather than treating Parts III/IV as a parallel research thread requiring "coupled formulation from the start."

**Substantial prior-art integration** per ASF's prior-art-integration discipline. The closest formal prior art is the POMDP / Bayesian decision theory line (Astrom 1965; Kaelbling, Littman, Cassandra 1998 *AI* 101) — Bayesian belief-state filters are goal-blind by construction; the wrapping move recapitulates this in the AAD vocabulary. Cognitive architectures (Newell 1990 *Unified Theories*; Laird 2012 *Soar*; Anderson 2007 *ACT-R*; Sun 2016 CLARION; Baars 1988 / Dehaene 2014 Global Workspace) have done modular agent design with separated belief / goal / action state for 40+ years. Adjacent literatures: MDP-homomorphism / state-abstraction (Ravindran-Barto 2004; Taylor-Precup-Panangaden 2008; Abel et al. 2016/2020; Subramanian-Mahajan 2020; Congeduti-Mey-Oliehoek 2020), categorical structured systems theory (Smithe 2024; Capucci et al. 2022), FEP-RG / scale-free active inference (Friston 2019 *JTB*; Friston et al. 2025 *Front. Network Physiology*), IB-as-RG (Mehta-Schwab 2014; Kline-Palmer 2022), singular-perturbation–RG (Chen-Goldenfeld-Oono 1996; Chiba 2009), tool-using LLM frameworks (Yao 2022 ReAct; Shinn 2023 Reflexion; Park 2023 Generative Agents; Packer 2023 MemGPT; Schick 2023 Toolformer). AAD's contribution within this landscape is the *integration synthesis* — the wrapping construction bound up with sector-Lyapunov persistence, Brooks's-Law tempo accounting, the Class-1/2/3 directed-separation classification, and the LLM-specific (C1)–(C3) admissibility/leakage conditions — plus the W₀/W₂/W₁ regime hierarchy (no clear prior art for the *graded* structural-vs-behavioral distinction).

**Segment-level updates beyond the two new segments:** Discussion-section additions to `#der-directed-separation` (structural-vs-behavioral Class-1 sub-distinction), `#hyp-directed-separation-under-composition` (constructive special case promoted), `#form-composition-closure` (wrapping as constructive (A1)–(A4); $\varepsilon^\ast_{\text{track}}$ vs $\varepsilon^\ast_{\text{coerce}}$ disambiguation), `#der-tempo-composition` (wrapping as Brooks's-Law instance), `#def-auxilia-hierarchy` (auxilia as W₁ realization candidate for logogenic substrate), `#scope-eli` (ELI structure as added load on the wrapping substrate). OUTLINE.md updated in both `01-aad-core/` (Section III table) and `03-logogenic-agents/` (§03.II Scaffolded sub-scope table).

**Reasoning-trail provenance:** spike directories at `spikes/class-coercion-wrapping/` (this cycle's spike) and `spikes/temporal-nesting-rg/` (the prior cycle's RG-form-preservation spike whose findings shaped the class-coercion framing). The integration plan at `spikes/class-coercion-wrapping/INTEGRATION-PLAN.md` records what was interned into segments. Per the segment-voice / spike-references-only-in-Working-Notes disciplines, the spike directories are no longer load-bearing for any segment content; substantive theory lives in segments.

**Discipline reinforcements observed during the cycle.** Three project conventions surfaced as decisive: (a) `feedback_segment_voice_not_diff_voice` — segments speak as the current theory, not as a chronicle of changes; the wrapping-segment Discussion sections cite Friston 2019/2025 directly without citing the temporal-nesting-rg spike that surfaced them. (b) `feedback_spike_references_only_in_working_notes` — the spike directories live as reasoning trails; segment substantive content is self-contained. (c) Joseph's framing correction mid-spike: *"the spike itself should focus on strengthening ASF theory and discovering truth"* — paper-extraction reframed as natural downstream consequence rather than goal; the spike's verdict and integration land theory-strengthening shape, with paper-shaped findings as possible follow-on rather than load.

| `def-change-distance` | `def-discontinuity-distance` | F1; segment's own load-bearing framing word |

| `deriv-strategic-dynamics` | `deriv-edge-credence-dynamics` | F1; names what evolves and where |

Heaviest rename: `deriv-strategic-dynamics` → `deriv-edge-credence-dynamics` (159 substitutions across 62 files). Lightest: `obs-simulation-results` (15 / 9). Spike-file references to legacy forms left as research-artifact archaeology per the established discipline.

**bin/rename-slug bug fixes** (commit `3cdb1a6`). Two real bugs surfaced during the obs-gates-advantage rename pilot, both fixed before the §A sweep continued:
- *Hardcoded old directory.* `COMPONENT_SRCS` listed `04-logozoetic-agents/src` (renamed to `04-eli/` on 2026-05-01); the script silently skipped the missing dir, meaning any rename targeting a slug inside `04-eli/` segments would not touch those files. Fixed: both `COMPONENT_SRCS` and `COMPONENT_OUTLINES` now reference `04-eli/`. Smoke-tested with `--dry-run scope-eli scope-eli-test`: finds 29 replacements (was 0).
- *Bare-filename markdown links not rewritten.* `path_re` matched only `src/SLUG.md`; bare `[text](SLUG.md)` markdown links inside parens were missed. Fixed: regex extended to `(src/|\()SLUG.md` with capture-group preserving the boundary character. Caught one residual link in `obs-simulation-results.md` that the old regex would have missed.

**§B prose-vocabulary renames landed: 7 of 8.** Each as its own atomic commit; the pattern is *update canonical-introduction site to use the compound form* + *write `terminology/entries/<slug>.md` with `aliases:` for in-segment shorthand and `do_not_confuse:` for overload risks* + *record `bin/term decide canonicalize`*.

| Item | Rename | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| §B.2 | (new) **Knowledge Type** axis (Static / Learning) | LEXICON-only commitment elevating `doc/DOMAINS.md` working-table convention |
| §B.3 | `grafting` → `strategic grafting` | Citability; bare retained as in-segment shorthand |
| §B.5 | `alignment uncertainty` → `teleological-unity uncertainty` | Anchors $U_{\text{align},ji}$ in unity vocabulary; symbol unchanged |
| §B.6 | `plan confidence` → `strategy-plan confidence` | Most pervasive; 8 segments touched; symbol $\hat P_\Sigma$ stays |
| §B.7 | `effective disturbance` → `regime-typed effective disturbance` | Compound canonical when AAD-internal regime decomposition is in scope; bare stays for AAD-generic Lyapunov statements |
| §B.8 | `routing structure` → `multi-agent routing structure` | Anchors $R_t$ in `#scope-multi-agent` context |
| §B.4 | `logozoetic agent` → `Emergent Logozoetic Intelligence (ELI)` | Class-label rename completing the directory-precedent (`fa63616`); slug `logozoetic-agent` → `eli`; 6 cross-referencing entries updated; 4 segments + OUTLINE + 2 README partials touched; README rebuilt via `bin/build-readme` |

The triple-hyphen compound-modifier form (e.g., `strategy-plan-confidence error`) is an unusual but unambiguous English construction; it works in body prose where the full noun-phrase modifies another noun.

**LEXICON.md grew from 39 to 45 entries.** Six new prose-vocabulary entries added; one entry renamed (logozoetic-agent → eli) with content expansion.

**Deferred:** `_lexicon-full-archive.md` partial has 12+ deep-philosophical mentions of "logozoetic agent" — not composed into the live README (`README.md.liquid` doesn't include it), so no urgency. Per-mention judgment warranted rather than mechanical replacement; queued for a future hygiene pass if the partial is ever brought back into composition.

**Remaining for §B:** item 1 — Class 1/2/3 → Separated/Coupled/Partial bundle with the coordinated Class 2 ↔ 3 numbering swap. Largest single piece (~8 segments + README + CLAUDE.md, with semantic-reversal implications for archival Class-N references). Warrants its own dedicated cycle.

**Methodological observation worth carrying.** The `bin/term decide` workflow (entry creation + decision-event recording) crystallized into a clean rhythm by mid-evening: read source segment, draft entry frontmatter + body, run `bin/term decide`, run `bin/term render`, edit `TERMINOLOGY-TODO.md` to remove the landed row, single commit per item. The decision-event audit trail (`terminology/decisions/<slug>/<ts>-<decider>-<action>.md`) accumulates naturally and reads cleanly on `bin/term show <slug>` afterward.

## 2026-05-08

### Naming-cycle Phase 6: terminology infrastructure live; LEXICON.md migrated to per-entry store; first §A slug rename landed

Three landings extending the Phase-5 infrastructure into operational use.

**Terminology-store goes live.** All 39 entries in `LEXICON.md` migrated into per-entry markdown-with-YAML-frontmatter files at `terminology/entries/<slug>.md` via a sub-agent walk over the source tables. Two were hand-authored as templates the prior session (`control-regret`, `aporia`); 37 created by the migration agent against the schema in `terminology/README.md`. `bin/term render` default destination switched from staging path (`terminology/_emitted/LEXICON.md`, gitignored) to root `LEXICON.md` — which is now an Auto-generated artifact carrying the marker-and-clobber-guard discipline. Hand-edits to LEXICON.md are blocked at the next render unless `--force`. Source-of-truth flow: edit `terminology/entries/<slug>.md` (or use `bin/term decide` to record decision events) → run `bin/term render` → LEXICON.md regenerated. Same shape as `bin/build-readme` regenerating README.md from `doc/readme/` partials.

**Section ordering** in the rendered LEXICON went from a curated `TAG_DISPLAY_ORDER` list to alphabetical-by-display-heading, with the `TAG_DISPLAY_NAMES` mapping retained for headings that shouldn't be auto-title-cased (acronyms like `eli` → `ELI`, parenthetical qualifiers like `software` → `Software (TST)`). Uncategorized always lands last regardless of alphabetic position — reads as a residual catch-all rather than a peer category.

**First §A slug rename landed:** `obs-gates-advantage` → `obs-gated-tempo-advantage` (TERMINOLOGY-TODO §A item 3; citability fix per Criterion 9). 35 substitutions across 16 files via `bin/rename-slug`, plus three manual touch-ups: H1 ("Observation Noise Gates Adversarial Advantage" → "Gated Tempo Advantage"), formal tag (`*[Observation (observation-gates-advantage, ...)]*` → `*[Observation (obs-gated-tempo-advantage, ...)]*`), and one bare-filename markdown link in `obs-simulation-results.md` that the script's `src/OLD.md` regex didn't match. `bin/lint-outline` clean.

**Two bin/rename-slug bugs** surfaced and queued in TODO.md: hardcoded `04-logozoetic-agents/src` in `COMPONENT_SRCS` (the directory was renamed to `04-eli/` on 2026-05-01); bare-filename `[text](OLD.md)` markdown links not covered by the path-replacement regex. Both gracefully fail rather than corrupt; both should land before the next bulk slug-rename batch.
